not tracked not covered covered 
// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
// Package filesystem provides atomic content-addressed APK persistence.
package filesystem

import (
        "bytes"
        "context"
        "crypto/sha256"
        "encoding/hex"
        "errors"
        "io"
        "os"
        "path/filepath"
        "regexp"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

var digestPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{64}$`)

type Store struct{ dir string }

// Open prepares a durable content-addressed directory; it never creates a temporary fallback.
// Usage: store, err := filesystem.Open("/var/lib/kylok/artifacts").
func Open(dir string) (*Store, error) {
        if dir == "" {
                return nil, errors.New("artifact directory required")
        }
        if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0700); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &Store{dir: dir}, nil
}

// Put verifies the digest and atomically publishes immutable bytes without replacing concurrent content.
// Usage: err := store.Put(ctx, digest, verifiedBytes).
func (s *Store) Put(ctx context.Context, digest string, data []byte) error {
        if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        sum := sha256.Sum256(data)
        if !digestPattern.MatchString(digest) || hex.EncodeToString(sum[:]) != digest {
                return errors.New("artifact digest mismatch")
        }
        // Link rather than rename: concurrent publication cannot overwrite existing bytes.
        f, err := os.CreateTemp(s.dir, ".publish-*")
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        defer os.Remove(f.Name())
        if _, err = f.Write(data); err != nil {
                f.Close()
                return err
        }
        if err = f.Sync(); err != nil {
                f.Close()
                return err
        }
        if err = f.Chmod(0400); err != nil {
                f.Close()
                return err
        }
        if err = f.Close(); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if err = ctx.Err(); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        target := filepath.Join(s.dir, digest+".apk")
        if err = os.Link(f.Name(), target); err != nil {
                if !errors.Is(err, os.ErrExist) {
                        return err
                }
                existing, err := os.ReadFile(target)
                if err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                if !bytes.Equal(existing, data) {
                        return errors.New("immutable artifact content conflict")
                }
        }
        dir, err := os.Open(s.dir)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        defer dir.Close()
        return dir.Sync()
}

type snapshot struct{ *bytes.Reader }

// Close satisfies the reader lifecycle contract; this verified snapshot owns only memory.
// Usage: defer reader.Close() after store.Open(ctx, digest).
func (snapshot) Close() error { return nil }

// Open verifies stored bytes and returns a seekable immutable snapshot, detecting on-disk corruption.
// Usage: reader, err := store.Open(ctx, digest); close reader after use.
func (s *Store) Open(ctx context.Context, digest string) (ports.ReadSeekCloser, error) {
        if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if !digestPattern.MatchString(digest) {
                return nil, errors.New("invalid artifact digest")
        }
        f, err := os.Open(filepath.Join(s.dir, digest+".apk"))
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer f.Close()
        data, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(f, 64<<20+1))
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        sum := sha256.Sum256(data)
        if hex.EncodeToString(sum[:]) != digest || len(data) > 64<<20 {
                return nil, errors.New("stored artifact integrity failure")
        }
        return snapshot{bytes.NewReader(data)}, nil
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
// Package postgres implements optional durable state storage using a host-owned database/sql pool.
package postgres

import (
        "context"
        "database/sql"
        "fmt"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/internal/sqlstore"
        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// Options selects a dedicated existing schema or explicitly opts in to initializing it.
// Schema must be a lowercase SQL identifier, at most 63 characters; no search_path changes are made.
type Options struct {
        Schema string
        // Initialize requires CREATE on the database/schema and creates only Kylok-owned objects.
        // Leave false at runtime after initialization; missing/incomplete/wrong-version schemas fail closed.
        Initialize bool
}

// Store implements the concurrent transactional state contract. PostgreSQL effects
// serialize per schema across processes using a transaction-level advisory lock.
// The host owns the pool, driver, TLS, credentials, limits and shutdown. No Close method owns that pool.
type Store struct{ ports.StateStore }

// Open verifies a dedicated schema on a supplied pool, optionally initializing it atomically.
// It never closes or reconfigures the pool, including on failure. It does not join host transactions.
// Usage: store, err := postgres.Open(ctx, db, postgres.Options{Schema: "kylok", Initialize: true}).
func Open(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, options Options) (*Store, error) {
        if options.Schema == "" {
                options.Schema = "kylok"
        }
        core, err := sqlstore.Open(ctx, db, "postgres", options.Schema, options.Initialize)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("open PostgreSQL state: %w", err)
        }
        return &Store{StateStore: core}, nil
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
// Package sqlite implements Kylok's durable single-node state store.
package sqlite

import (
        "context"
        "database/sql"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/internal/sqlstore"
        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
        _ "modernc.org/sqlite"
        "net/url"
        "os"
        "path/filepath"
)

// Store owns the SQLite pool and promotes the shared transactional state contract.
type Store struct {
        ports.StateStore
        pool *sql.DB
}

// Open requires a durable path. It never substitutes a temporary or memory DB.
// Usage: store, err := sqlite.Open(ctx, "/var/lib/kylok/state.sqlite").
func Open(ctx context.Context, path string) (*Store, error) {
        if path == "" || path == ":memory:" {
                return nil, errors.New("SQLite requires a durable file path")
        }
        path, err := filepath.Abs(path)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        // Ensure database and WAL inherit private directory/file permissions.
        f, err := os.OpenFile(path, os.O_CREATE|os.O_RDWR, 0600)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if err = f.Close(); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if err = os.Chmod(path, 0600); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        u := &url.URL{Scheme: "file", Path: path}
        db, err := sql.Open("sqlite", u.String()+"?_pragma=busy_timeout(5000)&_pragma=journal_mode(WAL)&_pragma=foreign_keys(1)&_pragma=synchronous(FULL)&_txlock=immediate")
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        db.SetMaxOpenConns(1)
        core, err := sqlstore.Open(ctx, db, "sqlite", "", true)
        if err != nil {
                db.Close()
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("migrate SQLite: %w", err)
        }
        return &Store{StateStore: core, pool: db}, nil
}

// Close releases the SQLite-owned pool after the host drains SDK users.
// Usage: defer store.Close().
func (s *Store) Close() error { return s.pool.Close() }

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package kylok

import (
        "context"
        "crypto/aes"
        "crypto/cipher"
        "crypto/ecdh"
        "crypto/ecdsa"
        "crypto/elliptic"
        "crypto/hkdf"
        "crypto/rand"
        "crypto/sha256"
        "crypto/x509"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "connectrpc.com/connect"
        kylokv1 "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/gen/kylok/v1"
        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
        "google.golang.org/protobuf/proto"
)

type agentService struct{ sdk *SDK }

// Encode a stable public reason/retryability detail without leaking backend errors or secrets.
func rpcError(code connect.Code, reason kylokv1.ErrorReason, retry bool) error {
        e := connect.NewError(code, errors.New(reason.String()))
        if detail, err := connect.NewErrorDetail(&kylokv1.ErrorDetail{Reason: reason, Retryable: retry}); err == nil {
                e.AddDetail(detail)
        }
        return e
}

// Map transactional/authentication failures into protocol status without revealing storage internals.
func storeError(err error) error {
        switch {
        case errors.Is(err, ports.ErrInvalidArgument):
                return rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
        case errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound):
                return rpcError(connect.CodeUnauthenticated, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_TOKEN_INVALID, false)
        case errors.Is(err, ports.ErrExpired):
                return rpcError(connect.CodeFailedPrecondition, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_TOKEN_EXPIRED, false)
        case errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConsumed):
                return rpcError(connect.CodeAlreadyExists, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_TOKEN_CONSUMED, false)
        case errors.Is(err, ports.ErrRecoveryExpired):
                return rpcError(connect.CodeFailedPrecondition, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_RECOVERY_EXPIRED, false)
        case errors.Is(err, ports.ErrRevoked):
                return rpcError(connect.CodePermissionDenied, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_DEVICE_REVOKED, false)
        case errors.Is(err, ports.ErrUnauthorized):
                return rpcError(connect.CodeUnauthenticated, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_UNAUTHENTICATED, false)
        case errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConflict):
                return rpcError(connect.CodeAlreadyExists, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_CONFLICT, false)
        case errors.Is(err, ports.ErrStale):
                return rpcError(connect.CodeFailedPrecondition, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_STALE_SEQUENCE, false)
        case errors.Is(err, context.Canceled):
                return connect.NewError(connect.CodeCanceled, context.Canceled)
        case errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded):
                return connect.NewError(connect.CodeDeadlineExceeded, context.DeadlineExceeded)
        default:
                return rpcError(connect.CodeUnavailable, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE, true)
        }
}

// Bound and validate self-reported ownership, capabilities and installed package metadata before persistence.
func validateInventory(i *kylokv1.Inventory) (ports.Inventory, error) {
        if i == nil || i.ApiLevel < 33 || i.ApiLevel > 37 || !i.DeviceOwner || len(i.Model) < 1 || len(i.Model) > 256 || len(i.BuildFingerprint) > 1024 || len(i.AgentVersion) < 1 || len(i.AgentVersion) > 128 {
                return ports.Inventory{}, rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
        }
        if len(i.Capabilities) > 32 || len(i.Applications) > 128 || len(i.SupportedAbis) > 16 {
                return ports.Inventory{}, rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
        }
        for _, value := range append(append([]string(nil), i.Capabilities...), i.SupportedAbis...) {
                if len(value) == 0 || len(value) > 64 {
                        return ports.Inventory{}, rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
                }
        }
        result := ports.Inventory{APILevel: i.ApiLevel, Model: i.Model, BuildFingerprint: i.BuildFingerprint, AgentVersion: i.AgentVersion, DeviceOwner: i.DeviceOwner, Capabilities: i.Capabilities, SupportedABIs: i.SupportedAbis}
        seen := map[string]bool{}
        for _, app := range i.Applications {
                if app.PackageName == "" || len(app.PackageName) > 256 || app.VersionCode < 1 || seen[app.PackageName] || len(app.SigningCertificates) == 0 || len(app.SigningCertificates) > 8 {
                        return result, rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
                }
                for _, cert := range app.SigningCertificates {
                        if !digestPattern.MatchString(cert) {
                                return result, rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
                        }
                }
                seen[app.PackageName] = true
                result.Applications = append(result.Applications, ports.InstalledApplication{PackageName: app.PackageName, VersionCode: app.VersionCode, SigningCertificates: app.SigningCertificates})
        }
        return result, nil
}

// Reject an unsupported wire version before interpreting its enrollment or sync fields.
func protocol(version uint32) error {
        if version != ProtocolVersion {
                return rpcError(connect.CodeFailedPrecondition, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_PROTOCOL_UNSUPPORTED, false)
        }
        return nil
}

// Enroll validates the attempt key and atomically claims or recovers an encrypted scoped identity.
// Usage: mounted AgentService handlers invoke this RPC after host listener registration.
func (a *agentService) Enroll(ctx context.Context, r *connect.Request[kylokv1.EnrollRequest]) (*connect.Response[kylokv1.EnrollResponse], error) {
        m := r.Msg
        if err := protocol(m.ProtocolVersion); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        inventory, err := validateInventory(m.Inventory)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if len(m.Token) != 43 || len(m.AttemptId) < 16 || !idPattern.MatchString(m.AttemptId) || len(m.PublicKey) > 256 {
                return nil, rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
        }
        public, err := x509.ParsePKIXPublicKey(m.PublicKey)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
        }
        ec, ok := public.(*ecdsa.PublicKey)
        if !ok || ec.Curve != elliptic.P256() {
                return nil, rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
        }
        recipient, err := ec.ECDH()
        if err != nil {
                return nil, rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
        }
        canonical, err := x509.MarshalPKIXPublicKey(ec)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, storeError(err)
        }
        keyHash := sha256.Sum256(canonical)
        enrollment, err := a.sdk.store.Enrollment(ctx, hash(m.Token))
        if err != nil {
                return nil, storeError(err)
        }
        id, err := secret()
        if err != nil {
                return nil, storeError(err)
        }
        credential, err := secret()
        if err != nil {
                return nil, storeError(err)
        }
        now := a.sdk.clock().UTC().Truncate(time.Millisecond)
        device := ports.Device{ID: id, ScopeID: enrollment.ScopeID, State: "ENROLLED", Inventory: inventory, CreatedAt: now}
        envelope, err := sealCredential(recipient, credential, device.ID, device.ScopeID, m.AttemptId)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, storeError(err)
        }
        response := &kylokv1.EnrollResponse{Device: deviceMessage(device), Credential: envelope, ProtocolVersion: ProtocolVersion}
        receipt, err := proto.Marshal(response)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, storeError(err)
        }
        receipt, err = a.sdk.store.Consume(ctx, ports.Attempt{TokenHash: hash(m.Token), ID: m.AttemptId, KeyHash: keyHash[:], Device: device, CredentialHash: hash(credential), Receipt: receipt, Now: now, RecoverUntil: now.Add(24 * time.Hour)})
        if err != nil {
                return nil, storeError(err)
        }
        if err = proto.Unmarshal(receipt, response); err != nil {
                return nil, storeError(err)
        }
        a.sdk.emit("device_enrolled", response.Device.ScopeId, response.Device.Id, response.Device.DesiredRevision, "")
        if response.Device == nil || response.Device.ScopeId != enrollment.ScopeID || response.Credential == nil || response.ProtocolVersion != ProtocolVersion {
                return nil, storeError(errors.New("invalid store receipt"))
        }
        return connect.NewResponse(response), nil
}

// Encrypt the device credential to the attempt's P-256 key with domain-separated authenticated binding.
func sealCredential(recipient *ecdh.PublicKey, credential, id, scope, attempt string) (*kylokv1.CredentialEnvelope, error) {
        ephemeral, err := ecdh.P256().GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        shared, err := ephemeral.ECDH(recipient)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        // The protocol label separates enrollment keys; 32 derived bytes select AES-256.
        key, err := hkdf.Key(sha256.New, shared, nil, "kylok/enrollment/v1", 32)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        block, err := aes.NewCipher(key)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        gcm, err := cipher.NewGCM(block)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        nonce := make([]byte, gcm.NonceSize())
        if _, err = rand.Read(nonce); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        public, err := x509.MarshalPKIXPublicKey(ephemeral.PublicKey())
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        // Bind ciphertext to the exact identity/attempt using the Android decoder's newline-separated encoding.
        aad := []byte(fmt.Sprintf("kylok/v1\n%s\n%s\n%s", id, scope, attempt))
        return &kylokv1.CredentialEnvelope{EphemeralPublicKey: public, Nonce: nonce, Ciphertext: gcm.Seal(nil, nonce, []byte(credential), aad)}, nil
}

// Sync validates bearer-bound requests and returns the transaction's durable snapshot using origin-relative APK paths.
// Usage: mounted AgentService handlers invoke this RPC after host listener registration.
func (a *agentService) Sync(ctx context.Context, r *connect.Request[kylokv1.SyncRequest]) (*connect.Response[kylokv1.SyncResponse], error) {
        if err := protocol(r.Msg.ProtocolVersion); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        inventory, err := validateInventory(r.Msg.Inventory)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        header := r.Header().Get("Authorization")
        if !strings.HasPrefix(header, "Bearer ") || len(header) != 7+43 || !devicePattern.MatchString(r.Msg.DeviceId) {
                return nil, rpcError(connect.CodeUnauthenticated, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_UNAUTHENTICATED, false)
        }
        if r.Msg.Sequence == 0 || r.Msg.Sequence > 1<<62 || len(r.Msg.Reports) > 64 || len(r.Msg.RetainedOperationIds) > 64 || len(r.Msg.CommandReports) > 16 {
                return nil, rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
        }
        input := ports.SyncInput{DeviceID: r.Msg.DeviceId, CredentialHash: hash(strings.TrimPrefix(header, "Bearer ")), Inventory: inventory, Now: a.sdk.clock().UTC(), Sequence: r.Msg.Sequence, ReportedRevision: r.Msg.ReportedRevision}
        seenRetained := map[string]bool{}
        for _, id := range r.Msg.RetainedOperationIds {
                if !devicePattern.MatchString(id) || seenRetained[id] {
                        return nil, rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
                }
                seenRetained[id] = true
                input.RetainedOperationIDs = append(input.RetainedOperationIDs, id)
        }
        if report := r.Msg.NetworkReport; report != nil {
                input.NetworkReport = &ports.NetworkReport{Revision: report.Revision, State: report.State, Reason: report.Reason, ActiveProfileID: report.ActiveProfileId, ConnectivityVerified: report.ConnectivityVerified, CleanupComplete: report.CleanupComplete}
                if report.Revision == 0 || report.Revision > r.Msg.ReportedRevision || len(report.Reason) > 128 || len(report.ActiveProfileId) > 128 {
                        return nil, rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
                }
        }
        seenCommands := map[string]bool{}
        for _, report := range r.Msg.CommandReports {
                if !devicePattern.MatchString(report.CommandId) || seenCommands[report.CommandId] || len(report.DispatchToken) != 48 || len(report.ErrorCode) > 128 || (report.State != "SUCCEEDED" && report.State != "FAILED" && report.State != "UNCERTAIN") {
                        return nil, rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
                }
                seenCommands[report.CommandId] = true
                result := ports.CommandReport{CommandID: report.CommandId, DispatchToken: report.DispatchToken, State: report.State, ErrorCode: report.ErrorCode}
                if value := report.Observation; value != nil {
                        result.Observation = &ports.CommandObservation{BootBefore: value.BootBefore, BootAfter: value.BootAfter, Secure: value.Secure, Locked: value.Locked}
                }
                input.CommandReports = append(input.CommandReports, result)
        }
        if report := r.Msg.PolicyReport; report != nil {
                if report.Revision == 0 || report.Revision > report.SnapshotRevision || report.SnapshotRevision > r.Msg.ReportedRevision || len(report.Results) > ports.MaximumPolicyResults {
                        return nil, rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
                }
                input.PolicyReport = &ports.PolicyReport{Revision: report.Revision, SnapshotRevision: report.SnapshotRevision}
                seen := map[string]bool{}
                for _, result := range report.Results {
                        state := strings.TrimPrefix(result.State.String(), "COMPLIANCE_STATE_")
                        if result.RuleId == "" || len(result.RuleId) > 600 || seen[result.RuleId] || len(result.Reason) > 128 || len(result.ObservedValue) > 20000 || (state != "APPLIED" && state != "PENDING" && state != "FAILED" && state != "UNSUPPORTED") {
                                return nil, rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
                        }
                        seen[result.RuleId] = true
                        input.PolicyReport.Results = append(input.PolicyReport.Results, ports.PolicyResult{RuleID: result.RuleId, State: state, Reason: result.Reason, ObservedValue: result.ObservedValue})
                }
        }
        for _, report := range r.Msg.Reports {
                state := strings.TrimPrefix(report.State.String(), "OPERATION_STATE_")
                if !idPattern.MatchString(report.Id) || !devicePattern.MatchString(report.OperationId) || len(report.LeaseId) != 48 || report.Sequence == 0 || report.Sequence > 1<<62 || report.DownloadedBytes < 0 || report.DownloadedBytes > 64<<20 || len(report.ErrorCode) > 128 || len(report.ErrorMessage) > 1024 || (state != "RUNNING" && state != "WAITING_USER" && state != "SUCCEEDED" && state != "FAILED") {
                        return nil, rpcError(connect.CodeInvalidArgument, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_INVALID_INPUT, false)
                }
                var observed *ports.InstalledApplication
                if report.Observed != nil {
                        observed = &ports.InstalledApplication{PackageName: report.Observed.PackageName, VersionCode: report.Observed.VersionCode, SigningCertificates: report.Observed.SigningCertificates}
                }
                input.Reports = append(input.Reports, ports.OperationReport{ID: report.Id, OperationID: report.OperationId, LeaseID: report.LeaseId, Sequence: report.Sequence, State: state, DownloadedBytes: report.DownloadedBytes, ErrorCode: report.ErrorCode, ErrorMessage: report.ErrorMessage, Observed: observed})
        }
        snapshot, err := a.sdk.store.Sync(ctx, input)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, storeError(err)
        }
        a.sdk.emit("device_synced", snapshot.Device.ScopeID, snapshot.Device.ID, snapshot.Device.DesiredRevision, fmt.Sprint(snapshot.Sequence))
        response := &kylokv1.SyncResponse{Device: deviceMessage(snapshot.Device), ProtocolVersion: ProtocolVersion, Sequence: snapshot.Sequence, StoppedOperationIds: snapshot.StoppedOperationIDs}
        response.DesiredNetworkPolicy, err = a.sdk.networkMessage(ctx, snapshot.Device.ScopeID, snapshot.Network.Desired)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, rpcError(connect.CodeUnavailable, kylokv1.ErrorReason_ERROR_REASON_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE, true)
        }
        response.CommandReceipts = snapshot.CommandReceipts
        for _, command := range snapshot.Commands {
                response.Commands = append(response.Commands, &kylokv1.Command{Id: command.ID, Kind: command.Kind, DispatchToken: command.DispatchToken, DeadlineUnixMillis: command.Deadline.UnixMilli()})
        }
        if want := snapshot.DesiredPolicy; want != nil {
                response.DesiredPolicy = &kylokv1.DesiredPolicy{Revision: want.Revision, Policy: policyMessage(want.Policy)}
        }
        for _, r := range snapshot.PolicyCompliance {
                response.PolicyCompliance = append(response.PolicyCompliance, &kylokv1.PolicyResult{RuleId: r.RuleID, State: kylokv1.ComplianceState(kylokv1.ComplianceState_value["COMPLIANCE_STATE_"+r.State]), Reason: r.Reason, ObservedValue: r.ObservedValue})
        }
        for _, d := range snapshot.Desired {
                response.DesiredApplications = append(response.DesiredApplications, &kylokv1.DesiredApplication{Artifact: artifactMessage(d.Artifact), Revision: d.Revision})
        }
        for _, op := range snapshot.Operations {
                // Relative paths keep downloads on the endpoint selected at enrollment,
                // including another LAN interface or a host-controlled domain alias.
                response.Operations = append(response.Operations, &kylokv1.Operation{Id: op.ID, Revision: op.Revision, Artifact: artifactMessage(op.Artifact), LeaseId: op.LeaseID, LeaseExpiresAtUnixMillis: op.LeaseUntil.UnixMilli(), DeadlineUnixMillis: op.Deadline.UnixMilli(), NotBeforeUnixMillis: windowStartMillis(op.NotBefore), State: kylokv1.OperationState(kylokv1.OperationState_value["OPERATION_STATE_"+op.State]), DownloadUrl: "/artifacts/" + op.ID + "/" + op.Artifact.SHA256 + ".apk"})
        }
        for _, r := range snapshot.Receipts {
                response.Receipts = append(response.Receipts, &kylokv1.ReportReceipt{ReportId: r.ReportID, Accepted: r.Accepted, Reason: r.Reason})
        }
        for _, c := range snapshot.Compliance {
                response.Compliance = append(response.Compliance, &kylokv1.ApplicationCompliance{PackageName: c.PackageName, DesiredRevision: c.Revision, State: kylokv1.ComplianceState(kylokv1.ComplianceState_value["COMPLIANCE_STATE_"+c.State]), Reason: c.Reason})
        }
        return connect.NewResponse(response), nil
}

// Convert verified store metadata to the shared wire shape without exposing artifact storage locations.
func artifactMessage(a ports.Artifact) *kylokv1.Artifact {
        return &kylokv1.Artifact{Sha256: a.SHA256, PackageName: a.PackageName, VersionCode: a.VersionCode, MinSdk: a.MinSDK, MaxSdk: a.MaxSDK, SigningCertificates: a.SigningCertificates, SupportedAbis: a.SupportedABIs, SizeBytes: a.SizeBytes}
}

// Encode immediate assignments as zero instead of Go's negative year-one Unix timestamp.
func windowStartMillis(value time.Time) int64 {
        if value.IsZero() {
                return 0
        }
        return value.UnixMilli()
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package kylok

import (
        "context"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/provisioning"
)

// ListApplications lists verified managed APKs in one authorized scope.
// Usage: page, err := sdk.ListApplications(ctx, scope, "", 100); use the final SHA256 as the next cursor.
func (s *SDK) ListApplications(ctx context.Context, scope, after string, limit int) ([]Artifact, error) {
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || limit < 1 || limit > 100 || (after != "" && !digestPattern.MatchString(after)) {
                return nil, invalidArgument("scope and valid cursor required; limit must be 1–100")
        }
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        values, err := s.store.ListArtifacts(ctx, scope, after, limit)
        result := make([]Artifact, 0, len(values))
        for _, value := range values {
                result = append(result, publicArtifact(value))
        }
        return result, err
}

// Bind verified APK properties to an explicit management scope before catalog publication.
func artifactMetadata(scope string, apk *provisioning.APK) ports.Artifact {
        m := apk.Metadata()
        return ports.Artifact{ScopeID: scope, SHA256: m.SHA256, PackageName: m.PackageName, VersionCode: m.VersionCode, MinSDK: uint32(m.MinSDK), MaxSDK: uint32(m.MaxSDK), SigningCertificates: m.SigningCertificates, SupportedABIs: m.SupportedABIs, SizeBytes: m.SizeBytes}
}

// RegisterApplication publishes verified immutable metadata in one authorized scope.
// Usage: artifact, err := sdk.RegisterApplication(ctx, scope, verifiedAPK).
func (s *SDK) RegisterApplication(ctx context.Context, scope string, apk *provisioning.APK) (Artifact, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return Artifact{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !apk.Verified() {
                return Artifact{}, invalidArgument("scope and verified application APK required")
        }
        if apk.Metadata().PackageName == s.bootstrap.Metadata().PackageName && !apk.IsDPC() {
                return Artifact{}, invalidArgument("self-update APK must retain the DPC provisioning components")
        }
        if apk.Metadata().Debuggable && !s.allowDebugAPK {
                return Artifact{}, invalidArgument("debuggable application requires AllowDebugAPK")
        }
        a := artifactMetadata(scope, apk)
        if err := s.artifacts.Put(ctx, a.SHA256, apk.Bytes()); err != nil {
                return publicArtifact(a), err
        }
        if err := s.store.RegisterArtifact(ctx, a); err != nil {
                return publicArtifact(a), err
        }
        return publicArtifact(a), nil
}

// AssignApplication creates a desired revision and one durable install operation.
// Reusing a key with the same device/artifact returns the original operation.
// Usage: operation, err := sdk.AssignApplication(ctx, scope, deviceID, digest, requestKey, 24*time.Hour).
func (s *SDK) AssignApplication(ctx context.Context, scope, device, digest, key string, timeout time.Duration) (Operation, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return Operation{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(device) || !digestPattern.MatchString(digest) || !idPattern.MatchString(key) || timeout < time.Minute || timeout > 7*24*time.Hour {
                return Operation{}, invalidArgument("valid scope/device/digest/idempotency key and timeout (1m–7d) required")
        }
        id, err := secret()
        if err != nil {
                return Operation{}, err
        }
        now := s.clock().UTC().Truncate(time.Millisecond)
        op, err := s.store.Assign(ctx, ports.Assignment{ScopeID: scope, DeviceID: device, ArtifactSHA256: digest, IdempotencyKey: key, OperationID: id, Now: now, Deadline: now.Add(timeout)})
        if err == nil {
                s.emit("application_assigned", scope, device, op.Revision, op.ID)
        }
        return publicOperation(op), err
}

// ListOperations returns durable scoped progress, failures and observed installation results.
// Usage: operations, err := sdk.ListOperations(ctx, scope, deviceID, "", 100).
func (s *SDK) ListOperations(ctx context.Context, scope, device, after string, limit int) ([]Operation, error) {
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(device) || limit < 1 || limit > 100 {
                return nil, invalidArgument("scope/device required; limit must be 1–100")
        }
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if after != "" && !devicePattern.MatchString(after) {
                return nil, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        values, err := s.store.ListOperations(ctx, scope, device, after, limit, s.clock().UTC())
        return publicOperations(values), err
}

// RemoveApplication removes unreferenced scoped catalog metadata. Content bytes
// are retained: deleting shared files concurrently with registrations is unsafe.
// Usage: err := sdk.RemoveApplication(ctx, scope, digest).
func (s *SDK) RemoveApplication(ctx context.Context, scope, digest string) error {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !digestPattern.MatchString(digest) {
                return invalidArgument("scope and digest required")
        }
        return s.store.RemoveArtifact(ctx, scope, digest)
}

// Accept only the exact opaque device credential form, never cookies or URL query credentials.
func bearer(value string) (string, bool) {
        if !strings.HasPrefix(value, "Bearer ") || len(value) != 50 {
                return "", false
        }
        return strings.TrimPrefix(value, "Bearer "), true
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package kylok

import (
        "bytes"
        "context"
        "encoding/base64"
        "encoding/json"
        "io"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

var ErrCursorExpired = ports.ErrCursorExpired

// Change is a durable scoped invalidation or application tombstone, without tokens or raw state.
// Refresh device_changed through GetDeviceState; application_changed through GetApplication.
// group_changed/fleet_changed require GetGroup/GetFleetAssignment.
// application_removed deletes that digest from the host projection. Sequence is strictly ascending per scope.
type Change struct {
        Sequence   uint64
        Kind       string
        ResourceID string
}

// ChangePage returns a checkpoint even when no records exist. Persist it with host projection updates;
// failures may replay a batch and must be handled idempotently. Changes are retained for 10,000 records per scope.
type ChangePage struct {
        Items  []Change
        Cursor string
        More   bool
}

// DesiredApplication is a host-facing verified desired package and revision.
type DesiredApplication struct {
        Artifact Artifact `json:"artifact"`
        Revision uint64   `json:"revision"`
}

// DeviceState is a consistent scoped projection, including complete operation history and desired/observed policy.
// Reading does not expire operations or trigger effects; deadline-aware reads use GetOperation/QueryOperations.
type DeviceState struct {
        Network    NetworkStatus        `json:"network"`
        Device     Device               `json:"device"`
        Desired    []DesiredApplication `json:"desired"`
        Operations []Operation          `json:"operations"`
        Commands   []Command            `json:"commands"`
        Policy     PolicyStatus         `json:"policy"`
}

// ProjectionItem is one snapshot resource: exactly one pointer is populated.
type ProjectionItem struct {
        Device      *DeviceState     `json:"device,omitempty"`
        Application *Artifact        `json:"application,omitempty"`
        Group       *Group           `json:"group,omitempty"`
        Fleet       *FleetAssignment `json:"fleet,omitempty"`
}

// ProjectionPage contains immutable scoped records. Build a staged projection across all pages,
// atomically replace the old projection, then use ChangesCursor after the final page.
// NextCursor expires after fifteen minutes or when this scope starts four newer scans.
type ProjectionPage struct {
        Items         []ProjectionItem
        NextCursor    string
        ChangesCursor string
        ExpiresAt     time.Time
}

type feedCursor struct {
        Version  int    `json:"v"`
        Scope    string `json:"s"`
        Kind     string `json:"k"`
        Sequence uint64 `json:"q"`
        Snapshot string `json:"n,omitempty"`
        After    int    `json:"a,omitempty"`
}

// Encode a scope-bound projection/change checkpoint; it never grants authorization to its holder.
func encodeFeedCursor(cursor feedCursor) string {
        data, _ := json.Marshal(cursor)
        return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(data)
}

// Strictly decode bounded cursor state, rejecting a different scope/kind and malformed snapshot continuations.
func decodeFeedCursor(value, scope, kind string) (feedCursor, error) {
        result := feedCursor{Version: 1, Scope: scope, Kind: kind}
        if value == "" {
                return result, nil
        }
        if len(value) > maximumCursorBytes {
                return result, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        data, err := base64.RawURLEncoding.DecodeString(value)
        if err != nil {
                return result, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        result = feedCursor{}
        decoder := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(data))
        decoder.DisallowUnknownFields()
        if err = decoder.Decode(&result); err != nil {
                return feedCursor{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        var extra any
        if decoder.Decode(&extra) != io.EOF || result.Version != 1 || result.Scope != scope || result.Kind != kind || result.After < 0 || result.Sequence > 1<<63-1 {
                return feedCursor{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        if kind == "changes" && (result.Snapshot != "" || result.After != 0) || kind == "projection" && (!devicePattern.MatchString(result.Snapshot) || result.Sequence != 0) {
                return feedCursor{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        return result, nil
}

// ListChanges reads a replayable bounded scope log. Empty cursor starts at sequence zero;
// ErrCursorExpired requires a consistent ReadProjection, never silently skipping missed changes.
// Hosts authorize the scope on every call; callbacks are only optional wakeups, not durable delivery.
// Usage: page, err := sdk.ListChanges(ctx, scope, savedCursor, 100).
func (s *SDK) ListChanges(ctx context.Context, scope, cursor string, limit int) (ChangePage, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return ChangePage{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || limit < 1 || limit > maximumPageSize {
                return ChangePage{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        checkpoint, err := decodeFeedCursor(cursor, scope, "changes")
        if err != nil {
                return ChangePage{}, err
        }
        page, err := s.store.ListChanges(ctx, scope, checkpoint.Sequence, limit)
        if err != nil {
                return ChangePage{}, err
        }
        result := ChangePage{Items: []Change{}, More: page.Next < page.Head}
        for _, item := range page.Items {
                result.Items = append(result.Items, Change{Sequence: item.Sequence, Kind: item.Kind, ResourceID: item.ResourceID})
        }
        result.Cursor = encodeFeedCursor(feedCursor{Version: 1, Scope: scope, Kind: "changes", Sequence: page.Next})
        return result, nil
}

// GetDeviceState reads a transactionally consistent scoped aggregate without exposing agent lease tokens.
// Usage: state, err := sdk.GetDeviceState(ctx, scope, deviceID).
func (s *SDK) GetDeviceState(ctx context.Context, scope, id string) (DeviceState, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return DeviceState{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(id) {
                return DeviceState{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        value, err := s.store.GetDeviceState(ctx, scope, id)
        if err != nil {
                return DeviceState{}, err
        }
        return publicDeviceState(value), nil
}

// GetApplication reads one scoped verified catalog entry; absent/foreign digests both return ErrNotFound.
// Usage: artifact, err := sdk.GetApplication(ctx, scope, digest).
func (s *SDK) GetApplication(ctx context.Context, scope, digest string) (Artifact, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return Artifact{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !digestPattern.MatchString(digest) {
                return Artifact{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        value, err := s.store.Artifact(ctx, scope, digest)
        if err != nil {
                return Artifact{}, err
        }
        return publicArtifact(value), nil
}

// ReadProjection starts or continues an immutable scoped snapshot and matching change watermark.
// Cursor "" starts a new scan. Complete all pages before committing ChangesCursor; expiry/eviction
// returns ErrCursorExpired and requires discarding the staged scan. Never combine pages from different scans.
// Usage: page, err := sdk.ReadProjection(ctx, scope, "", 100).
func (s *SDK) ReadProjection(ctx context.Context, scope, cursor string, limit int) (ProjectionPage, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return ProjectionPage{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || limit < 1 || limit > maximumPageSize {
                return ProjectionPage{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        checkpoint, err := decodeFeedCursor(cursor, scope, "projection")
        if err != nil {
                return ProjectionPage{}, err
        }
        page, err := s.store.ReadProjection(ctx, scope, checkpoint.Snapshot, checkpoint.After, limit, s.clock().UTC())
        if err != nil {
                return ProjectionPage{}, err
        }
        result := ProjectionPage{Items: []ProjectionItem{}, ExpiresAt: page.ExpiresAt, ChangesCursor: encodeFeedCursor(feedCursor{Version: 1, Scope: scope, Kind: "changes", Sequence: page.Watermark})}
        for _, item := range page.Items {
                value := ProjectionItem{}
                if item.Device != nil {
                        device := publicDeviceState(*item.Device)
                        value.Device = &device
                }
                if item.Application != nil {
                        artifact := publicArtifact(*item.Application)
                        value.Application = &artifact
                }
                if item.Group != nil {
                        value.Group = item.Group
                }
                if item.Fleet != nil {
                        value.Fleet = item.Fleet
                }
                result.Items = append(result.Items, value)
        }
        if page.More {
                result.NextCursor = encodeFeedCursor(feedCursor{Version: 1, Scope: scope, Kind: "projection", Snapshot: page.SnapshotID, After: page.After})
        }
        return result, nil
}

// Project detached aggregate values, stripping installer authorization/checkpoints from operation records.
func publicDeviceState(value ports.DeviceState) DeviceState {
        result := DeviceState{Network: value.Network, Device: publicDevice(value.Device), Operations: publicOperations(value.Operations), Policy: value.Policy, Desired: []DesiredApplication{}}
        for _, command := range value.Commands {
                result.Commands = append(result.Commands, publicCommand(command))
        }
        for _, desired := range value.Desired {
                result.Desired = append(result.Desired, DesiredApplication{Artifact: publicArtifact(desired.Artifact), Revision: desired.Revision})
        }
        return result
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package main

import (
        "context"
        "encoding/json"
        "flag"
        "log"
        "os"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/provisioning"
)

// Inspect a signed APK through public provisioning APIs and emit metadata for host tooling.
func main() {
        path := flag.String("apk", "", "signed APK to verify and inspect")
        application := flag.Bool("application", false, "inspect an ordinary managed app instead of requiring DPC components")
        signer := flag.String("apksigner", "apksigner", "Android SDK apksigner executable")
        analyzer := flag.String("apkanalyzer", "apkanalyzer", "Android SDK apkanalyzer executable")
        flag.Parse()
        inspect := provisioning.Inspect
        if *application {
                inspect = provisioning.InspectApplication
        }
        apk, err := inspect(context.Background(), *path, provisioning.Tools{APKSigner: *signer, APKAnalyzer: *analyzer})
        if err != nil {
                log.Fatal(err)
        }
        if err := json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(apk.Metadata()); err != nil {
                log.Fatal(err)
        }
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
// Command doc-index extracts handwritten Go declarations for documentation and comment coverage checks.
package main

import (
        "bytes"
        "encoding/json"
        "flag"
        "go/ast"
        "go/parser"
        "go/printer"
        "go/token"
        "os"
        "path/filepath"
        "strings"
)

// declaration preserves a source signature and its adjacent purpose/usage documentation.
type declaration struct {
        Name      string `json:"name"`
        File      string `json:"file"`
        Line      int    `json:"line"`
        Public    bool   `json:"public"`
        Kind      string `json:"kind"`
        Signature string `json:"signature"`
        Doc       string `json:"doc"`
}

// signature prints syntax without a function body so implementation statements cannot masquerade as API.
func signature(set *token.FileSet, node any) string {
        var b bytes.Buffer
        _ = printer.Fprint(&b, set, node)
        return b.String()
}

// main walks selected source trees and emits declarations without generated or test helpers.
func main() {
        root := flag.String("root", ".", "repository root")
        flag.Parse()
        set := token.NewFileSet()
        records := []declaration{}
        for _, dir := range []string{"go", "examples/minimal-server"} {
                err := filepath.WalkDir(filepath.Join(*root, dir), func(path string, entry os.DirEntry, err error) error {
                        if err != nil {
                                return err
                        }
                        if entry.IsDir() {
                                if entry.Name() == "gen" || entry.Name() == "build" {
                                        return filepath.SkipDir
                                }
                                return nil
                        }
                        if !strings.HasSuffix(path, ".go") || strings.HasSuffix(path, "_test.go") {
                                return nil
                        }
                        data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
                        if err != nil {
                                return err
                        }
                        file, err := parser.ParseFile(set, path, data, parser.ParseComments)
                        if err != nil {
                                return err
                        }
                        // Use Go's header marker rather than matching examples or string literals inside handwritten tools.
                        if ast.IsGenerated(file) {
                                return nil
                        }
                        rel, _ := filepath.Rel(*root, path)
                        for _, decl := range file.Decls {
                                switch node := decl.(type) {
                                case *ast.FuncDecl:
                                        copy := *node
                                        copy.Body = nil
                                        name := node.Name.Name
                                        if node.Recv != nil {
                                                name = signature(set, node.Recv.List[0].Type) + "." + name
                                        }
                                        records = append(records, declaration{name, filepath.ToSlash(rel), set.Position(node.Pos()).Line, ast.IsExported(node.Name.Name), "method", signature(set, &copy), node.Doc.Text()})
                                case *ast.GenDecl:
                                        // Standard AST filtering keeps constants/errors and public fields while hiding implementation state.
                                        ast.FileExports(&ast.File{Decls: []ast.Decl{node}})
                                        for _, spec := range node.Specs {
                                                if value, ok := spec.(*ast.ValueSpec); ok {
                                                        doc := node.Doc.Text()
                                                        if value.Doc != nil {
                                                                doc = value.Doc.Text()
                                                        }
                                                        for _, name := range value.Names {
                                                                records = append(records, declaration{name.Name, filepath.ToSlash(rel), set.Position(value.Pos()).Line, true, "value", node.Tok.String() + " " + signature(set, value), doc})
                                                        }
                                                        continue
                                                }
                                                typeSpec, ok := spec.(*ast.TypeSpec)
                                                if !ok || !ast.IsExported(typeSpec.Name.Name) {
                                                        continue
                                                }
                                                doc := node.Doc.Text()
                                                if typeSpec.Doc != nil {
                                                        doc = typeSpec.Doc.Text()
                                                }
                                                records = append(records, declaration{typeSpec.Name.Name, filepath.ToSlash(rel), set.Position(spec.Pos()).Line, true, "type", "type " + signature(set, typeSpec), doc})
                                                if contract, ok := typeSpec.Type.(*ast.InterfaceType); ok {
                                                        for _, method := range contract.Methods.List {
                                                                for _, name := range method.Names {
                                                                        records = append(records, declaration{typeSpec.Name.Name + "." + name.Name, filepath.ToSlash(rel), set.Position(method.Pos()).Line, true, "method", name.Name + strings.TrimPrefix(signature(set, method.Type), "func"), method.Doc.Text()})
                                                                }
                                                        }
                                                }
                                        }
                                }
                        }
                        return nil
                })
                if err != nil {
                        panic(err)
                }
        }
        if err := json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(records); err != nil {
                panic(err)
        }
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package kylok

import (
        "context"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

var ErrUnsupported = ports.ErrUnsupported

// CommandKind selects a one-shot Device Owner action, subject to platform capability and observed outcome.
type CommandKind string

const (
        CommandReboot CommandKind = "REBOOT"
        CommandLock   CommandKind = "LOCK"
)

// CommandObservation is platform readback, not proof of who caused a reboot or cryptographic device attestation.
type CommandObservation = ports.CommandObservation

// Command exposes a one-shot lifecycle without the agent's dispatch credential.
// UNCERTAIN requires an explicit host decision; retrying the same key never schedules another action.
type Command struct {
        ID          string              `json:"id"`
        DeviceID    string              `json:"device_id"`
        Kind        CommandKind         `json:"kind"`
        State       string              `json:"state"`
        Deadline    time.Time           `json:"deadline"`
        ErrorCode   string              `json:"error_code,omitempty"`
        Observation *CommandObservation `json:"observation,omitempty"`
}

// CommandPage contains a live ascending-ID history page for one authorized device.
type CommandPage struct {
        Items      []Command
        NextCursor string
}

// SubmitCommand queues reboot or lock only when the device advertises the corresponding Device Owner capability.
// A stable key binds device/kind; its original deadline is retained on retry (timeout 1m–7d).
// Reboot success needs a boot-count increase; lock success needs a configured secure lock and locked readback.
// Usage: command, err := sdk.SubmitCommand(ctx, scope, deviceID, kylok.CommandReboot, "maintenance", time.Hour).
func (s *SDK) SubmitCommand(ctx context.Context, scope, device string, kind CommandKind, key string, timeout time.Duration) (Command, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return Command{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(device) || !idPattern.MatchString(key) || kind != CommandReboot && kind != CommandLock || timeout < time.Minute || timeout > 7*24*time.Hour {
                return Command{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        id, err := secret()
        if err != nil {
                return Command{}, err
        }
        now := s.clock().UTC().Truncate(time.Millisecond)
        command, err := s.store.SubmitCommand(ctx, ports.CommandAssignment{ScopeID: scope, DeviceID: device, Kind: string(kind), IdempotencyKey: key, ID: id, Now: now, Deadline: now.Add(timeout)})
        return publicCommand(command), err
}

// GetCommand reads an authorized device's one-shot outcome; overdue delivered actions are UNCERTAIN, never replayed.
// Usage: command, err := sdk.GetCommand(ctx, scope, deviceID, commandID).
func (s *SDK) GetCommand(ctx context.Context, scope, device, id string) (Command, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return Command{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(device) || !devicePattern.MatchString(id) {
                return Command{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        command, err := s.store.GetCommand(ctx, scope, device, id, s.clock().UTC())
        return publicCommand(command), err
}

// QueryCommands reads bounded command history with a scope/device-bound cursor; it never triggers a platform action.
// Usage: page, err := sdk.QueryCommands(ctx, scope, deviceID, PageRequest{Limit:20}).
func (s *SDK) QueryCommands(ctx context.Context, scope, device string, page PageRequest) (CommandPage, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return CommandPage{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(device) {
                return CommandPage{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        binding := cursorBinding("commands", scope, device, "", "")
        after, limit, err := readPage(page, binding)
        if err != nil {
                return CommandPage{}, err
        }
        values, err := s.store.ListCommands(ctx, scope, device, after, limit+1, s.clock().UTC())
        if err != nil {
                return CommandPage{}, err
        }
        result := CommandPage{}
        if len(values) > limit {
                result.NextCursor = nextPage(binding, values[limit-1].ID)
                values = values[:limit]
        }
        for _, command := range values {
                result.Items = append(result.Items, publicCommand(command))
        }
        return result, nil
}

// CancelCommand cancels queued work; committed delivery becomes UNCERTAIN until a valid platform result resolves it.
// Cancellation cannot undo a delivered reboot/lock, and never schedules a replacement action.
// Usage: command, err := sdk.CancelCommand(ctx, scope, deviceID, commandID).
func (s *SDK) CancelCommand(ctx context.Context, scope, device, id string) (Command, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return Command{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(device) || !devicePattern.MatchString(id) {
                return Command{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        command, err := s.store.CancelCommand(ctx, scope, device, id, s.clock().UTC())
        return publicCommand(command), err
}

// Strip the stable dispatch token and detach the observation before returning host domain data.
func publicCommand(value ports.Command) Command {
        result := Command{ID: value.ID, DeviceID: value.DeviceID, Kind: CommandKind(value.Kind), State: value.State, Deadline: value.Deadline, ErrorCode: value.ErrorCode}
        if value.Observation != nil {
                observed := *value.Observation
                result.Observation = &observed
        }
        return result
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package kylok

import (
        "bytes"
        "crypto/sha256"
        "encoding/hex"
        "io"
        "net/http"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/provisioning"
)

// Serve immutable managed APK ranges only after device, operation and lease authorization.
func (s *SDK) applicationHandler() http.Handler {
        return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
                if r.Method != "GET" && r.Method != "HEAD" {
                        w.Header().Set("Allow", "GET, HEAD")
                        w.WriteHeader(405)
                        return
                }
                parts := strings.Split(strings.TrimPrefix(r.URL.Path, "/artifacts/"), "/")
                if len(parts) != 2 || !devicePattern.MatchString(parts[0]) || !strings.HasSuffix(parts[1], ".apk") {
                        http.NotFound(w, r)
                        return
                }
                digest := strings.TrimSuffix(parts[1], ".apk")
                credential, ok := bearer(r.Header.Get("Authorization"))
                id := r.Header.Get("X-Kylok-Device-ID")
                if !ok || !devicePattern.MatchString(id) || !digestPattern.MatchString(digest) {
                        http.Error(w, "Unauthorized", 401)
                        return
                }
                a, err := s.store.AuthorizeDownload(r.Context(), id, hash(credential), parts[0], digest, r.Header.Get("X-Kylok-Lease-ID"), s.clock().UTC())
                if err != nil {
                        http.Error(w, "Unauthorized", 403)
                        return
                }
                reader, err := s.artifacts.Open(r.Context(), digest)
                if err != nil {
                        http.Error(w, "Artifact unavailable", 503)
                        return
                }
                defer reader.Close()
                data, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(reader, provisioning.MaxAPKBytes+1))
                sum := sha256.Sum256(data)
                if err != nil || int64(len(data)) != a.SizeBytes || len(data) > provisioning.MaxAPKBytes || hex.EncodeToString(sum[:]) != digest {
                        http.Error(w, "Artifact integrity failure", 500)
                        return
                }
                w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/vnd.android.package-archive")
                w.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "private, no-store")
                w.Header().Set("ETag", `"`+digest+`"`)
                w.Header().Set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
                http.ServeContent(w, r, a.PackageName+".apk", time.Time{}, bytes.NewReader(data))
        })
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package kylok

import (
        "context"
        "fmt"
        "time"
)

// Event contains identifiers and committed revisions, never tokens, credentials,
// enrollment keys, QR contents or raw inventory. ID is stable across RPC retries.
type Event struct {
        ID       string    `json:"id"`
        Kind     string    `json:"kind"`
        ScopeID  string    `json:"scope_id"`
        DeviceID string    `json:"device_id"`
        Revision uint64    `json:"revision"`
        At       time.Time `json:"at"`
}

// EventSink is an optional best-effort notification hook. Calls are serialized on
// one SDK-owned worker and must honor cancellation. Errors/panics never change
// committed results. Delivery can be dropped, duplicated, or lost on shutdown;
// use ListChanges/ReadProjection for durable projection recovery and export permanent audit records to host storage before retention expires.
type EventSink func(context.Context, Event) error

type eventDispatcher struct {
        ctx     context.Context
        cancel  context.CancelFunc
        queue   chan Event
        done    chan struct{}
        timeout time.Duration
        sink    EventSink
}

// Create one bounded notification worker only when a host supplies a sink.
func newEvents(sink EventSink, timeout time.Duration) *eventDispatcher {
        if sink == nil {
                return nil
        }
        ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
        // Keep at most 64 pending notifications; slow hosts drop events instead of blocking committed SDK work.
        d := &eventDispatcher{ctx: ctx, cancel: cancel, queue: make(chan Event, 64), done: make(chan struct{}), timeout: timeout, sink: sink}
        go func() {
                defer close(d.done)
                for {
                        select {
                        case <-ctx.Done():
                                return
                        case e := <-d.queue:
                                if ctx.Err() != nil {
                                        return
                                }
                                d.deliver(e)
                        }
                }
        }()
        return d
}

// Give each host callback a deadline and contain its errors/panics outside committed SDK work.
func (d *eventDispatcher) deliver(e Event) {
        ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(d.ctx, d.timeout)
        defer cancel()
        defer func() { _ = recover() }()
        _ = d.sink(ctx, e)
}

// Cancel notification delivery and bound shutdown even if a host callback ignores cancellation.
func (d *eventDispatcher) close() {
        if d == nil {
                return
        }
        d.cancel()
        timer := time.NewTimer(d.timeout)
        defer timer.Stop()
        select {
        case <-d.done:
        case <-timer.C:
        }
}

// Queue sanitized committed identifiers without delaying authentication or leaking enrollment credentials.
func (s *SDK) emit(kind, scope, device string, revision uint64, suffix string) {
        if s.events == nil {
                return
        }
        e := Event{ID: fmt.Sprintf("%s:%s:%s", kind, device, suffix), Kind: kind, ScopeID: scope, DeviceID: device, Revision: revision, At: s.clock().UTC()}
        select {
        case <-s.events.ctx.Done():
        case s.events.queue <- e:
        default:
        }
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package kylok

import (
        "context"
        "slices"
        "sort"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// Group is an opaque scoped membership set, capped at 100 devices; it carries no business metadata.
type Group = ports.Group

// FleetRequest selects one group or an explicit device set with an absolute deployment window.
// Request ID is the scope-local retry key; membership freezes on creation.
type FleetRequest = ports.FleetRequest

// FleetItem describes submission progress; use OperationID to read the device's observed execution result.
type FleetItem = ports.FleetItem

// FleetAssignment is a detached durable fan-out checkpoint, not a claim that devices applied its APK.
type FleetAssignment = ports.FleetAssignment

// GroupPage contains a live ascending-ID membership page and a scope-bound continuation.
type GroupPage struct {
        Items      []Group
        NextCursor string
}

// FleetPage contains durable assignment checkpoints; hosts scan these to resume incomplete work after restart.
type FleetPage struct {
        Items      []FleetAssignment
        NextCursor string
}

// Validate and canonicalize the target set without mutating a host-owned slice.
func validFleetTargets(ids []string) ([]string, error) {
        if len(ids) > ports.MaximumFleetDevices {
                return nil, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        result := slices.Clone(ids)
        sort.Strings(result)
        for i, id := range result {
                if !devicePattern.MatchString(id) || i > 0 && result[i-1] == id {
                        return nil, ErrInvalidArgument
                }
        }
        return result, nil
}

// ReplaceGroup replaces the complete membership using optimistic concurrency (zero creates).
// An identical retry with the same expected revision returns the committed set; foreign/revoked members fail.
// Existing fleet assignments keep their frozen targets. An empty set clears membership.
// Usage: group, err := sdk.ReplaceGroup(ctx, scope, "lobby", []string{deviceID}, 0).
func (s *SDK) ReplaceGroup(ctx context.Context, scope, id string, devices []string, expectedRevision uint64) (Group, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return Group{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !idPattern.MatchString(id) || expectedRevision >= 1<<62 {
                return Group{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        ids, err := validFleetTargets(devices)
        if err != nil {
                return Group{}, err
        }
        return s.store.ReplaceGroup(ctx, Group{ScopeID: scope, ID: id, DeviceIDs: ids}, expectedRevision)
}

// GetGroup reads membership in one authorized scope; foreign and absent IDs both return ErrNotFound.
// Usage: group, err := sdk.GetGroup(ctx, scope, "lobby").
func (s *SDK) GetGroup(ctx context.Context, scope, id string) (Group, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return Group{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !idPattern.MatchString(id) {
                return Group{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        return s.store.GetGroup(ctx, scope, id)
}

// QueryGroups reads a bounded live membership page. Hosts authorize the scope on every call.
// Usage: page, err := sdk.QueryGroups(ctx, scope, PageRequest{Limit:20}).
func (s *SDK) QueryGroups(ctx context.Context, scope string, page PageRequest) (GroupPage, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return GroupPage{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) {
                return GroupPage{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        binding := cursorBinding("groups", scope, "", "", "")
        after, limit, err := readKeysetPage(page, binding, idPattern)
        if err != nil {
                return GroupPage{}, err
        }
        items, err := s.store.ListGroups(ctx, scope, after, limit+1)
        if err != nil {
                return GroupPage{}, err
        }
        result := GroupPage{Items: items}
        if len(items) > limit {
                result.NextCursor = nextPage(binding, items[limit-1].ID)
                result.Items = items[:limit]
        }
        return result, nil
}

// ScheduleApplication creates one durable install with an absolute [notBefore, deadline) window (at most seven days).
// The same key binds artifact and window exactly. The latest committed assignment wins per package;
// cancellation/supersession cannot undo an already delivered platform effect. Expired retries still read the original.
// Usage: op, err := sdk.ScheduleApplication(ctx, scope, deviceID, digest, "nightly", start, start.Add(time.Hour)).
func (s *SDK) ScheduleApplication(ctx context.Context, scope, device, digest, key string, notBefore, deadline time.Time) (Operation, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return Operation{}, err
        }
        notBefore, deadline = notBefore.UTC().Truncate(time.Millisecond), deadline.UTC().Truncate(time.Millisecond)
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(device) || !digestPattern.MatchString(digest) || !idPattern.MatchString(key) || notBefore.UnixMilli() <= 0 || !notBefore.Before(deadline) || deadline.Sub(notBefore) > 7*24*time.Hour {
                return Operation{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        id, err := secret()
        if err != nil {
                return Operation{}, err
        }
        op, err := s.store.Assign(ctx, ports.Assignment{ScopeID: scope, DeviceID: device, ArtifactSHA256: digest, IdempotencyKey: key, OperationID: id, Now: s.clock().UTC(), NotBefore: notBefore, Deadline: deadline})
        return publicOperation(op), err
}

// CreateFleetAssignment freezes up to 100 targets without submitting device work. Set GroupID or DeviceIDs, never both.
// Reusing ID requires the same selector, APK and absolute window. Group edits do not expand retries.
// Hosts drive AdvanceFleetAssignment and retain a scan/checkpoint for their authorized scopes.
// Usage: batch, err := sdk.CreateFleetAssignment(ctx, FleetRequest{ScopeID:scope, ID:"nightly", GroupID:"lobby", ArtifactSHA256:digest, NotBefore:start, Deadline:start.Add(time.Hour)}).
func (s *SDK) CreateFleetAssignment(ctx context.Context, request FleetRequest) (FleetAssignment, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return FleetAssignment{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(request.ScopeID) || !idPattern.MatchString(request.ID) || !digestPattern.MatchString(request.ArtifactSHA256) || request.GroupID != "" && !idPattern.MatchString(request.GroupID) {
                return FleetAssignment{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        ids, err := validFleetTargets(request.DeviceIDs)
        if err != nil {
                return FleetAssignment{}, err
        }
        request.DeviceIDs = ids
        request.NotBefore = request.NotBefore.UTC().Truncate(time.Millisecond)
        request.Deadline = request.Deadline.UTC().Truncate(time.Millisecond)
        return s.store.CreateFleetAssignment(ctx, request, s.clock().UTC())
}

// GetFleetAssignment reads frozen targets and per-device submission results, without advancing work.
// Usage: batch, err := sdk.GetFleetAssignment(ctx, scope, "nightly").
func (s *SDK) GetFleetAssignment(ctx context.Context, scope, id string) (FleetAssignment, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return FleetAssignment{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !idPattern.MatchString(id) {
                return FleetAssignment{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        return s.store.GetFleetAssignment(ctx, scope, id)
}

// QueryFleetAssignments scans durable checkpoints so host workers can resume pending fan-out after restart.
// Usage: page, err := sdk.QueryFleetAssignments(ctx, scope, PageRequest{Limit:20}).
func (s *SDK) QueryFleetAssignments(ctx context.Context, scope string, page PageRequest) (FleetPage, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return FleetPage{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) {
                return FleetPage{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        binding := cursorBinding("fleet", scope, "", "", "")
        after, limit, err := readKeysetPage(page, binding, idPattern)
        if err != nil {
                return FleetPage{}, err
        }
        items, err := s.store.ListFleetAssignments(ctx, scope, after, limit+1)
        if err != nil {
                return FleetPage{}, err
        }
        result := FleetPage{Items: items}
        if len(items) > limit {
                result.NextCursor = nextPage(binding, items[limit-1].Request.ID)
                result.Items = items[:limit]
        }
        return result, nil
}

// AdvanceFleetAssignment atomically submits at most limit pending targets (1–100); permanent per-device errors are retained.
// Infrastructure/context failures roll back the chunk. Concurrent workers and lost responses cannot create duplicate work.
// Complete means fan-out finished; read each OperationID for installation/compliance results.
// Usage: batch, err := sdk.AdvanceFleetAssignment(ctx, scope, "nightly", 20).
func (s *SDK) AdvanceFleetAssignment(ctx context.Context, scope, id string, limit int) (FleetAssignment, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return FleetAssignment{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !idPattern.MatchString(id) || limit < 1 || limit > ports.MaximumFleetDevices {
                return FleetAssignment{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        return s.store.AdvanceFleetAssignment(ctx, scope, id, s.clock().UTC(), limit)
}

// CancelFleetAssignment cancels pending targets and stops future dispatch for active submitted operations.
// Completed/superseded operations are preserved; already delivered Android effects may still complete.
// Usage: batch, err := sdk.CancelFleetAssignment(ctx, scope, "nightly").
func (s *SDK) CancelFleetAssignment(ctx context.Context, scope, id string) (FleetAssignment, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return FleetAssignment{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !idPattern.MatchString(id) {
                return FleetAssignment{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        return s.store.CancelFleetAssignment(ctx, scope, id, s.clock().UTC())
}

// CancelOperation stops future dispatch/download and signals the agent to abandon uncommitted local work.
// CANCELLED does not prove that a delivered installation stopped. Desired intent stays recorded;
// no implicit uninstall/rollback or drift retry follows. Reassign with a new key to resume.
// Usage: op, err := sdk.CancelOperation(ctx, scope, deviceID, operationID).
func (s *SDK) CancelOperation(ctx context.Context, scope, device, id string) (Operation, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return Operation{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(device) || !devicePattern.MatchString(id) {
                return Operation{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        op, err := s.store.CancelOperation(ctx, scope, device, id, s.clock().UTC())
        return publicOperation(op), err
}

// Full replacement. Omitted settings release previously managed settings to
// their captured baseline; an empty document releases all Kylok policy controls.

        state                   protoimpl.MessageState  `protogen:"open.v1"`

        MaximumTimeToLockMillis *int64                  `protobuf:"varint,7,opt,name=maximum_time_to_lock_millis,json=maximumTimeToLockMillis,proto3,oneof" json:"maximum_time_to_lock_millis,omitempty"`

// AgentServiceClient is a client for the kylok.v1.AgentService service.
type AgentServiceClient interface {
        Enroll(context.Context, *connect.Request[v1.EnrollRequest]) (*connect.Response[v1.EnrollResponse], error)
        // Atomic inventory/report exchange and durable desired-state delivery.
        Sync(context.Context, *connect.Request[v1.SyncRequest]) (*connect.Response[v1.SyncResponse], error)
}

// NewAgentServiceClient constructs a client for the kylok.v1.AgentService service. By default, it
// uses the Connect protocol with the binary Protobuf Codec, asks for gzipped responses, and sends
// uncompressed requests. To use the gRPC or gRPC-Web protocols, supply the connect.WithGRPC() or
// connect.WithGRPCWeb() options.
//
// The URL supplied here should be the base URL for the Connect or gRPC server (for example,
// http://api.acme.com or https://acme.com/grpc).
func NewAgentServiceClient(httpClient connect.HTTPClient, baseURL string, opts ...connect.ClientOption) AgentServiceClient {
        baseURL = strings.TrimRight(baseURL, "/")
        agentServiceMethods := v1.File_kylok_v1_agent_proto.Services().ByName("AgentService").Methods()
        return &agentServiceClient{
                enroll: connect.NewClient[v1.EnrollRequest, v1.EnrollResponse](
                        httpClient,
                        baseURL+AgentServiceEnrollProcedure,
                        connect.WithSchema(agentServiceMethods.ByName("Enroll")),
                        connect.WithClientOptions(opts...),
                ),
                sync: connect.NewClient[v1.SyncRequest, v1.SyncResponse](
                        httpClient,
                        baseURL+AgentServiceSyncProcedure,
                        connect.WithSchema(agentServiceMethods.ByName("Sync")),
                        connect.WithClientOptions(opts...),
                ),
        }
}

// agentServiceClient implements AgentServiceClient.
type agentServiceClient struct {
        enroll *connect.Client[v1.EnrollRequest, v1.EnrollResponse]
        sync   *connect.Client[v1.SyncRequest, v1.SyncResponse]
}

// Enroll calls kylok.v1.AgentService.Enroll.
func (c *agentServiceClient) Enroll(ctx context.Context, req *connect.Request[v1.EnrollRequest]) (*connect.Response[v1.EnrollResponse], error) {
        return c.enroll.CallUnary(ctx, req)
}

// Sync calls kylok.v1.AgentService.Sync.
func (c *agentServiceClient) Sync(ctx context.Context, req *connect.Request[v1.SyncRequest]) (*connect.Response[v1.SyncResponse], error) {
        return c.sync.CallUnary(ctx, req)
}

// AgentServiceHandler is an implementation of the kylok.v1.AgentService service.
type AgentServiceHandler interface {
        Enroll(context.Context, *connect.Request[v1.EnrollRequest]) (*connect.Response[v1.EnrollResponse], error)
        // Atomic inventory/report exchange and durable desired-state delivery.
        Sync(context.Context, *connect.Request[v1.SyncRequest]) (*connect.Response[v1.SyncResponse], error)
}
