// NewAgentServiceHandler builds an HTTP handler from the service implementation. It returns the
// path on which to mount the handler and the handler itself.
//
// By default, handlers support the Connect, gRPC, and gRPC-Web protocols with the binary Protobuf
// and JSON codecs. They also support gzip compression.
func NewAgentServiceHandler(svc AgentServiceHandler, opts ...connect.HandlerOption) (string, http.Handler) {
        agentServiceMethods := v1.File_kylok_v1_agent_proto.Services().ByName("AgentService").Methods()
        agentServiceEnrollHandler := connect.NewUnaryHandler(
                AgentServiceEnrollProcedure,
                svc.Enroll,
                connect.WithSchema(agentServiceMethods.ByName("Enroll")),
                connect.WithHandlerOptions(opts...),
        )
        agentServiceSyncHandler := connect.NewUnaryHandler(
                AgentServiceSyncProcedure,
                svc.Sync,
                connect.WithSchema(agentServiceMethods.ByName("Sync")),
                connect.WithHandlerOptions(opts...),
        )
        return "/kylok.v1.AgentService/", http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
                switch r.URL.Path {
                case AgentServiceEnrollProcedure:
                        agentServiceEnrollHandler.ServeHTTP(w, r)
                case AgentServiceSyncProcedure:
                        agentServiceSyncHandler.ServeHTTP(w, r)
                default:
                        http.NotFound(w, r)
                }
        })
}

// UnimplementedAgentServiceHandler returns CodeUnimplemented from all methods.
type UnimplementedAgentServiceHandler struct{}

func (UnimplementedAgentServiceHandler) Enroll(context.Context, *connect.Request[v1.EnrollRequest]) (*connect.Response[v1.EnrollResponse], error) {
        return nil, connect.NewError(connect.CodeUnimplemented, errors.New("kylok.v1.AgentService.Enroll is not implemented"))
}

func (UnimplementedAgentServiceHandler) Sync(context.Context, *connect.Request[v1.SyncRequest]) (*connect.Response[v1.SyncResponse], error) {
        return nil, connect.NewError(connect.CodeUnimplemented, errors.New("kylok.v1.AgentService.Sync is not implemented"))
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package sqlstore

import (
        "context"
        "crypto/rand"
        "database/sql"
        "encoding/base64"
        "encoding/json"
        "errors"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// Record invalidations atomically with effects. A transaction deduplicates device changes,
// retaining one notification for the final aggregate rather than transient lease/checkpoint updates.
func recordChange(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, scope, kind, id string) error {
        if scope == "" {
                return nil
        } // Empty scope is immutable public bootstrap catalog, not a management scope.
        key := scope + "\x00" + kind + "\x00" + id
        if tx.changes == nil {
                tx.changes = map[string]bool{}
        }
        if tx.changes[key] {
                return nil
        }
        if _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, "INSERT INTO change_heads(scope_id,sequence,floor) VALUES(?,0,0) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING", scope); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        var head uint64
        if err := tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT sequence FROM change_heads WHERE scope_id=?", scope).Scan(&head); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        head++
        if _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, "INSERT INTO changes(scope_id,sequence,kind,resource_id) VALUES(?,?,?,?)", scope, head, kind, id); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        floor := uint64(0)
        if head > ports.ChangeRetention {
                floor = head - ports.ChangeRetention
        }
        if _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, "UPDATE change_heads SET sequence=?,floor=? WHERE scope_id=?", head, floor, scope); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if floor > 0 {
                if _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, "DELETE FROM changes WHERE scope_id=? AND sequence<=?", scope, floor); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        tx.changes[key] = true
        return nil
}

// Resolve a device's authoritative scope inside the mutation transaction before recording its final aggregate.
func deviceChanged(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, id string) error {
        var scope string
        if err := tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT scope_id FROM devices WHERE id=?", id).Scan(&scope); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return recordChange(ctx, tx, scope, "device_changed", id)
}

// Read a scope head/floor within a serialized transaction, including an empty scope history.
func changeHead(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, scope string) (uint64, uint64, error) {
        var head, floor uint64
        err := tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT sequence,floor FROM change_heads WHERE scope_id=?", scope).Scan(&head, &floor)
        if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return 0, 0, nil
        }
        return head, floor, err
}

// ListChanges reads committed scoped invalidations in sequence order and explicitly rejects gaps.
// Usage: page, err := store.ListChanges(ctx, scope, lastSequence, 100).
func (s *Store) ListChanges(ctx context.Context, scope string, after uint64, limit int) (ports.ChangesPage, error) {
        if scope == "" || limit < 1 || limit > 100 {
                return ports.ChangesPage{}, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.ChangesPage{}, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        head, floor, err := changeHead(ctx, tx, scope)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.ChangesPage{}, err
        }
        if after < floor {
                return ports.ChangesPage{}, ports.ErrCursorExpired
        }
        if after > head {
                return ports.ChangesPage{}, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        rows, err := tx.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT sequence,kind,resource_id FROM changes WHERE scope_id=? AND sequence>? ORDER BY sequence LIMIT ?", scope, after, limit)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.ChangesPage{}, err
        }
        result := ports.ChangesPage{Items: []ports.Change{}, Next: after, Head: head}
        for rows.Next() {
                var item ports.Change
                if err = rows.Scan(&item.Sequence, &item.Kind, &item.ResourceID); err != nil {
                        rows.Close()
                        return ports.ChangesPage{}, err
                }
                result.Items = append(result.Items, item)
                result.Next = item.Sequence
        }
        if err = errors.Join(rows.Err(), rows.Close()); err != nil {
                return ports.ChangesPage{}, err
        }
        return result, tx.Commit()
}

// Read a device's desired/observed state consistently; lease/checkpoint details are stripped by the public facade.
func readDeviceState(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, scope, id string) (ports.DeviceState, error) {
        var result ports.DeviceState
        device, _, err := scanDevice(tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT "+deviceColumns+" FROM devices WHERE scope_id=? AND id=?", scope, id))
        if err != nil {
                return result, err
        }
        result.Network, err = networkStatus(ctx, tx, device, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return result, err
        }
        result.Device = device
        if result.Desired, err = desiredApplications(ctx, tx, device); err != nil {
                return result, err
        }
        if result.Policy, err = policyStatus(ctx, tx, device, result.Desired, nil); err != nil {
                return result, err
        }
        rows, err := tx.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM operations WHERE device_id=? ORDER BY id", id)
        if err != nil {
                return result, err
        }
        for rows.Next() {
                var data []byte
                var op ports.Operation
                if err = rows.Scan(&data); err == nil {
                        err = json.Unmarshal(data, &op)
                }
                if err != nil {
                        rows.Close()
                        return ports.DeviceState{}, err
                }
                result.Operations = append(result.Operations, op)
        }
        if err = errors.Join(rows.Err(), rows.Close()); err != nil {
                return result, err
        }
        rows, err = tx.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM commands WHERE device_id=? ORDER BY id", id)
        if err != nil {
                return result, err
        }
        for rows.Next() {
                var data []byte
                var command ports.Command
                if err = rows.Scan(&data); err == nil {
                        err = json.Unmarshal(data, &command)
                }
                if err != nil {
                        rows.Close()
                        return result, err
                }
                result.Commands = append(result.Commands, command)
        }
        return result, errors.Join(rows.Err(), rows.Close())
}

// GetDeviceState returns one scoped transactionally consistent aggregate without expiring or changing work.
// Usage: state, err := store.GetDeviceState(ctx, scope, deviceID).
func (s *Store) GetDeviceState(ctx context.Context, scope, id string) (ports.DeviceState, error) {
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.DeviceState{}, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        result, err := readDeviceState(ctx, tx, scope, id)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.DeviceState{}, err
        }
        return result, tx.Commit()
}

// Delete saved scan rows before deleting their scope/expiry envelope; no external provider owns these records.
func removeScan(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, id string) error {
        if _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, "DELETE FROM projection_items WHERE snapshot_id=?", id); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, "DELETE FROM projection_scans WHERE id=?", id)
        return err
}

// Materialize stable projection records and their watermark under the same state serialization lock.
// Scans are capped per scope and expire; an evicted/expired continuation requires a new scan.
func createScan(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, scope string, now time.Time) (string, error) {
        rows, err := tx.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT id,expires_at FROM projection_scans WHERE scope_id=? ORDER BY ordinal", scope)
        if err != nil {
                return "", err
        }
        var retained []string
        var expired []string
        for rows.Next() {
                var id string
                var expires int64
                if err = rows.Scan(&id, &expires); err != nil {
                        rows.Close()
                        return "", err
                }
                if expires <= now.UnixMilli() {
                        expired = append(expired, id)
                } else {
                        retained = append(retained, id)
                }
        }
        if err = errors.Join(rows.Err(), rows.Close()); err != nil {
                return "", err
        }
        for _, id := range expired {
                if err = removeScan(ctx, tx, id); err != nil {
                        return "", err
                }
        }
        for len(retained) >= ports.SnapshotLimit {
                if err = removeScan(ctx, tx, retained[0]); err != nil {
                        return "", err
                }
                retained = retained[1:]
        }
        random := make([]byte, 32)
        if _, err = rand.Read(random); err != nil {
                return "", err
        }
        id := base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(random)
        head, _, err := changeHead(ctx, tx, scope)
        if err != nil {
                return "", err
        }
        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "INSERT INTO projection_scans(id,ordinal,scope_id,watermark,expires_at) VALUES(?,(SELECT COALESCE(MAX(ordinal),0)+1 FROM projection_scans),?,?,?)", id, scope, head, now.Add(ports.SnapshotLifetime).UnixMilli()); err != nil {
                return "", err
        }
        rows, err = tx.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT id FROM devices WHERE scope_id=? ORDER BY id", scope)
        if err != nil {
                return "", err
        }
        var devices []string
        for rows.Next() {
                var device string
                if err = rows.Scan(&device); err != nil {
                        rows.Close()
                        return "", err
                }
                devices = append(devices, device)
        }
        if err = errors.Join(rows.Err(), rows.Close()); err != nil {
                return "", err
        }
        index := 0
        for _, device := range devices {
                state, err := readDeviceState(ctx, tx, scope, device)
                if err != nil {
                        return "", err
                }
                if err = saveProjection(ctx, tx, id, index, ports.ProjectionItem{Device: &state}); err != nil {
                        return "", err
                }
                index++
        }
        rows, err = tx.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM artifacts WHERE scope_id=? ORDER BY digest", scope)
        if err != nil {
                return "", err
        }
        var applications []ports.Artifact
        for rows.Next() {
                var data []byte
                var artifact ports.Artifact
                if err = rows.Scan(&data); err == nil {
                        err = json.Unmarshal(data, &artifact)
                }
                if err != nil {
                        rows.Close()
                        return "", err
                }
                applications = append(applications, artifact)
        }
        if err = errors.Join(rows.Err(), rows.Close()); err != nil {
                return "", err
        }
        for _, artifact := range applications {
                if err = saveProjection(ctx, tx, id, index, ports.ProjectionItem{Application: &artifact}); err != nil {
                        return "", err
                }
                index++
        }
        // Freeze group and fleet resources under the same watermark as devices/catalog metadata.
        for _, query := range []string{
                "SELECT data FROM fleet_groups WHERE scope_id=? ORDER BY id",
                "SELECT data FROM fleet_assignments WHERE scope_id=? ORDER BY id",
        } {
                rows, err := tx.QueryContext(ctx, query, scope)
                if err != nil {
                        return "", err
                }
                var items []ports.ProjectionItem
                for rows.Next() {
                        var data []byte
                        item := ports.ProjectionItem{}
                        if err = rows.Scan(&data); err != nil {
                                rows.Close()
                                return "", err
                        }
                        if query == "SELECT data FROM fleet_groups WHERE scope_id=? ORDER BY id" {
                                item.Group = &ports.Group{}
                                err = json.Unmarshal(data, item.Group)
                        } else {
                                item.Fleet = &ports.FleetAssignment{}
                                err = json.Unmarshal(data, item.Fleet)
                        }
                        if err != nil {
                                rows.Close()
                                return "", err
                        }
                        items = append(items, item)
                }
                if err = errors.Join(rows.Err(), rows.Close()); err != nil {
                        return "", err
                }
                for _, item := range items {
                        if err = saveProjection(ctx, tx, id, index, item); err != nil {
                                return "", err
                        }
                        index++
                }
        }
        return id, nil
}

// Save one detached immutable scan record inside the transaction that selected its watermark.
func saveProjection(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, id string, index int, item ports.ProjectionItem) error {
        data, err := json.Marshal(item)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "INSERT INTO projection_items(snapshot_id,item_index,data) VALUES(?,?,?)", id, index, data)
        return err
}

// ReadProjection returns a persisted consistent snapshot page across reconnect/restart.
// It does not change observed/desired state. Expired or evicted scans return ErrCursorExpired.
// Usage: page, err := store.ReadProjection(ctx, scope, "", 0, 100, now).
func (s *Store) ReadProjection(ctx context.Context, scope, id string, after, limit int, now time.Time) (ports.ProjectionPage, error) {
        if scope == "" || after < 0 || limit < 1 || limit > 100 || id == "" && after != 0 {
                return ports.ProjectionPage{}, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.ProjectionPage{}, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        if id == "" {
                id, err = createScan(ctx, tx, scope, now)
                if err != nil {
                        return ports.ProjectionPage{}, err
                }
        }
        result := ports.ProjectionPage{SnapshotID: id, After: after, Items: []ports.ProjectionItem{}}
        var expires int64
        if err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT watermark,expires_at FROM projection_scans WHERE id=? AND scope_id=?", id, scope).Scan(&result.Watermark, &expires); errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return ports.ProjectionPage{}, ports.ErrCursorExpired
        } else if err != nil {
                return ports.ProjectionPage{}, err
        }
        result.ExpiresAt = time.UnixMilli(expires).UTC()
        if !now.Before(result.ExpiresAt) {
                return ports.ProjectionPage{}, ports.ErrCursorExpired
        }
        rows, err := tx.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT item_index,data FROM projection_items WHERE snapshot_id=? AND item_index>=? ORDER BY item_index LIMIT ?", id, after, limit+1)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.ProjectionPage{}, err
        }
        for rows.Next() {
                var index int
                var data []byte
                var item ports.ProjectionItem
                if err = rows.Scan(&index, &data); err == nil {
                        err = json.Unmarshal(data, &item)
                }
                if err != nil {
                        rows.Close()
                        return ports.ProjectionPage{}, err
                }
                if len(result.Items) == limit {
                        result.More = true
                        break
                }
                result.Items = append(result.Items, item)
                result.After = index + 1
        }
        if err = errors.Join(rows.Err(), rows.Close()); err != nil {
                return ports.ProjectionPage{}, err
        }
        return result, tx.Commit()
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package sqlstore

import (
        "bytes"
        "context"
        "crypto/rand"
        "database/sql"
        "encoding/hex"
        "encoding/json"
        "errors"
        "slices"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// Bound outstanding one-shot actions independently of installation operation limits.
const maximumActiveCommands = 16

// Load the original command checkpoint without exposing another device's existence to public callers.
func readCommand(ctx context.Context, q queryer, id string) (ports.Command, error) {
        var result ports.Command
        var data []byte
        err := q.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM commands WHERE id=?", id).Scan(&data)
        if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return result, ports.ErrNotFound
        }
        if err != nil {
                return result, err
        }
        err = json.Unmarshal(data, &result)
        return result, err
}

// Keep the indexed lifecycle and durable result aligned in the same device-change transaction.
func saveCommand(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, command ports.Command) error {
        data, err := json.Marshal(command)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "UPDATE commands SET state=?,data=? WHERE id=?", command.State, data, command.ID); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return deviceChanged(ctx, tx, command.DeviceID)
}

// Read active commands in host submission order; Android receives at most one one-shot action per sync.
func activeCommands(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, device string) ([]ports.Command, error) {
        rows, err := tx.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM commands WHERE device_id=? AND state IN ('QUEUED','DISPATCHED') ORDER BY ordinal", device)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer rows.Close()
        result := []ports.Command{}
        for rows.Next() {
                var data []byte
                var command ports.Command
                if err = rows.Scan(&data); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                if err = json.Unmarshal(data, &command); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                result = append(result, command)
        }
        return result, rows.Err()
}

// Expiry cannot prove whether a dispatched destructive effect happened; never requeue such work.
func expireCommands(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, device string, now time.Time) error {
        commands, err := activeCommands(ctx, tx, device)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        for _, command := range commands {
                if now.Before(command.Deadline) {
                        continue
                }
                command.ErrorCode = "DEADLINE_EXCEEDED"
                if command.State == "DISPATCHED" {
                        command.State = "UNCERTAIN"
                } else {
                        command.State = "FAILED"
                }
                if err = saveCommand(ctx, tx, command); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        return nil
}

// Resolve scoped ownership before observing command history or applying cancellation.
func commandDevice(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, scope, device string) (ports.Device, error) {
        value, _, err := scanDevice(tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT "+deviceColumns+" FROM devices WHERE scope_id=? AND id=?", scope, device))
        return value, err
}

// SubmitCommand atomically binds a host retry key and capability-gates a bounded one-shot action queue.
// Usage: command, err := store.SubmitCommand(ctx, assignment).
func (s *Store) SubmitCommand(ctx context.Context, a ports.CommandAssignment) (ports.Command, error) {
        empty := ports.Command{}
        if a.ScopeID == "" || a.DeviceID == "" || a.ID == "" || a.IdempotencyKey == "" || a.Kind != "REBOOT" && a.Kind != "LOCK" {
                return empty, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        device, err := commandDevice(ctx, tx, a.ScopeID, a.DeviceID)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if device.State == "REVOKED" {
                return empty, ports.ErrRevoked
        }
        var id string
        err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT id FROM commands WHERE device_id=? AND idempotency_key=?", a.DeviceID, a.IdempotencyKey).Scan(&id)
        if err == nil {
                command, err := readCommand(ctx, tx, id)
                if err != nil {
                        return empty, err
                }
                if command.Kind != a.Kind {
                        return empty, ports.ErrConflict
                }
                return command, nil
        }
        if !errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return empty, err
        }
        capability := "command.lock"
        if a.Kind == "REBOOT" {
                capability = "command.reboot"
        }
        if !device.Inventory.DeviceOwner || !slices.Contains(device.Inventory.Capabilities, capability) {
                return empty, ports.ErrUnsupported
        }
        if !a.Now.Before(a.Deadline) || a.Deadline.Sub(a.Now) > 7*24*time.Hour {
                return empty, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        if err = expireCommands(ctx, tx, a.DeviceID, a.Now); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        commands, err := activeCommands(ctx, tx, a.DeviceID)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if len(commands) >= maximumActiveCommands {
                return empty, ports.ErrConflict
        }
        command := ports.Command{ID: a.ID, DeviceID: a.DeviceID, Kind: a.Kind, State: "QUEUED", Deadline: a.Deadline}
        data, err := json.Marshal(command)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "INSERT INTO commands(id,ordinal,device_id,idempotency_key,state,data) VALUES(?,(SELECT COALESCE(MAX(ordinal),0)+1 FROM commands),?,?,?,?)", a.ID, a.DeviceID, a.IdempotencyKey, command.State, data); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if err = deviceChanged(ctx, tx, a.DeviceID); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        return command, tx.Commit()
}

// GetCommand reads a scoped result after classifying deadline expiry without replaying an action.
// Usage: command, err := store.GetCommand(ctx, scope, deviceID, commandID, now).
func (s *Store) GetCommand(ctx context.Context, scope, device, id string, now time.Time) (ports.Command, error) {
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Command{}, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        if _, err = commandDevice(ctx, tx, scope, device); err != nil {
                return ports.Command{}, err
        }
        if err = expireCommands(ctx, tx, device, now); err != nil {
                return ports.Command{}, err
        }
        result, err := readCommand(ctx, tx, id)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Command{}, err
        }
        if result.DeviceID != device {
                return ports.Command{}, ports.ErrNotFound
        }
        return result, tx.Commit()
}

// ListCommands returns one authorized device's history; reads classify but never replay expired actions.
// Usage: commands, err := store.ListCommands(ctx, scope, deviceID, afterID, 20, now).
func (s *Store) ListCommands(ctx context.Context, scope, device, after string, limit int, now time.Time) ([]ports.Command, error) {
        if limit < 1 || limit > 101 {
                return nil, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        if _, err = commandDevice(ctx, tx, scope, device); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if err = expireCommands(ctx, tx, device, now); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        rows, err := tx.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM commands WHERE device_id=? AND id>? ORDER BY id LIMIT ?", device, after, limit)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        result := []ports.Command{}
        for rows.Next() {
                var data []byte
                var command ports.Command
                if err = rows.Scan(&data); err == nil {
                        err = json.Unmarshal(data, &command)
                }
                if err != nil {
                        rows.Close()
                        return nil, err
                }
                result = append(result, command)
        }
        if err = errors.Join(rows.Err(), rows.Close()); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return result, tx.Commit()
}

// CancelCommand stops undispatched work; already committed delivery remains uncertain until platform observation.
// Usage: command, err := store.CancelCommand(ctx, scope, deviceID, commandID, now).
func (s *Store) CancelCommand(ctx context.Context, scope, device, id string, now time.Time) (ports.Command, error) {
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Command{}, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        if _, err = commandDevice(ctx, tx, scope, device); err != nil {
                return ports.Command{}, err
        }
        if err = expireCommands(ctx, tx, device, now); err != nil {
                return ports.Command{}, err
        }
        result, err := readCommand(ctx, tx, id)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Command{}, err
        }
        if result.DeviceID != device {
                return ports.Command{}, ports.ErrNotFound
        }
        switch result.State {
        case "QUEUED":
                result.State, result.ErrorCode = "CANCELLED", "CANCELLED_BY_HOST"
        case "DISPATCHED":
                result.State, result.ErrorCode = "UNCERTAIN", "CANCELLED_AFTER_DISPATCH"
        default:
                return result, nil
        }
        if err = saveCommand(ctx, tx, result); err != nil {
                return ports.Command{}, err
        }
        return result, tx.Commit()
}

// Validate an observed result and bind its exact bytes once; late readback can resolve an uncertain dispatched action.
func applyCommandReport(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, device string, report ports.CommandReport) error {
        command, err := readCommand(ctx, tx, report.CommandID)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if command.DeviceID != device {
                return ports.ErrNotFound
        }
        if command.DispatchToken == "" || command.DispatchToken != report.DispatchToken {
                return ports.ErrConflict
        }
        data, err := json.Marshal(report)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        var previous []byte
        if err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT report FROM commands WHERE id=?", command.ID).Scan(&previous); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if len(previous) > 0 {
                if !bytes.Equal(previous, data) {
                        return ports.ErrConflict
                }
                return nil
        }
        if report.State != "SUCCEEDED" && report.State != "FAILED" && report.State != "UNCERTAIN" {
                return ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        if report.State == "SUCCEEDED" {
                observed := report.Observation
                if observed == nil || command.Kind == "REBOOT" && (observed.BootBefore < 0 || observed.BootAfter <= observed.BootBefore) || command.Kind == "LOCK" && (!observed.Secure || !observed.Locked) {
                        return ports.ErrInvalidArgument
                }
        }
        command.State, command.ErrorCode, command.Observation = report.State, report.ErrorCode, report.Observation
        if err = saveCommand(ctx, tx, command); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "UPDATE commands SET report=? WHERE id=?", data, command.ID)
        return err
}

// Dispatch one stable action token and redeliver that same token until reported or expired; never create a new attempt.
func dispatchCommand(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, device string, inventory ports.Inventory) ([]ports.Command, error) {
        commands, err := activeCommands(ctx, tx, device)
        if err != nil || len(commands) == 0 {
                return nil, err
        }
        command := commands[0]
        if command.State == "QUEUED" {
                capability := "command.lock"
                if command.Kind == "REBOOT" {
                        capability = "command.reboot"
                }
                if !inventory.DeviceOwner || !slices.Contains(inventory.Capabilities, capability) {
                        command.State, command.ErrorCode = "FAILED", "CAPABILITY_UNAVAILABLE"
                        return nil, saveCommand(ctx, tx, command)
                }
                nonce := make([]byte, 24)
                if _, err = rand.Read(nonce); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                command.State, command.DispatchToken = "DISPATCHED", hex.EncodeToString(nonce)
                if err = saveCommand(ctx, tx, command); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
        }
        return []ports.Command{command}, nil
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package sqlstore

import (
        "context"
        "crypto/sha256"
        "database/sql"
        "encoding/binary"
        "regexp"
        "strconv"
        "strings"
)

var schemaPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^[a-z_][a-z0-9_]{0,62}$`)
var tableNames = map[string]bool{"network_policies": true, "network_requests": true, "commands": true, "fleet_groups": true, "fleet_assignments": true, "change_heads": true, "changes": true, "projection_scans": true, "projection_items": true, "schema_version": true, "enrollments": true, "devices": true, "artifacts": true, "operations": true, "desired_apps": true, "reports": true, "policies": true, "policy_requests": true}

type database struct {
        pool            *sql.DB
        dialect, schema string
}
type transaction struct {
        *sql.Tx
        database *database
        changes  map[string]bool
}

// Translate only static SQL identifiers/placeholders outside quoted literals. Values always remain bound parameters.
func (d *database) bind(query string) string {
        if d.dialect != "postgres" {
                return query
        }
        var out strings.Builder
        parameter := 0
        for index := 0; index < len(query); {
                c := query[index]
                if c == '\'' || c == '"' {
                        quote := c
                        out.WriteByte(c)
                        index++
                        for index < len(query) {
                                ch := query[index]
                                out.WriteByte(ch)
                                index++
                                if ch == quote {
                                        if index < len(query) && query[index] == quote {
                                                out.WriteByte(query[index])
                                                index++
                                        } else {
                                                break
                                        }
                                }
                        }
                } else if c == '?' {
                        parameter++
                        out.WriteByte('$')
                        out.WriteString(strconv.Itoa(parameter))
                        index++
                } else if c >= 'a' && c <= 'z' || c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z' || c == '_' {
                        start := index
                        index++
                        for index < len(query) {
                                ch := query[index]
                                if !(ch >= 'a' && ch <= 'z' || ch >= 'A' && ch <= 'Z' || ch >= '0' && ch <= '9' || ch == '_') {
                                        break
                                }
                                index++
                        }
                        word := query[start:index]
                        if tableNames[word] {
                                out.WriteByte('"')
                                out.WriteString(d.schema)
                                out.WriteString(`".`)
                        }
                        out.WriteString(word)
                } else {
                        out.WriteByte(c)
                        index++
                }
        }
        return out.String()
}

// BeginTx serializes PostgreSQL control-plane effects per schema before the first state read.
// A transaction-level lock is released on rollback, commit, connection loss or server restart.
// Read committed ensures reads after lock acquisition see the previous holder's committed state.
// Usage: tx, err := database.BeginTx(ctx, nil); defer tx.Rollback().
func (d *database) BeginTx(ctx context.Context, options *sql.TxOptions) (*transaction, error) {
        if d.dialect == "postgres" {
                options = &sql.TxOptions{Isolation: sql.LevelReadCommitted}
        }
        tx, err := d.pool.BeginTx(ctx, options)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if d.dialect == "postgres" {
                sum := sha256.Sum256([]byte("kylok.state/" + d.schema))
                key := int64(binary.BigEndian.Uint64(sum[:8]))
                if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock($1)", key); err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return nil, err
                }
        }
        return &transaction{Tx: tx, database: d}, nil
}

// ExecContext wraps standalone writes in the same transaction/lock discipline as multi-statement effects.
// Usage: result, err := database.ExecContext(ctx, query, args...).
func (d *database) ExecContext(ctx context.Context, query string, args ...any) (sql.Result, error) {
        tx, err := d.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        result, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, query, args...)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return result, tx.Commit()
}

// QueryRowContext performs a single committed observation without opening an ambient host transaction.
// Usage: err := database.QueryRowContext(ctx, query, args...).Scan(&value).
func (d *database) QueryRowContext(ctx context.Context, query string, args ...any) *sql.Row {
        return d.pool.QueryRowContext(ctx, d.bind(query), args...)
}

// QueryContext reads one statement's committed rows; the caller closes them.
// Usage: rows, err := database.QueryContext(ctx, query, args...).
func (d *database) QueryContext(ctx context.Context, query string, args ...any) (*sql.Rows, error) {
        return d.pool.QueryContext(ctx, d.bind(query), args...)
}

// PingContext checks the supplied pool without changing its settings or ownership.
// Usage: err := database.PingContext(ctx).
func (d *database) PingContext(ctx context.Context) error { return d.pool.PingContext(ctx) }

// ExecContext binds one statement to this SDK-owned state transaction.
// Usage: result, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, query, args...).
func (t *transaction) ExecContext(ctx context.Context, query string, args ...any) (sql.Result, error) {
        return t.Tx.ExecContext(ctx, t.database.bind(query), args...)
}

// QueryRowContext reads within the transaction's serialization boundary.
// Usage: err := tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, query, args...).Scan(&value).
func (t *transaction) QueryRowContext(ctx context.Context, query string, args ...any) *sql.Row {
        return t.Tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, t.database.bind(query), args...)
}

// QueryContext reads rows within this transaction; close them before committing.
// Usage: rows, err := tx.QueryContext(ctx, query, args...).
func (t *transaction) QueryContext(ctx context.Context, query string, args ...any) (*sql.Rows, error) {
        return t.Tx.QueryContext(ctx, t.database.bind(query), args...)
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package sqlstore

import (
        "bytes"
        "context"
        "crypto/sha256"
        "database/sql"
        "encoding/base64"
        "encoding/json"
        "errors"
        "slices"
        "sort"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// Canonicalize a bounded target set without retaining or modifying caller-owned slices.
func fleetTargets(ids []string) ([]string, error) {
        if len(ids) > ports.MaximumFleetDevices {
                return nil, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        ids = slices.Clone(ids)
        sort.Strings(ids)
        for i, id := range ids {
                if id == "" || i > 0 && ids[i-1] == id {
                        return nil, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
                }
        }
        if ids == nil {
                ids = []string{}
        }
        return ids, nil
}

// Read one opaque group without treating an absent scope as global access.
func readGroup(ctx context.Context, q queryer, scope, id string) (ports.Group, error) {
        var result ports.Group
        var data []byte
        err := q.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM fleet_groups WHERE scope_id=? AND id=?", scope, id).Scan(&data)
        if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return result, ports.ErrNotFound
        }
        if err != nil {
                return result, err
        }
        err = json.Unmarshal(data, &result)
        return result, err
}

// ReplaceGroup validates scope ownership and atomically compares membership revision; exact retries are harmless.
// Usage: group, err := store.ReplaceGroup(ctx, desiredGroup, expectedRevision).
func (s *Store) ReplaceGroup(ctx context.Context, group ports.Group, expected uint64) (ports.Group, error) {
        if group.ScopeID == "" || group.ID == "" || expected >= 1<<62 {
                return ports.Group{}, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        ids, err := fleetTargets(group.DeviceIDs)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Group{}, err
        }
        group.DeviceIDs = ids
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Group{}, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        previous, err := readGroup(ctx, tx, group.ScopeID, group.ID)
        if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                return ports.Group{}, err
        }
        if previous.Revision == expected+1 && slices.Equal(previous.DeviceIDs, ids) {
                return previous, nil
        }
        if previous.Revision != expected {
                return ports.Group{}, ports.ErrConflict
        }
        for _, id := range ids {
                var state string
                err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT state FROM devices WHERE scope_id=? AND id=?", group.ScopeID, id).Scan(&state)
                if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                        return ports.Group{}, ports.ErrNotFound
                }
                if err != nil {
                        return ports.Group{}, err
                }
                if state == "REVOKED" {
                        return ports.Group{}, ports.ErrRevoked
                }
        }
        group.Revision = expected + 1
        data, err := json.Marshal(group)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Group{}, err
        }
        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "INSERT INTO fleet_groups(scope_id,id,data) VALUES(?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(scope_id,id) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data", group.ScopeID, group.ID, data); err != nil {
                return ports.Group{}, err
        }
        if err = recordChange(ctx, tx, group.ScopeID, "group_changed", group.ID); err != nil {
                return ports.Group{}, err
        }
        return group, tx.Commit()
}

// GetGroup returns detached scope-local membership, including a deliberately empty group.
// Usage: group, err := store.GetGroup(ctx, scope, groupID).
func (s *Store) GetGroup(ctx context.Context, scope, id string) (ports.Group, error) {
        return readGroup(ctx, s.db, scope, id)
}

// ListGroups reads a bounded live group page ordered by opaque group ID.
// Usage: groups, err := store.ListGroups(ctx, scope, afterID, 20).
func (s *Store) ListGroups(ctx context.Context, scope, after string, limit int) ([]ports.Group, error) {
        if scope == "" || limit < 1 || limit > 101 {
                return nil, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        rows, err := s.db.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM fleet_groups WHERE scope_id=? AND id>? ORDER BY id LIMIT ?", scope, after, limit)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer rows.Close()
        result := []ports.Group{}
        for rows.Next() {
                var data []byte
                var group ports.Group
                if err = rows.Scan(&data); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                if err = json.Unmarshal(data, &group); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                result = append(result, group)
        }
        return result, rows.Err()
}

// Load a fan-out checkpoint in the caller's serialization boundary.
func readFleet(ctx context.Context, q queryer, scope, id string) (ports.FleetAssignment, error) {
        var result ports.FleetAssignment
        var data []byte
        err := q.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM fleet_assignments WHERE scope_id=? AND id=?", scope, id).Scan(&data)
        if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return result, ports.ErrNotFound
        }
        if err != nil {
                return result, err
        }
        err = json.Unmarshal(data, &result)
        return result, err
}

// Commit the batch checkpoint and its scoped invalidation with all per-device writes in this transaction.
func saveFleet(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, batch ports.FleetAssignment) error {
        data, err := json.Marshal(batch)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "INSERT INTO fleet_assignments(scope_id,id,artifact_sha256,pending,data) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(scope_id,id) DO UPDATE SET pending=excluded.pending,data=excluded.data", batch.Request.ScopeID, batch.Request.ID, batch.Request.ArtifactSHA256, fleetPending(batch), data); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return recordChange(ctx, tx, batch.Request.ScopeID, "fleet_changed", batch.Request.ID)
}

// Keep the artifact referenced until every frozen target was submitted or explicitly stopped.
func fleetPending(batch ports.FleetAssignment) int {
        if batch.Complete || batch.Cancelled {
                return 0
        }
        return 1
}

// CreateFleetAssignment freezes targets once, binding the original selector/window before any fan-out.
// Usage: batch, err := store.CreateFleetAssignment(ctx, request, now).
func (s *Store) CreateFleetAssignment(ctx context.Context, request ports.FleetRequest, now time.Time) (ports.FleetAssignment, error) {
        empty := ports.FleetAssignment{}
        ids, err := fleetTargets(request.DeviceIDs)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        request.DeviceIDs = ids
        if request.ScopeID == "" || request.ID == "" || request.ArtifactSHA256 == "" || (request.GroupID == "") == (len(ids) == 0) || request.NotBefore.UnixMilli() <= 0 || !request.NotBefore.Before(request.Deadline) || request.Deadline.Sub(request.NotBefore) > 7*24*time.Hour {
                return empty, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        previous, err := readFleet(ctx, tx, request.ScopeID, request.ID)
        if err == nil {
                before, _ := json.Marshal(previous.Request)
                after, _ := json.Marshal(request)
                if !bytes.Equal(before, after) {
                        return empty, ports.ErrConflict
                }
                return previous, nil
        }
        if !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                return empty, err
        }
        if !now.Before(request.Deadline) {
                return empty, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        if _, err = artifact(ctx, tx, request.ScopeID, request.ArtifactSHA256); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        batch := ports.FleetAssignment{Request: request, Items: []ports.FleetItem{}}
        if request.GroupID != "" {
                group, err := readGroup(ctx, tx, request.ScopeID, request.GroupID)
                if err != nil {
                        return empty, err
                }
                ids, batch.GroupRevision = group.DeviceIDs, group.Revision
        }
        for _, id := range ids {
                batch.Items = append(batch.Items, ports.FleetItem{DeviceID: id, State: "PENDING"})
        }
        batch.Complete = len(batch.Items) == 0
        if err = saveFleet(ctx, tx, batch); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        return batch, tx.Commit()
}

// GetFleetAssignment returns detached frozen targets and submission progress in one authorized scope.
// Usage: batch, err := store.GetFleetAssignment(ctx, scope, batchID).
func (s *Store) GetFleetAssignment(ctx context.Context, scope, id string) (ports.FleetAssignment, error) {
        return readFleet(ctx, s.db, scope, id)
}

// ListFleetAssignments lets a restarted host find incomplete batches through a bounded live page.
// Usage: batches, err := store.ListFleetAssignments(ctx, scope, afterID, 20).
func (s *Store) ListFleetAssignments(ctx context.Context, scope, after string, limit int) ([]ports.FleetAssignment, error) {
        if scope == "" || limit < 1 || limit > 101 {
                return nil, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        rows, err := s.db.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM fleet_assignments WHERE scope_id=? AND id>? ORDER BY id LIMIT ?", scope, after, limit)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer rows.Close()
        result := []ports.FleetAssignment{}
        for rows.Next() {
                var data []byte
                var batch ports.FleetAssignment
                if err = rows.Scan(&data); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                if err = json.Unmarshal(data, &batch); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                result = append(result, batch)
        }
        return result, rows.Err()
}

// Derive a collision-resistant device request key from its frozen batch identity; no user key namespace is reserved.
func fleetKey(scope, batch, device string) string {
        data, _ := json.Marshal([]string{"kylok.fleet", scope, batch, device})
        sum := sha256.Sum256(data)
        return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(sum[:])
}

// AdvanceFleetAssignment commits a bounded submission chunk and its progress atomically; infrastructure errors roll it back.
// Usage: batch, err := store.AdvanceFleetAssignment(ctx, scope, batchID, now, 20).
func (s *Store) AdvanceFleetAssignment(ctx context.Context, scope, id string, now time.Time, limit int) (ports.FleetAssignment, error) {
        empty := ports.FleetAssignment{}
        if limit < 1 || limit > ports.MaximumFleetDevices {
                return empty, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        batch, err := readFleet(ctx, tx, scope, id)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if batch.Complete || batch.Cancelled {
                return batch, nil
        }
        count := 0
        for i := range batch.Items {
                item := &batch.Items[i]
                if item.State != "PENDING" || count >= limit {
                        continue
                }
                count++
                if !now.Before(batch.Request.Deadline) {
                        item.State, item.ErrorCode = "FAILED", "DEADLINE_EXCEEDED"
                        continue
                }
                // The same device gets the same key across restarts, concurrent workers and lost responses.
                key := fleetKey(scope, id, item.DeviceID)
                op, err := assign(ctx, tx, ports.Assignment{ScopeID: scope, DeviceID: item.DeviceID, ArtifactSHA256: batch.Request.ArtifactSHA256, IdempotencyKey: "fleet-" + key, OperationID: key, Now: now, NotBefore: batch.Request.NotBefore, Deadline: batch.Request.Deadline})
                switch {
                case err == nil:
                        item.State, item.OperationID = "ASSIGNED", op.ID
                case errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound):
                        item.State, item.ErrorCode = "FAILED", "NOT_FOUND"
                case errors.Is(err, ports.ErrRevoked):
                        item.State, item.ErrorCode = "FAILED", "DEVICE_REVOKED"
                case errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConflict):
                        item.State, item.ErrorCode = "FAILED", "CONFLICT"
                case errors.Is(err, ports.ErrInvalidArgument):
                        item.State, item.ErrorCode = "FAILED", "INVALID_ARGUMENT"
                default:
                        return empty, err
                }
        }
        batch.Complete = true
        for _, item := range batch.Items {
                if item.State == "PENDING" {
                        batch.Complete = false
                }
        }
        if err = saveFleet(ctx, tx, batch); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        return batch, tx.Commit()
}

// Stop active dispatch while retaining immutable desired intent and any already observed completion.
func cancelOperation(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, scope, device, id string, now time.Time) (ports.Operation, error) {
        var found string
        if err := tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT id FROM devices WHERE scope_id=? AND id=?", scope, device).Scan(&found); errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return ports.Operation{}, ports.ErrNotFound
        } else if err != nil {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        if err := expire(ctx, tx, device, now); err != nil {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        op, err := operation(ctx, tx, id)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        if op.DeviceID != device {
                return ports.Operation{}, ports.ErrNotFound
        }
        if op.State == "QUEUED" || op.State == "RUNNING" || op.State == "WAITING_USER" {
                op.State, op.ErrorCode = "CANCELLED", "CANCELLED_BY_HOST"
                op.ErrorMessage = "Future dispatch stopped; a delivered installation may still complete"
                if err = saveOperation(ctx, tx, op); err != nil {
                        return ports.Operation{}, err
                }
        }
        return op, nil
}

// CancelOperation stops dispatch and future downloads; it cannot revoke a platform effect already delivered.
// Usage: operation, err := store.CancelOperation(ctx, scope, deviceID, operationID, now).
func (s *Store) CancelOperation(ctx context.Context, scope, device, id string, now time.Time) (ports.Operation, error) {
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        op, err := cancelOperation(ctx, tx, scope, device, id, now)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        return op, tx.Commit()
}

// CancelFleetAssignment serializes with fan-out, stopping unsubmitted targets and active submitted operations only.
// Usage: batch, err := store.CancelFleetAssignment(ctx, scope, batchID, now).
func (s *Store) CancelFleetAssignment(ctx context.Context, scope, id string, now time.Time) (ports.FleetAssignment, error) {
        empty := ports.FleetAssignment{}
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        batch, err := readFleet(ctx, tx, scope, id)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if batch.Cancelled {
                return batch, nil
        }
        for i := range batch.Items {
                item := &batch.Items[i]
                if item.State == "PENDING" {
                        item.State = "CANCELLED"
                }
                if item.State == "ASSIGNED" {
                        if _, err = cancelOperation(ctx, tx, scope, item.DeviceID, item.OperationID, now); err != nil {
                                return empty, err
                        }
                }
        }
        batch.Cancelled, batch.Complete = true, true
        if err = saveFleet(ctx, tx, batch); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        return batch, tx.Commit()
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package sqlstore

import (
        "context"
        "database/sql"
        "encoding/json"
        "errors"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// Check verifies database connectivity without creating a transaction or changing state.
// Usage: err := store.Check(ctx).
func (s *Store) Check(ctx context.Context) error { return s.db.PingContext(ctx) }

// GetDevice hides absent/foreign identities behind the same scoped not-found contract.
// Usage: device, err := store.GetDevice(ctx, scope, deviceID).
func (s *Store) GetDevice(ctx context.Context, scope, id string) (ports.Device, error) {
        value, _, err := scanDevice(s.db.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT "+deviceColumns+" FROM devices WHERE scope_id=? AND id=?", scope, id))
        return value, err
}

// QueryDevices evaluates exact filters and keyset bounds in one database statement.
// Usage: values, err := store.QueryDevices(ctx, ports.DeviceQuery{ScopeID: scope, Limit: 20}).
func (s *Store) QueryDevices(ctx context.Context, input ports.DeviceQuery) ([]ports.Device, error) {
        if input.ScopeID == "" || input.Limit < 1 || input.Limit > 101 {
                return nil, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        rows, err := s.db.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT "+deviceColumns+" FROM devices WHERE scope_id=? AND id>? AND (?='' OR state=?) AND (?='' OR correlation_id=?) ORDER BY id LIMIT ?", input.ScopeID, input.After, input.State, input.State, input.CorrelationID, input.CorrelationID, input.Limit)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer rows.Close()
        result := []ports.Device{}
        for rows.Next() {
                value, _, err := scanDevice(rows)
                if err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                result = append(result, value)
        }
        return result, rows.Err()
}

// GetOperation verifies ownership and evaluates deadline expiry before reading a detached result.
// Usage: operation, err := store.GetOperation(ctx, scope, deviceID, operationID, now).
func (s *Store) GetOperation(ctx context.Context, scope, device, id string, now time.Time) (ports.Operation, error) {
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        var found string
        if err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT id FROM devices WHERE scope_id=? AND id=?", scope, device).Scan(&found); errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return ports.Operation{}, ports.ErrNotFound
        } else if err != nil {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        if err = expire(ctx, tx, device, now); err != nil {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        value, err := operation(ctx, tx, id)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        if value.DeviceID != device {
                return ports.Operation{}, ports.ErrNotFound
        }
        return value, tx.Commit()
}

// QueryOperations serializes expiry with scoped, filtered keyset reads.
// Usage: values, err := store.QueryOperations(ctx, ports.OperationQuery{ScopeID: scope, DeviceID: deviceID, Limit: 20, Now: now}).
func (s *Store) QueryOperations(ctx context.Context, input ports.OperationQuery) ([]ports.Operation, error) {
        if input.ScopeID == "" || input.DeviceID == "" || input.Limit < 1 || input.Limit > 101 {
                return nil, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        var found string
        if err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT id FROM devices WHERE scope_id=? AND id=?", input.ScopeID, input.DeviceID).Scan(&found); errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return nil, ports.ErrNotFound
        } else if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if err = expire(ctx, tx, input.DeviceID, input.Now); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        rows, err := tx.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM operations WHERE device_id=? AND id>? AND (?='' OR state=?) ORDER BY id LIMIT ?", input.DeviceID, input.After, input.State, input.State, input.Limit)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        result := []ports.Operation{}
        for rows.Next() {
                var data []byte
                if err = rows.Scan(&data); err != nil {
                        rows.Close()
                        return nil, err
                }
                var value ports.Operation
                if err = json.Unmarshal(data, &value); err != nil {
                        rows.Close()
                        return nil, err
                }
                result = append(result, value)
        }
        if err = errors.Join(rows.Err(), rows.Close()); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return result, tx.Commit()
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package sqlstore

import (
        "bytes"
        "context"
        "database/sql"
        "encoding/json"
        "errors"
        "slices"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// Read and optionally persist bounded network observations against the exact current desired revision.
func networkStatus(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, device ports.Device, report *ports.NetworkReport) (ports.NetworkStatus, error) {
        status := ports.NetworkStatus{State: "UNMANAGED"}
        var data, reported []byte
        err := tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT data,reported FROM network_policies WHERE device_id=?", device.ID).Scan(&data, &reported)
        if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return status, nil
        }
        if err != nil {
                return status, err
        }
        status.Desired = &ports.DesiredNetworkPolicy{}
        if err = json.Unmarshal(data, status.Desired); err != nil {
                return status, err
        }
        if report != nil {
                if report.Revision > status.Desired.Revision || report.Revision == 0 || len(report.Reason) > 128 || len(report.ActiveProfileID) > 128 || !slices.Contains([]string{"APPLIED", "PENDING", "FAILED", "UNSUPPORTED"}, report.State) {
                        return status, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
                }
                if report.Revision == status.Desired.Revision {
                        if report.State == "APPLIED" && (!report.ConnectivityVerified || !report.CleanupComplete || report.ActiveProfileID != status.Desired.Policy.PrimaryID) {
                                return status, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
                        }
                        reported, err = json.Marshal(report)
                        if err != nil {
                                return status, err
                        }
                        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "UPDATE network_policies SET reported=? WHERE device_id=?", reported, device.ID); err != nil {
                                return status, err
                        }
                }
        }
        if len(reported) > 0 {
                status.Reported = &ports.NetworkReport{}
                if err = json.Unmarshal(reported, status.Reported); err != nil {
                        return status, err
                }
        }
        status.State, status.Reason = "PENDING", "NOT_OBSERVED"
        if !device.Inventory.DeviceOwner || !slices.Contains(device.Inventory.Capabilities, "network.lifecycle") {
                status.State, status.Reason = "UNSUPPORTED", "CAPABILITY_UNAVAILABLE"
                return status, nil
        }
        if status.Reported != nil && status.Reported.Revision == status.Desired.Revision {
                status.State, status.Reason = status.Reported.State, status.Reported.Reason
        }
        return status, nil
}

// SetNetworkPolicy binds a normalized reference-only document and advances desired revision in the same transaction.
// Usage: desired, err := store.SetNetworkPolicy(ctx, assignment).
func (s *Store) SetNetworkPolicy(ctx context.Context, a ports.NetworkAssignment) (ports.DesiredNetworkPolicy, error) {
        empty := ports.DesiredNetworkPolicy{}
        if a.ScopeID == "" || a.DeviceID == "" || a.IdempotencyKey == "" {
                return empty, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        policy, err := ports.NormalizeNetworkPolicy(a.Policy)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        body, err := json.Marshal(policy)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        device, err := commandDevice(ctx, tx, a.ScopeID, a.DeviceID)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if device.State == "REVOKED" {
                return empty, ports.ErrRevoked
        }
        var previous, response []byte
        err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT body,response FROM network_requests WHERE device_id=? AND idempotency_key=?", a.DeviceID, a.IdempotencyKey).Scan(&previous, &response)
        if err == nil {
                if !bytes.Equal(previous, body) {
                        return empty, ports.ErrConflict
                }
                var desired ports.DesiredNetworkPolicy
                err = json.Unmarshal(response, &desired)
                return desired, err
        }
        if !errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return empty, err
        }
        desired := ports.DesiredNetworkPolicy{Revision: device.DesiredRevision + 1, Policy: policy}
        response, err = json.Marshal(desired)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "INSERT INTO network_policies(device_id,data) VALUES(?,?) ON CONFLICT(device_id) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data", a.DeviceID, response); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "INSERT INTO network_requests(device_id,idempotency_key,body,response) VALUES(?,?,?,?)", a.DeviceID, a.IdempotencyKey, body, response); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "UPDATE devices SET desired_revision=? WHERE id=?", desired.Revision, a.DeviceID); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if err = deviceChanged(ctx, tx, a.DeviceID); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        return desired, tx.Commit()
}

// GetNetworkPolicy returns scoped consistent desired/report/compliance without resolving secrets or triggering effects.
// Usage: status, err := store.GetNetworkPolicy(ctx, scope, deviceID).
func (s *Store) GetNetworkPolicy(ctx context.Context, scope, device string) (ports.NetworkStatus, error) {
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.NetworkStatus{}, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        value, err := commandDevice(ctx, tx, scope, device)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.NetworkStatus{}, err
        }
        status, err := networkStatus(ctx, tx, value, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return status, err
        }
        return status, tx.Commit()
}

// GetNetworkRequest reads a detached retry receipt only after checking device/scope ownership.
// Usage: desired, err := store.GetNetworkRequest(ctx, scope, deviceID, requestKey).
func (s *Store) GetNetworkRequest(ctx context.Context, scope, device, key string) (ports.DesiredNetworkPolicy, error) {
        var result ports.DesiredNetworkPolicy
        var response []byte
        err := s.db.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT response FROM network_requests n JOIN devices d ON d.id=n.device_id WHERE d.scope_id=? AND d.id=? AND n.idempotency_key=?", scope, device, key).Scan(&response)
        if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return result, ports.ErrNotFound
        }
        if err != nil {
                return result, err
        }
        err = json.Unmarshal(response, &result)
        return result, err
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package sqlstore

import (
        "bytes"
        "context"
        "crypto/rand"
        "crypto/sha256"
        "crypto/subtle"
        "database/sql"
        "encoding/base64"
        "encoding/hex"
        "encoding/json"
        "errors"
        "sort"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

type queryer interface {
        QueryRowContext(context.Context, string, ...any) *sql.Row
}

// Read one immutable artifact record through the caller's current connection or transaction.
func artifact(ctx context.Context, q queryer, scope, digest string) (ports.Artifact, error) {
        var data []byte
        err := q.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM artifacts WHERE scope_id=? AND digest=?", scope, digest).Scan(&data)
        if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return ports.Artifact{}, ports.ErrNotFound
        }
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Artifact{}, err
        }
        var a ports.Artifact
        err = json.Unmarshal(data, &a)
        return a, err
}

// RegisterArtifact inserts scoped immutable metadata or accepts an identical repeat.
// Usage: err := store.RegisterArtifact(ctx, artifact).
func (s *Store) RegisterArtifact(ctx context.Context, a ports.Artifact) error {
        data, err := json.Marshal(a)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        var existing []byte
        err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM artifacts WHERE scope_id=? AND digest=?", a.ScopeID, a.SHA256).Scan(&existing)
        if err == nil {
                if !bytes.Equal(existing, data) {
                        return ports.ErrConflict
                }
                return nil
        }
        if !errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return err
        }
        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "INSERT INTO artifacts(scope_id,digest,data) VALUES(?,?,?)", a.ScopeID, a.SHA256, data); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if err = recordChange(ctx, tx, a.ScopeID, "application_changed", a.SHA256); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return tx.Commit()
}

// Artifact loads metadata only from the requested scope and digest.
// Usage: artifact, err := store.Artifact(ctx, scope, digest).
func (s *Store) Artifact(ctx context.Context, scope, digest string) (ports.Artifact, error) {
        return artifact(ctx, s.db, scope, digest)
}

// ListArtifacts returns a stable scoped catalog page without exposing bootstrap entries.
// Usage: artifacts, err := store.ListArtifacts(ctx, "site", "", 100).
func (s *Store) ListArtifacts(ctx context.Context, scope, after string, limit int) ([]ports.Artifact, error) {
        if scope == "" {
                return nil, ports.ErrNotFound
        }
        rows, err := s.db.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM artifacts WHERE scope_id=? AND digest>? ORDER BY digest LIMIT ?", scope, after, limit)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer rows.Close()
        result := []ports.Artifact{}
        for rows.Next() {
                var data []byte
                var a ports.Artifact
                if err = rows.Scan(&data); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                if err = json.Unmarshal(data, &a); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                result = append(result, a)
        }
        return result, rows.Err()
}

// RemoveArtifact rejects desired/active references before removing scoped catalog metadata.
// Usage: err := store.RemoveArtifact(ctx, scope, digest).
func (s *Store) RemoveArtifact(ctx context.Context, scope, digest string) error {
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        if _, err = artifact(ctx, tx, scope, digest); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        var pending int
        if err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT count(*) FROM fleet_assignments WHERE scope_id=? AND artifact_sha256=? AND pending=1", scope, digest).Scan(&pending); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if pending != 0 {
                return ports.ErrReferenced
        }
        var count int
        err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM desired_apps a JOIN devices d ON d.id=a.device_id WHERE d.scope_id=? AND a.artifact_sha256=?`, scope, digest).Scan(&count)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if count != 0 {
                return ports.ErrReferenced
        }
        err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM operations o JOIN devices d ON d.id=o.device_id WHERE d.scope_id=? AND o.artifact_sha256=? AND o.state IN ('QUEUED','RUNNING','WAITING_USER')`, scope, digest).Scan(&count)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if count != 0 {
                return ports.ErrReferenced
        }
        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "DELETE FROM artifacts WHERE scope_id=? AND digest=?", scope, digest); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if err = recordChange(ctx, tx, scope, "application_removed", digest); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return tx.Commit()
}

// Load a durable operation by globally unique ID inside the current transaction.
func operation(ctx context.Context, q queryer, id string) (ports.Operation, error) {
        var data []byte
        err := q.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM operations WHERE id=?", id).Scan(&data)
        if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return ports.Operation{}, ports.ErrNotFound
        }
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        var op ports.Operation
        err = json.Unmarshal(data, &op)
        return op, err
}

// Persist an operation and its indexed state together so queries agree with its JSON record.
func saveOperation(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, op ports.Operation) error {
        data, err := json.Marshal(op)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "UPDATE operations SET state=?,data=? WHERE id=?", op.State, data, op.ID)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return deviceChanged(ctx, tx, op.DeviceID)
}

// Read pending work in deterministic revision order before leasing or expiry decisions.
func activeOperations(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, deviceID string) ([]ports.Operation, error) {
        rows, err := tx.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM operations WHERE device_id=? AND state IN ('QUEUED','RUNNING','WAITING_USER') ORDER BY revision,id", deviceID)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer rows.Close()
        var result []ports.Operation
        for rows.Next() {
                var data []byte
                if err := rows.Scan(&data); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                var op ports.Operation
                if err = json.Unmarshal(data, &op); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                result = append(result, op)
        }
        return result, rows.Err()
}

// Fail operations whose host-selected deadline passed without manufacturing an observed success.
func expire(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, deviceID string, now time.Time) error {
        ops, err := activeOperations(ctx, tx, deviceID)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        for _, op := range ops {
                if !now.Before(op.Deadline) {
                        op.State = "FAILED"
                        op.ErrorCode = "DEADLINE_EXCEEDED"
                        op.ErrorMessage = "Device did not confirm the desired installation before its deadline"
                        if err := saveOperation(ctx, tx, op); err != nil {
                                return err
                        }
                }
        }
        return nil
}

// Assign atomically advances desired app state, binds retries and supersedes older pending work.
// Usage: operation, err := store.Assign(ctx, assignment).
func (s *Store) Assign(ctx context.Context, a ports.Assignment) (ports.Operation, error) {
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        op, err := assign(ctx, tx, a)
        if err != nil {
                return op, err
        }
        return op, tx.Commit()
}

// Apply one assignment within the caller's serialized transaction so fleet progress commits with its device effects.
func assign(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, a ports.Assignment) (ports.Operation, error) {
        d, _, err := scanDevice(tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT "+deviceColumns+" FROM devices WHERE scope_id=? AND id=?", a.ScopeID, a.DeviceID))
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        if d.State == "REVOKED" {
                return ports.Operation{}, ports.ErrRevoked
        }
        var existing string
        err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT id FROM operations WHERE device_id=? AND idempotency_key=?", a.DeviceID, a.IdempotencyKey).Scan(&existing)
        if err == nil {
                op, e := operation(ctx, tx, existing)
                if e != nil {
                        return op, e
                }
                if op.Artifact.SHA256 != a.ArtifactSHA256 || !op.NotBefore.Equal(a.NotBefore) || !a.NotBefore.IsZero() && !op.Deadline.Equal(a.Deadline) {
                        return op, ports.ErrConflict
                }
                return op, nil
        }
        if !errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        if !a.Now.Before(a.Deadline) || !a.NotBefore.IsZero() && !a.NotBefore.Before(a.Deadline) {
                return ports.Operation{}, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
