        apk, err := artifact(ctx, tx, a.ScopeID, a.ArtifactSHA256)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        var count int
        if err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT count(*) FROM desired_apps WHERE device_id=? AND package_name<>?", a.DeviceID, apk.PackageName).Scan(&count); err != nil {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        if count >= 64 {
                return ports.Operation{}, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        ops, err := activeOperations(ctx, tx, a.DeviceID)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        for _, op := range ops {
                if op.Artifact.PackageName == apk.PackageName {
                        op.State = "SUPERSEDED"
                        op.ErrorCode = "NEWER_DESIRED_REVISION"
                        if err = saveOperation(ctx, tx, op); err != nil {
                                return ports.Operation{}, err
                        }
                }
        }
        op := ports.Operation{ID: a.OperationID, DeviceID: a.DeviceID, Artifact: apk, Revision: d.DesiredRevision + 1, State: "QUEUED", NotBefore: a.NotBefore, Deadline: a.Deadline}
        data, err := json.Marshal(op)
        if err != nil {
                return op, err
        }
        _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "INSERT INTO operations(id,ordinal,device_id,package_name,artifact_sha256,state,revision,idempotency_key,data) VALUES(?,(SELECT COALESCE(MAX(ordinal),0)+1 FROM operations),?,?,?,?,?,?,?)", op.ID, a.DeviceID, apk.PackageName, apk.SHA256, op.State, op.Revision, a.IdempotencyKey, data)
        if err != nil {
                return op, err
        }
        _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, `INSERT INTO desired_apps(device_id,package_name,artifact_sha256,revision) VALUES(?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(device_id,package_name) DO UPDATE SET artifact_sha256=excluded.artifact_sha256,revision=excluded.revision`, a.DeviceID, apk.PackageName, apk.SHA256, op.Revision)
        if err != nil {
                return op, err
        }
        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "UPDATE devices SET desired_revision=? WHERE id=?", op.Revision, a.DeviceID); err != nil {
                return op, err
        }
        if err = deviceChanged(ctx, tx, a.DeviceID); err != nil {
                return ports.Operation{}, err
        }
        return op, nil
}

// ListOperations expires overdue work and returns a scoped cursor page of durable results.
// Usage: operations, err := store.ListOperations(ctx, scope, deviceID, "", 100, now).
func (s *Store) ListOperations(ctx context.Context, scope, device, after string, limit int, now time.Time) ([]ports.Operation, error) {
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        var id string
        if err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT id FROM devices WHERE scope_id=? AND id=?", scope, device).Scan(&id); errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return nil, ports.ErrNotFound
        } else if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if err = expire(ctx, tx, device, now); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        rows, err := tx.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM operations WHERE device_id=? AND id>? ORDER BY id LIMIT ?", device, after, limit)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        result := []ports.Operation{}
        for rows.Next() {
                var data []byte
                if err = rows.Scan(&data); err != nil {
                        rows.Close()
                        return nil, err
                }
                var op ports.Operation
                if err = json.Unmarshal(data, &op); err != nil {
                        rows.Close()
                        return nil, err
                }
                result = append(result, op)
        }
        err = errors.Join(rows.Err(), rows.Close())
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return result, tx.Commit()
}

// AuthorizeDownload checks credential, device, scope, active lease and deadline before exposing an artifact.
// Usage: artifact, err := store.AuthorizeDownload(ctx, deviceID, credentialHash, operationID, digest, leaseID, now).
func (s *Store) AuthorizeDownload(ctx context.Context, id string, hash []byte, operationID, digest, lease string, now time.Time) (ports.Artifact, error) {
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Artifact{}, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        d, actual, err := scanDevice(tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT "+deviceColumns+" FROM devices WHERE id=?", id))
        if errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                return ports.Artifact{}, ports.ErrUnauthorized
        }
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Artifact{}, err
        }
        if subtle.ConstantTimeCompare(actual, hash) != 1 {
                return ports.Artifact{}, ports.ErrUnauthorized
        }
        if d.State == "REVOKED" {
                return ports.Artifact{}, ports.ErrRevoked
        }
        op, err := operation(ctx, tx, operationID)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.Artifact{}, ports.ErrNotFound
        }
        if op.DeviceID != id || op.Artifact.ScopeID != d.ScopeID || op.Artifact.SHA256 != digest || op.LeaseID != lease || lease == "" || now.Before(op.NotBefore) || !now.Before(op.Deadline) || !now.Before(op.LeaseUntil) || (op.State != "RUNNING" && op.State != "WAITING_USER") {
                return ports.Artifact{}, ports.ErrUnauthorized
        }
        return artifact(ctx, tx, d.ScopeID, digest)
}

// Compare exact package, version and signer sets instead of trusting an installer callback.
func sameInstalled(observed *ports.InstalledApplication, a ports.Artifact) bool {
        if observed == nil || observed.PackageName != a.PackageName || observed.VersionCode != a.VersionCode {
                return false
        }
        left, right := append([]string(nil), observed.SigningCertificates...), append([]string(nil), a.SigningCertificates...)
        sort.Strings(left)
        sort.Strings(right)
        if len(left) != len(right) {
                return false
        }
        for i := range left {
                if left[i] != right[i] {
                        return false
                }
        }
        return true
}

// Deduplicate and bind a report to its assigned operation/lease before accepting an observed outcome.
func applyReport(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, device string, r ports.OperationReport) (ports.ReportReceipt, error) {
        data, err := json.Marshal(r)
        if err != nil {
                return ports.ReportReceipt{}, err
        }
        sum := sha256.Sum256(data)
        var previous, encoded []byte
        err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT body_hash,receipt FROM reports WHERE device_id=? AND report_id=?", device, r.ID).Scan(&previous, &encoded)
        if err == nil {
                if !bytes.Equal(previous, sum[:]) {
                        return ports.ReportReceipt{}, ports.ErrConflict
                }
                var receipt ports.ReportReceipt
                err = json.Unmarshal(encoded, &receipt)
                return receipt, err
        }
        if !errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return ports.ReportReceipt{}, err
        }
        receipt := ports.ReportReceipt{ReportID: r.ID}
        op, err := operation(ctx, tx, r.OperationID)
        switch {
        case errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound):
                receipt.Reason = "NOT_ASSIGNED"
        case err != nil:
                return receipt, err
        case op.DeviceID != device:
                receipt.Reason = "NOT_ASSIGNED"
        case op.State == "SUPERSEDED":
                receipt.Accepted = true
                receipt.Reason = "SUPERSEDED"
        case op.State == "SUCCEEDED" || op.State == "FAILED" || op.State == "CANCELLED":
                receipt.Accepted = true
                receipt.Reason = "TERMINAL"
        case op.LeaseID == "" || op.LeaseID != r.LeaseID:
                receipt.Reason = "STALE_LEASE"
        case r.Sequence <= op.ReportSequence:
                receipt.Reason = "STALE_REPORT"
        case r.State == "SUCCEEDED" && !sameInstalled(r.Observed, op.Artifact):
                receipt.Reason = "OBSERVATION_MISMATCH"
        default:
                op.State, op.ReportSequence, op.DownloadedBytes = r.State, r.Sequence, r.DownloadedBytes
                op.ErrorCode, op.ErrorMessage, op.Observed = r.ErrorCode, r.ErrorMessage, r.Observed
                if err = saveOperation(ctx, tx, op); err != nil {
                        return receipt, err
                }
                receipt.Accepted = true
        }
        encoded, err = json.Marshal(receipt)
        if err != nil {
                return receipt, err
        }
        _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "INSERT INTO reports(device_id,report_id,body_hash,receipt) VALUES(?,?,?,?)", device, r.ID, sum[:], encoded)
        return receipt, err
}

// Sync atomically authenticates, binds replay bytes, accepts observations and checkpoints the desired snapshot.
// Usage: snapshot, err := store.Sync(ctx, input).
func (s *Store) Sync(ctx context.Context, in ports.SyncInput) (ports.Snapshot, error) {
        empty := ports.Snapshot{}
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        d, actual, err := scanDevice(tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT "+deviceColumns+" FROM devices WHERE id=?", in.DeviceID))
        if errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                return empty, ports.ErrUnauthorized
        }
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if subtle.ConstantTimeCompare(actual, in.CredentialHash) != 1 {
                return empty, ports.ErrUnauthorized
        }
        if d.State == "REVOKED" {
                return empty, ports.ErrRevoked
        }
        payload, _ := json.Marshal(struct {
                Sequence, Revision   uint64
                Inventory            ports.Inventory
                Reports              []ports.OperationReport
                PolicyReport         *ports.PolicyReport
                RetainedOperationIDs []string
                CommandReports       []ports.CommandReport
                NetworkReport        *ports.NetworkReport
        }{in.Sequence, in.ReportedRevision, in.Inventory, in.Reports, in.PolicyReport, in.RetainedOperationIDs, in.CommandReports, in.NetworkReport})
        sum := sha256.Sum256(payload)
        var sequence uint64
        var previous, cached []byte
        if err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT sync_sequence,sync_hash,sync_snapshot FROM devices WHERE id=?", in.DeviceID).Scan(&sequence, &previous, &cached); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if in.Sequence == 0 || in.Sequence < sequence {
                return empty, ports.ErrStale
        }
        if in.Sequence == sequence {
                if !bytes.Equal(previous, sum[:]) {
                        return empty, ports.ErrConflict
                }
                var snapshot ports.Snapshot
                err = json.Unmarshal(cached, &snapshot)
                return snapshot, err
        }
        if in.ReportedRevision < d.ReportedRevision || in.ReportedRevision > d.DesiredRevision {
                return empty, ports.ErrStale
        }
        if err = expire(ctx, tx, in.DeviceID, in.Now); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if err = expireCommands(ctx, tx, in.DeviceID, in.Now); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        snapshot := ports.Snapshot{Sequence: in.Sequence}
        if len(in.CommandReports) > 16 {
                return empty, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        for _, report := range in.CommandReports {
                if err = applyCommandReport(ctx, tx, in.DeviceID, report); err != nil {
                        return empty, err
                }
                snapshot.CommandReceipts = append(snapshot.CommandReceipts, report.CommandID)
        }
        snapshot.Commands, err = dispatchCommand(ctx, tx, in.DeviceID, in.Inventory)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if len(in.RetainedOperationIDs) > 64 {
                return empty, ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        for _, id := range in.RetainedOperationIDs {
                op, err := operation(ctx, tx, id)
                if errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                        snapshot.StoppedOperationIDs = append(snapshot.StoppedOperationIDs, id)
                        continue
                }
                if err != nil {
                        return empty, err
                }
                if op.DeviceID != in.DeviceID || op.State == "CANCELLED" || op.State == "FAILED" || op.State == "SUPERSEDED" {
                        snapshot.StoppedOperationIDs = append(snapshot.StoppedOperationIDs, id)
                }
        }
        for _, report := range in.Reports {
                receipt, err := applyReport(ctx, tx, in.DeviceID, report)
                if err != nil {
                        return empty, err
                }
                snapshot.Receipts = append(snapshot.Receipts, receipt)
        }
        snapshot.Desired, err = desiredApplications(ctx, tx, d)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        // Reconcile drift after a previously confirmed install. A success report in
        // this request can precede its inventory observation, so wait for the next sync.
        for _, desired := range snapshot.Desired {
                matches := false
                for _, app := range in.Inventory.Applications {
                        if sameInstalled(&app, desired.Artifact) {
                                matches = true
                        }
                }
                if matches {
                        continue
                }
                var data []byte
                err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM operations WHERE device_id=? AND revision=? ORDER BY ordinal DESC LIMIT 1", in.DeviceID, desired.Revision).Scan(&data)
                if err != nil {
                        return empty, err
                }
                var previous ports.Operation
                if err = json.Unmarshal(data, &previous); err != nil {
                        return empty, err
                }
                if previous.State != "SUCCEEDED" {
                        continue
                }
                justCompleted := false
                for _, report := range in.Reports {
                        if report.OperationID == previous.ID && report.State == "SUCCEEDED" {
                                justCompleted = true
                        }
                }
                if justCompleted {
                        continue
                }
                random := make([]byte, 32)
                if _, err = rand.Read(random); err != nil {
                        return empty, err
                }
                id := base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(random)
                op := ports.Operation{ID: id, DeviceID: in.DeviceID, Artifact: desired.Artifact, Revision: desired.Revision, State: "QUEUED", Deadline: in.Now.Add(24 * time.Hour)}
                data, err = json.Marshal(op)
                if err != nil {
                        return empty, err
                }
                _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "INSERT INTO operations(id,ordinal,device_id,package_name,artifact_sha256,state,revision,idempotency_key,data) VALUES(?,(SELECT COALESCE(MAX(ordinal),0)+1 FROM operations),?,?,?,?,?,?,?)", id, in.DeviceID, desired.Artifact.PackageName, desired.Artifact.SHA256, op.State, op.Revision, "reconcile-"+id, data)
                if err != nil {
                        return empty, err
                }
        }
        ops, err := activeOperations(ctx, tx, in.DeviceID)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        for _, op := range ops {
                if in.Now.Before(op.NotBefore) {
                        continue
                }
                if len(snapshot.Operations) >= 8 {
                        break
                }
                if op.LeaseID == "" || !in.Now.Before(op.LeaseUntil) {
                        if op.Attempts >= 8 {
                                op.State = "FAILED"
                                op.ErrorCode = "ATTEMPTS_EXHAUSTED"
                                if err = saveOperation(ctx, tx, op); err != nil {
                                        return empty, err
                                }
                                continue
                        }
                        nonce := make([]byte, 24)
                        if _, err = rand.Read(nonce); err != nil {
                                return empty, err
                        }
                        op.LeaseID = hex.EncodeToString(nonce)
                        op.Attempts++
                }
                op.LeaseUntil = in.Now.Add(30 * time.Minute)
                if op.State == "QUEUED" {
                        op.State = "RUNNING"
                }
                if err = saveOperation(ctx, tx, op); err != nil {
                        return empty, err
                }
                snapshot.Operations = append(snapshot.Operations, op)
        }
        for _, want := range snapshot.Desired {
                compliance := ports.ApplicationCompliance{PackageName: want.Artifact.PackageName, Revision: want.Revision, State: "PENDING", Reason: "NOT_OBSERVED"}
                for _, app := range in.Inventory.Applications {
                        if sameInstalled(&app, want.Artifact) {
                                compliance.State, compliance.Reason = "APPLIED", ""
                        }
                }
                if compliance.State != "APPLIED" {
                        for _, cap := range in.Inventory.Capabilities {
                                if cap == "install_apk" {
                                        compliance.Reason = "INSTALL_PENDING"
                                }
                        }
                        if compliance.Reason == "NOT_OBSERVED" {
                                compliance.State, compliance.Reason = "UNSUPPORTED", "INSTALL_CAPABILITY_MISSING"
                        }
                        var data []byte
                        err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT data FROM operations WHERE device_id=? AND revision=? ORDER BY ordinal DESC LIMIT 1", in.DeviceID, want.Revision).Scan(&data)
                        if err != nil {
                                return empty, err
                        }
                        var op ports.Operation
                        if err = json.Unmarshal(data, &op); err != nil {
                                return empty, err
                        }
                        if op.State == "FAILED" || op.State == "CANCELLED" {
                                compliance.State, compliance.Reason = "FAILED", op.ErrorCode
                        }
                        if in.Now.Before(op.NotBefore) && op.State == "QUEUED" {
                                compliance.State, compliance.Reason = "PENDING", "SCHEDULED"
                        }
                        if op.State == "WAITING_USER" {
                                compliance.State, compliance.Reason = "PENDING", "USER_ACTION_REQUIRED"
                        }
                }
                snapshot.Compliance = append(snapshot.Compliance, compliance)
        }
        d.State, d.Inventory, d.LastSeenAt, d.ReportedRevision = "MANAGED", in.Inventory, in.Now, in.ReportedRevision
        policy, err := policyStatus(ctx, tx, d, snapshot.Desired, in.PolicyReport)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        snapshot.Network, err = networkStatus(ctx, tx, d, in.NetworkReport)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        snapshot.DesiredPolicy, snapshot.PolicyCompliance = policy.Desired, policy.Compliance
        snapshot.Device = d
        inventory, err := json.Marshal(in.Inventory)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        cached, err = json.Marshal(snapshot)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "UPDATE devices SET inventory=?,state='MANAGED',last_seen_at=?,reported_revision=?,sync_sequence=?,sync_hash=?,sync_snapshot=? WHERE id=?", inventory, in.Now.UnixMilli(), in.ReportedRevision, in.Sequence, sum[:], cached, in.DeviceID)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if err = deviceChanged(ctx, tx, in.DeviceID); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if err = tx.Commit(); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        return snapshot, nil
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package sqlstore

import (
        "bytes"
        "context"
        "database/sql"
        "encoding/json"
        "errors"
        "slices"
        "sort"
        "strings"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// Join current desired app revisions to scoped verified metadata within a consistent transaction.
func desiredApplications(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, d ports.Device) ([]ports.DesiredApplication, error) {
        rows, err := tx.QueryContext(ctx, `SELECT a.data,w.revision FROM desired_apps w LEFT JOIN artifacts a ON a.scope_id=? AND a.digest=w.artifact_sha256 WHERE w.device_id=? ORDER BY w.package_name`, d.ScopeID, d.ID)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer rows.Close()
        var apps []ports.DesiredApplication
        for rows.Next() {
                var data []byte
                var app ports.DesiredApplication
                if err = rows.Scan(&data, &app.Revision); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                if err = json.Unmarshal(data, &app.Artifact); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                apps = append(apps, app)
        }
        return apps, rows.Err()
}

// SetPolicy atomically validates required assignments and binds a normalized replacement to its request key.
// Usage: desired, err := store.SetPolicy(ctx, assignment).
func (s *Store) SetPolicy(ctx context.Context, input ports.PolicyAssignment) (ports.DesiredPolicy, error) {
        var empty ports.DesiredPolicy
        p, err := ports.NormalizePolicy(input.Policy)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        body, err := json.Marshal(p)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        d, _, err := scanDevice(tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT "+deviceColumns+" FROM devices WHERE scope_id=? AND id=?", input.ScopeID, input.DeviceID))
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if d.State == "REVOKED" {
                return empty, ports.ErrRevoked
        }
        var previous, response []byte
        err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT body,response FROM policy_requests WHERE device_id=? AND idempotency_key=?", d.ID, input.IdempotencyKey).Scan(&previous, &response)
        if err == nil {
                if !bytes.Equal(previous, body) {
                        return empty, ports.ErrConflict
                }
                err = json.Unmarshal(response, &empty)
                return empty, err
        }
        if !errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return empty, err
        }
        apps, err := desiredApplications(ctx, tx, d)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        for _, name := range p.RequiredPackages {
                if !slices.ContainsFunc(apps, func(a ports.DesiredApplication) bool { return a.Artifact.PackageName == name }) {
                        return empty, ports.ErrNotFound
                }
        }
        want := ports.DesiredPolicy{Revision: d.DesiredRevision + 1, Policy: p}
        response, err = json.Marshal(want)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, `INSERT INTO policies(device_id,data) VALUES(?,?) ON CONFLICT(device_id) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data,reported=NULL`, d.ID, response); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "INSERT INTO policy_requests(device_id,idempotency_key,body,response) VALUES(?,?,?,?)", d.ID, input.IdempotencyKey, body, response); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "UPDATE devices SET desired_revision=? WHERE id=?", want.Revision, d.ID); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        if err = deviceChanged(ctx, tx, d.ID); err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        return want, tx.Commit()
}

// Accept only current-snapshot observations and derive per-rule compliance independently of assignment.
func policyStatus(ctx context.Context, tx *transaction, d ports.Device, apps []ports.DesiredApplication, incoming *ports.PolicyReport) (ports.PolicyStatus, error) {
        var status ports.PolicyStatus
        var data, reported []byte
        err := tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT data,reported FROM policies WHERE device_id=?", d.ID).Scan(&data, &reported)
        if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return status, nil
        }
        if err != nil {
                return status, err
        }
        if err = json.Unmarshal(data, &status.Desired); err != nil {
                return status, err
        }
        if len(reported) > 0 {
                if err = json.Unmarshal(reported, &status.Reported); err != nil {
                        return status, err
                }
        }
        expected := ports.PolicyExpectations(status.Desired.Policy, apps)
        if incoming != nil && incoming.Revision == status.Desired.Revision && incoming.SnapshotRevision <= d.DesiredRevision && incoming.SnapshotRevision >= incoming.Revision {
                for _, r := range incoming.Results {
                        if _, ok := expected[r.RuleID]; !ok {
                                return status, ports.ErrConflict
                        }
                }
                if status.Reported == nil || incoming.SnapshotRevision >= status.Reported.SnapshotRevision {
                        status.Reported = incoming
                        encoded, err := json.Marshal(incoming)
                        if err != nil {
                                return status, err
                        }
                        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "UPDATE policies SET reported=? WHERE device_id=?", encoded, d.ID); err != nil {
                                return status, err
                        }
                }
        }
        keys := make([]string, 0, len(expected))
        for key := range expected {
                keys = append(keys, key)
        }
        sort.Strings(keys)
        for _, key := range keys {
                r := ports.PolicyResult{RuleID: key, State: "PENDING", Reason: "NOT_OBSERVED"}
                if !slices.Contains(d.Inventory.Capabilities, ports.PolicyCapability(key)) {
                        r.State, r.Reason = "UNSUPPORTED", "CAPABILITY_MISSING"
                }
                if status.Reported != nil && status.Reported.Revision == status.Desired.Revision && status.Reported.SnapshotRevision == d.DesiredRevision {
                        for _, observed := range status.Reported.Results {
                                if observed.RuleID != key {
                                        continue
                                }
                                r = observed
                                if r.State == "APPLIED" && r.ObservedValue != expected[key] {
                                        r.State, r.Reason = "FAILED", "OBSERVATION_MISMATCH"
                                }
                        }
                }
                status.Compliance = append(status.Compliance, r)
        }
        missing := map[string]bool{}
        for _, name := range status.Desired.Policy.RequiredPackages {
                matches := false
                for _, want := range apps {
                        if want.Artifact.PackageName == name {
                                for _, actual := range d.Inventory.Applications {
                                        if sameInstalled(&actual, want.Artifact) {
                                                matches = true
                                        }
                                }
                        }
                }
                missing[name] = !matches
        }
        for i := range status.Compliance {
                r := &status.Compliance[i]
                if d.State == "REVOKED" {
                        r.State, r.Reason = "PENDING", "DEVICE_REVOKED"
                        continue
                }
                if r.State != "APPLIED" {
                        continue
                }
                if strings.HasPrefix(r.RuleID, "required:") && missing[strings.TrimPrefix(r.RuleID, "required:")] {
                        r.State, r.Reason = "PENDING", "INVENTORY_MISMATCH"
                }
                if r.RuleID == "lock_task" {
                        for _, absent := range missing {
                                if absent {
                                        r.State, r.Reason = "PENDING", "REQUIRED_APPS_PENDING"
                                }
                        }
                }
                for name, absent := range missing {
                        if absent && (strings.HasPrefix(r.RuleID, "permission:"+name+":") || r.RuleID == "configuration:"+name) {
                                r.State, r.Reason = "PENDING", "REQUIRED_APP_PENDING"
                        }
                }
        }
        return status, nil
}

// GetPolicy returns one consistent scoped desired/reported/compliance view without mutating policy.
// Usage: status, err := store.GetPolicy(ctx, scope, deviceID).
func (s *Store) GetPolicy(ctx context.Context, scope, device string) (ports.PolicyStatus, error) {
        var empty ports.PolicyStatus
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        d, _, err := scanDevice(tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT "+deviceColumns+" FROM devices WHERE scope_id=? AND id=?", scope, device))
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        apps, err := desiredApplications(ctx, tx, d)
        if err != nil {
                return empty, err
        }
        return policyStatus(ctx, tx, d, apps, nil)
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
// Package sqlstore preserves shared transaction semantics across supported SQL dialects.
package sqlstore

import (
        "context"
        "crypto/subtle"
        "database/sql"
        "encoding/json"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

type Store struct{ db *database }

// Open verifies or explicitly initializes the current schema on a supplied pool; it never closes the pool.
// Usage: store, err := sqlstore.Open(ctx, db, "postgres", "kylok", true).
func Open(ctx context.Context, pool *sql.DB, dialect, schema string, initialize bool) (*Store, error) {
        if pool == nil {
                return nil, errors.New("database pool required")
        }
        if dialect != "sqlite" && dialect != "postgres" {
                return nil, errors.New("unsupported SQL dialect")
        }
        if dialect == "postgres" && !schemaPattern.MatchString(schema) {
                return nil, errors.New("schema must be a lowercase SQL identifier of 1–63 characters")
        }
        s := &Store{db: &database{pool: pool, dialect: dialect, schema: schema}}
        if err := s.migrate(ctx, initialize); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return s, nil
}

// Initialize the current development schema or reject an incomplete existing schema without repair.
func (s *Store) migrate(ctx context.Context, initialize bool) error {
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        var version int
        if s.db.dialect == "postgres" {
                if initialize {
                        if _, err = tx.Tx.ExecContext(ctx, `CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS "`+s.db.schema+`"`); err != nil {
                                return err
                        }
                        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_version(version BIGINT NOT NULL)"); err != nil {
                                return err
                        }
                }
                err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT version FROM schema_version").Scan(&version)
                if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) && initialize {
                        version = 0
                } else if err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        } else if err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "PRAGMA user_version").Scan(&version); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if version != 0 && version != 1 {
                return fmt.Errorf("unsupported database version %d", version)
        }
        if version == 0 {
                if !initialize {
                        return errors.New("schema initialization requires explicit opt-in")
                }
                schemaSQL := `
CREATE TABLE network_policies(device_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,data BLOB NOT NULL,reported BLOB);
CREATE TABLE network_requests(device_id TEXT NOT NULL,idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL,body BLOB NOT NULL,response BLOB NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(device_id,idempotency_key));
CREATE TABLE commands(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE,device_id TEXT NOT NULL,idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL,state TEXT NOT NULL,data BLOB NOT NULL,report BLOB,UNIQUE(device_id,idempotency_key));
CREATE INDEX commands_device ON commands(device_id,state,ordinal);
CREATE TABLE fleet_groups(scope_id TEXT NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,data BLOB NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(scope_id,id));
CREATE TABLE fleet_assignments(scope_id TEXT NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,artifact_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,pending INTEGER NOT NULL,data BLOB NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(scope_id,id));
CREATE TABLE enrollments(token_hash BLOB PRIMARY KEY,scope_id TEXT NOT NULL,expires_at INTEGER NOT NULL,artifact_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',attempt_id TEXT,key_hash BLOB,device_id TEXT,receipt BLOB,recover_until INTEGER);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX enrollments_correlation ON enrollments(scope_id,correlation_id) WHERE correlation_id<>'';
CREATE TABLE devices(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,scope_id TEXT NOT NULL,state TEXT NOT NULL,correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',inventory BLOB NOT NULL,credential_hash BLOB NOT NULL,created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,last_seen_at INTEGER NOT NULL,desired_revision INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,reported_revision INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,sync_sequence INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,sync_hash BLOB,sync_snapshot BLOB);
CREATE INDEX devices_scope_id ON devices(scope_id,id);
CREATE TABLE artifacts(scope_id TEXT NOT NULL,digest TEXT NOT NULL,data BLOB NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(scope_id,digest));
CREATE TABLE operations(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE,device_id TEXT NOT NULL,package_name TEXT NOT NULL,artifact_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,state TEXT NOT NULL,revision INTEGER NOT NULL,idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL,data BLOB NOT NULL,UNIQUE(device_id,idempotency_key));
CREATE INDEX operations_device ON operations(device_id,state,revision);
CREATE TABLE desired_apps(device_id TEXT NOT NULL,package_name TEXT NOT NULL,artifact_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,revision INTEGER NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(device_id,package_name));
CREATE TABLE reports(device_id TEXT NOT NULL,report_id TEXT NOT NULL,body_hash BLOB NOT NULL,receipt BLOB NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(device_id,report_id));
CREATE TABLE policies(device_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,data BLOB NOT NULL,reported BLOB);
CREATE TABLE policy_requests(device_id TEXT NOT NULL,idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL,body BLOB NOT NULL,response BLOB NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(device_id,idempotency_key));
CREATE TABLE change_heads(scope_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,sequence INTEGER NOT NULL,floor INTEGER NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE changes(scope_id TEXT NOT NULL,sequence INTEGER NOT NULL,kind TEXT NOT NULL,resource_id TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(scope_id,sequence));
CREATE TABLE projection_scans(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE,scope_id TEXT NOT NULL,watermark INTEGER NOT NULL,expires_at INTEGER NOT NULL);
CREATE INDEX projection_expiry ON projection_scans(scope_id,expires_at,id);
CREATE TABLE projection_items(snapshot_id TEXT NOT NULL,item_index INTEGER NOT NULL,data BLOB NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(snapshot_id,item_index));
PRAGMA user_version=1;`
                if s.db.dialect == "postgres" {
                        schemaSQL = strings.ReplaceAll(schemaSQL, " BLOB", " BYTEA")
                        schemaSQL = strings.ReplaceAll(schemaSQL, " INTEGER", " BIGINT")
                        schemaSQL = strings.ReplaceAll(schemaSQL, "PRAGMA user_version=1;", "INSERT INTO schema_version(version) VALUES(1);")
                }
                _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, schemaSQL)
                if err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        // user_version alone does not prove that the database still has the schema.
        // Resolve every required column before returning a ready store, without
        // scanning device data or attempting to repair an existing damaged database.
        for _, query := range []string{
                "SELECT device_id,data,reported FROM network_policies LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT device_id,idempotency_key,body,response FROM network_requests LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT id,ordinal,device_id,idempotency_key,state,data,report FROM commands LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT scope_id,id,data FROM fleet_groups LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT scope_id,id,artifact_sha256,pending,data FROM fleet_assignments LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT token_hash,scope_id,expires_at,artifact_sha256,correlation_id,attempt_id,key_hash,device_id,receipt,recover_until FROM enrollments LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT " + deviceColumns + " FROM devices LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT sync_sequence,sync_hash,sync_snapshot FROM devices LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT scope_id,digest,data FROM artifacts LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT id,ordinal,device_id,package_name,artifact_sha256,state,revision,idempotency_key,data FROM operations LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT device_id,package_name,artifact_sha256,revision FROM desired_apps LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT device_id,report_id,body_hash,receipt FROM reports LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT device_id,data,reported FROM policies LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT device_id,idempotency_key,body,response FROM policy_requests LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT scope_id,sequence,floor FROM change_heads LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT scope_id,sequence,kind,resource_id FROM changes LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT id,ordinal,scope_id,watermark,expires_at FROM projection_scans LIMIT 0",
                "SELECT snapshot_id,item_index,data FROM projection_items LIMIT 0",
        } {
                rows, err := tx.QueryContext(ctx, query)
                if err != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("invalid development schema: %w", err)
                }
                if err := rows.Close(); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        return tx.Commit()
}

// CreateEnrollment persists only a token verifier, scope, expiry and immutable bootstrap binding.
// Usage: err := store.CreateEnrollment(ctx, enrollment).
func (s *Store) CreateEnrollment(ctx context.Context, e ports.Enrollment) error {
        result, err := s.db.ExecContext(ctx, "INSERT INTO enrollments(token_hash,scope_id,expires_at,artifact_sha256,correlation_id) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING", e.TokenHash, e.ScopeID, e.ExpiresAt.UnixMilli(), e.ArtifactSHA256, e.CorrelationID)
        if err == nil {
                n, countErr := result.RowsAffected()
                if countErr != nil {
                        return countErr
                }
                if n == 0 {
                        return ports.ErrConflict
                }
        }
        return err
}

// Enrollment loads the token verifier's original scope, expiry and bootstrap identity.
// Usage: enrollment, err := store.Enrollment(ctx, tokenHash).
func (s *Store) Enrollment(ctx context.Context, hash []byte) (ports.Enrollment, error) {
        var e ports.Enrollment
        var expiry int64
        err := s.db.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT token_hash,scope_id,expires_at,artifact_sha256,correlation_id FROM enrollments WHERE token_hash=?", hash).Scan(&e.TokenHash, &e.ScopeID, &expiry, &e.ArtifactSHA256, &e.CorrelationID)
        if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return e, ports.ErrNotFound
        }
        e.ExpiresAt = time.UnixMilli(expiry).UTC()
        return e, err
}

// Consume serializes competing claims and atomically stores identity, credential verifier and encrypted recovery receipt.
// Usage: receipt, err := store.Consume(ctx, attempt).
func (s *Store) Consume(ctx context.Context, a ports.Attempt) ([]byte, error) {
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        var scope, correlation string
        var expiry int64
        var id, deviceID sql.NullString
        var recoverUntil sql.NullInt64
        var key, receipt []byte
        err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, `SELECT scope_id,correlation_id,expires_at,attempt_id,key_hash,device_id,receipt,recover_until FROM enrollments WHERE token_hash=?`, a.TokenHash).Scan(&scope, &correlation, &expiry, &id, &key, &deviceID, &receipt, &recoverUntil)
        if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return nil, ports.ErrNotFound
        }
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if id.Valid {
                if id.String != a.ID || subtle.ConstantTimeCompare(key, a.KeyHash) != 1 {
                        return nil, ports.ErrConsumed
                }
                var state string
                if err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT state FROM devices WHERE id=?", deviceID.String).Scan(&state); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                if state == "REVOKED" {
                        return nil, ports.ErrRevoked
                }
                if a.Now.UnixMilli() >= recoverUntil.Int64 {
                        return nil, ports.ErrRecoveryExpired
                }
                return receipt, nil
        }
        if a.Now.UnixMilli() >= expiry {
                return nil, ports.ErrExpired
        }
        if a.Device.ScopeID != scope || len(a.Receipt) == 0 || len(a.CredentialHash) != 32 || len(a.KeyHash) != 32 || a.ID == "" || !a.RecoverUntil.After(a.Now) {
                return nil, errors.New("invalid atomic enrollment candidate")
        }
        inventory, err := json.Marshal(a.Device.Inventory)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, `INSERT INTO devices(id,scope_id,state,correlation_id,inventory,credential_hash,created_at,last_seen_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`, a.Device.ID, scope, "ENROLLED", correlation, inventory, a.CredentialHash, a.Now.UnixMilli(), int64(0))
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, `UPDATE enrollments SET attempt_id=?,key_hash=?,device_id=?,receipt=?,recover_until=? WHERE token_hash=?`, a.ID, a.KeyHash, a.Device.ID, a.Receipt, a.RecoverUntil.UnixMilli(), a.TokenHash)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if err = recordChange(ctx, tx, scope, "device_changed", a.Device.ID); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if err = tx.Commit(); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return append([]byte(nil), a.Receipt...), nil
}

type scanner interface{ Scan(...any) error }

// Decode persisted device columns and normalize timestamps while preserving not-found semantics.
func scanDevice(row scanner) (ports.Device, []byte, error) {
        var d ports.Device
        var inventory, hash []byte
        var created, seen int64
        err := row.Scan(&d.ID, &d.ScopeID, &d.State, &d.CorrelationID, &inventory, &hash, &created, &seen, &d.DesiredRevision, &d.ReportedRevision)
        if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return d, nil, ports.ErrNotFound
        }
        if err != nil {
                return d, nil, err
        }
        if err = json.Unmarshal(inventory, &d.Inventory); err != nil {
                return d, nil, err
        }
        d.CreatedAt = time.UnixMilli(created).UTC()
        if seen != 0 {
                d.LastSeenAt = time.UnixMilli(seen).UTC()
        }
        return d, hash, nil
}

const deviceColumns = "id,scope_id,state,correlation_id,inventory,credential_hash,created_at,last_seen_at,desired_revision,reported_revision"

// Authenticate checks the credential verifier before rejecting revoked identities.
// Usage: device, err := store.Authenticate(ctx, deviceID, credentialHash).
func (s *Store) Authenticate(ctx context.Context, id string, hash []byte) (ports.Device, error) {
        d, actual, err := scanDevice(s.db.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT "+deviceColumns+" FROM devices WHERE id=?", id))
        if errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                return d, ports.ErrUnauthorized
        }
        if err != nil {
                return d, err
        }
        if subtle.ConstantTimeCompare(actual, hash) != 1 {
                return ports.Device{}, ports.ErrUnauthorized
        }
        if d.State == "REVOKED" {
                return ports.Device{}, ports.ErrRevoked
        }
        return d, nil
}

// ListDevices returns only the authorized scope's stable device cursor page.
// Usage: devices, err := store.ListDevices(ctx, scope, "", 100).
func (s *Store) ListDevices(ctx context.Context, scope, after string, limit int) ([]ports.Device, error) {
        rows, err := s.db.QueryContext(ctx, "SELECT "+deviceColumns+" FROM devices WHERE scope_id=? AND id>? ORDER BY id LIMIT ?", scope, after, limit)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer rows.Close()
        devices := []ports.Device{}
        for rows.Next() {
                d, _, err := scanDevice(rows)
                if err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                devices = append(devices, d)
        }
        return devices, rows.Err()
}

// Revoke serializes revocation with enrollment recovery and sync; it does not reset Android.
// Usage: err := store.Revoke(ctx, scope, deviceID).
func (s *Store) Revoke(ctx context.Context, scope, id string) error {
        tx, err := s.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        defer tx.Rollback()
        var state string
        if err = tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT state FROM devices WHERE scope_id=? AND id=?", scope, id).Scan(&state); errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
                return ports.ErrNotFound
        } else if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if state == "REVOKED" {
                return nil
        }
        if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, "UPDATE devices SET state='REVOKED' WHERE scope_id=? AND id=?", scope, id); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if err = recordChange(ctx, tx, scope, "device_changed", id); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return tx.Commit()
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
// Package testapk supplies mocked external-tool output for engine unit tests.
// These fixtures do not establish real APK signature or Android compatibility.
package testapk

import (
        "archive/zip"
        "bytes"
        "context"
        "os"
        "path/filepath"
        "testing"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/provisioning"
)

const Manifest = `<manifest xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android" package="io.kylok.unit" android:versionCode="3">
<uses-sdk android:minSdkVersion="33" android:targetSdkVersion="37"/>
<application android:allowBackup="false">
<receiver android:name=".Admin" android:exported="true" android:permission="android.permission.BIND_DEVICE_ADMIN">
<meta-data android:name="android.app.device_admin" android:resource="@xml/admin"/>
<intent-filter><action android:name="android.app.action.DEVICE_ADMIN_ENABLED"/></intent-filter></receiver>
<activity android:name=".Mode" android:exported="true" android:permission="android.permission.BIND_DEVICE_ADMIN"><intent-filter><action android:name="android.app.action.GET_PROVISIONING_MODE"/><category android:name="android.intent.category.DEFAULT"/></intent-filter></activity>
<activity android:name=".Compliance" android:exported="true" android:permission="android.permission.BIND_DEVICE_ADMIN"><intent-filter><action android:name="android.app.action.ADMIN_POLICY_COMPLIANCE"/><category android:name="android.intent.category.DEFAULT"/></intent-filter></activity>
</application></manifest>`

// Make creates a verified deterministic APK fixture using isolated fake Android tools.
// Usage: apk := testapk.Make(t, manifest).
func Make(t testing.TB, manifest string) *provisioning.APK {
        t.Helper()
        apk, err := InspectManifest(t, manifest)
        if err != nil {
                t.Fatal(err)
        }
        return apk
}

// InspectManifest exercises DPC validation against a supplied decoded manifest fixture.
// Usage: apk, err := testapk.InspectManifest(t, manifest).
func InspectManifest(t testing.TB, manifest string) (*provisioning.APK, error) {
        return inspect(t, manifest, false)
}

// InspectApplication exercises ordinary APK validation without DPC component requirements.
// Usage: apk, err := testapk.InspectApplication(t, manifest).
func InspectApplication(t testing.TB, manifest string) (*provisioning.APK, error) {
        return inspect(t, manifest, true)
}

// Build isolated fake verifier tools while keeping the same public APK inspection path under test.
func inspect(t testing.TB, manifest string, application bool) (*provisioning.APK, error) {
        t.Helper()
        dir := t.TempDir()
        path := filepath.Join(dir, "unit.apk")
        var data bytes.Buffer
        writer := zip.NewWriter(&data)
        entry, err := writer.Create("AndroidManifest.xml")
        if err != nil {
                t.Fatal(err)
        }
        if _, err = entry.Write([]byte(manifest)); err != nil {
                t.Fatal(err)
        }
        libEntry, err := writer.Create("lib/arm64-v8a/libkylok.so")
        if err != nil {
                t.Fatal(err)
        }
        if _, err = libEntry.Write([]byte("elf")); err != nil {
                t.Fatal(err)
        }
        if err = writer.Close(); err != nil {
                t.Fatal(err)
        }
        if err := os.WriteFile(path, data.Bytes(), 0600); err != nil {
                t.Fatal(err)
        }
        signer := filepath.Join(dir, "apksigner")
        analyzer := filepath.Join(dir, "apkanalyzer")
        if err := os.WriteFile(signer, []byte("#!/bin/sh\nprintf '%s\\n' 'Signer #1 certificate SHA-256 digest: aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa'\n"), 0700); err != nil {
                t.Fatal(err)
        }
        if err := os.WriteFile(analyzer, []byte("#!/bin/sh\ncat <<'MANIFEST'\n"+manifest+"\nMANIFEST\n"), 0700); err != nil {
                t.Fatal(err)
        }
        tools := provisioning.Tools{APKSigner: signer, APKAnalyzer: analyzer}
        if application {
                return provisioning.InspectApplication(context.Background(), path, tools)
        }
        return provisioning.Inspect(context.Background(), path, tools)
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
// Package kylok embeds enrollment, app delivery and device policies in a
// host-owned HTTP or HTTPS server, with durable defaults and optional adapters.
// Public APIs are experimental until the v1 release gates pass.
package kylok

import (
        "bytes"
        "context"
        "crypto/rand"
        "crypto/sha256"
        "encoding/base64"
        "encoding/hex"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "io"
        "net/http"
        "os"
        "path/filepath"
        "reflect"
        "regexp"
        "strings"
        "sync"
        "sync/atomic"
        "time"

        "connectrpc.com/connect"
        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/adapters/filesystem"
        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/adapters/sqlite"
        kylokv1 "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/gen/kylok/v1"
        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/gen/kylok/v1/kylokv1connect"
        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/provisioning"
)

// Options configures one embeddable SDK. Supply verified bootstrap content and a
// device-reachable origin; durable defaults use DataDir. Supplied providers remain host-owned.
type Options struct {
        // HTTP/HTTPS origin advertised to devices; it does not bind or start a listener.
        PublicURL string
        // Signed DPC APK verified by provisioning.Inspect.
        Bootstrap *provisioning.APK
        // Persistent default database/artifact directory; unnecessary when both providers are supplied.
        DataDir string
        // Optional concurrent transactional provider; never replaced with an in-memory fallback on failure.
        Store ports.StateStore
        // Optional immutable content provider; must preserve the ArtifactStore integrity contract.
        Artifacts ports.ArtifactStore
        // Explicit opt-in for local provisioning proofs; disabled for normal hosts.
        AllowDebugAPK bool
        // Optional wall clock for deterministic expiry/deadline checks; defaults to time.Now.
        Clock func() time.Time
        // Optional APK origin. Defaults to PublicURL; independently supports HTTP/HTTPS
        // and domain/IP hosts. Mount BootstrapHandler on a separate origin as needed.
        BootstrapURL string
        // Optional sanitized best-effort committed-event sink; see EventSink for cancellation/delivery rules.
        Events EventSink
        // Optional host-owned resolver for immutable scoped Wi-Fi passphrase versions. Required for protected profiles.
        NetworkSecrets ports.NetworkSecretProvider
        // Per callback; defaults to one second, at most 30 seconds. A non-cooperative
        // host callback can stall only its one worker, never authentication or sync.
        EventTimeout time.Duration
}

// SDK owns the management facade and agent handlers. Hosts own authorization and
// listeners, drain requests before Close, and close their own supplied providers.
type SDK struct {
        store          ports.StateStore
        artifacts      ports.ArtifactStore
        networkSecrets ports.NetworkSecretProvider
        bootstrap      *provisioning.APK
        origin         string
        bootstrapURL   string
        clock          func() time.Time
        closeStore     func() error
        closed         atomic.Bool
        closeOnce      sync.Once
        closeErr       error
        allowDebugAPK  bool
        events         *eventDispatcher
}
type Enrollment struct {
        CorrelationID string
        Token         string
        ScopeID       string
        ExpiresAt     time.Time
}

var idPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,127}$`)
var devicePattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$`)
var digestPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{64}$`)

// New validates options/current storage schema and registers the verified APK.
// It starts no listeners. An optional event sink uses one bounded SDK worker.
// The host owns TLS, authorization, supplied providers and shutdown.
// Custom providers remain host-owned even if construction fails.
// Usage: sdk, err := kylok.New(ctx, kylok.Options{PublicURL: origin, Bootstrap: apk, DataDir: dataDir}).
func New(ctx context.Context, o Options) (_ *SDK, err error) {
        if o.EventTimeout == 0 {
                o.EventTimeout = time.Second
        }
        if o.EventTimeout < time.Millisecond || o.EventTimeout > 30*time.Second {
                return nil, errors.New("EventTimeout must be 1ms–30s")
        }
        for _, provider := range []any{o.Store, o.Artifacts, o.NetworkSecrets} {
                if provider != nil && reflect.ValueOf(provider).Kind() == reflect.Ptr && reflect.ValueOf(provider).IsNil() {
                        return nil, errors.New("typed nil persistence provider")
                }
        }
        origin, err := provisioning.Origin(o.PublicURL)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        bootstrapURL := origin
        if o.BootstrapURL != "" {
                bootstrapURL, err = provisioning.Origin(o.BootstrapURL)
                if err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
        }
        if !o.Bootstrap.IsDPC() {
                return nil, errors.New("verified Bootstrap APK required")
        }
        if o.Bootstrap.Metadata().Debuggable && !o.AllowDebugAPK {
                return nil, errors.New("debuggable bootstrap APK requires explicit AllowDebugAPK")
        }
        if o.Store == nil || o.Artifacts == nil {
                if o.DataDir == "" {
                        return nil, errors.New("DataDir required for default persistence providers")
                }
                if err = os.MkdirAll(o.DataDir, 0700); err != nil {
                        return nil, fmt.Errorf("create DataDir: %w", err)
                }
        }
        s := &SDK{origin: origin, bootstrapURL: bootstrapURL, bootstrap: o.Bootstrap, store: o.Store, artifacts: o.Artifacts, clock: o.Clock, allowDebugAPK: o.AllowDebugAPK, networkSecrets: o.NetworkSecrets}
        defer func() {
                if err != nil {
                        s.Close()
                }
        }()
        if s.clock == nil {
                s.clock = time.Now
        }
        if s.store == nil {
                store, e := sqlite.Open(ctx, filepath.Join(o.DataDir, "kylok.sqlite"))
                if e != nil {
                        return nil, e
                }
                s.store, s.closeStore = store, store.Close
        }
        if s.artifacts == nil {
                s.artifacts, err = filesystem.Open(filepath.Join(o.DataDir, "artifacts"))
                if err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
        }
        metadata := s.bootstrap.Metadata()
        if err = s.artifacts.Put(ctx, metadata.SHA256, s.bootstrap.Bytes()); err != nil {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("persist bootstrap: %w", err)
        }
        reader, err := s.artifacts.Open(ctx, metadata.SHA256)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("open persisted bootstrap: %w", err)
        }
        actual, readErr := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(reader, provisioning.MaxAPKBytes+1))
        closeErr := reader.Close()
        if err = errors.Join(readErr, closeErr); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if !bytes.Equal(actual, s.bootstrap.Bytes()) {
                return nil, errors.New("artifact provider returned different bootstrap content")
        }
        if err = s.store.RegisterArtifact(ctx, artifactMetadata("", s.bootstrap)); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        s.events = newEvents(o.Events, o.EventTimeout)
        return s, nil
}

// Close cancels SDK-owned notifications and storage exactly once; supplied providers remain host-owned.
// Usage: defer sdk.Close() after draining host listeners; hosts close supplied stores separately.
func (s *SDK) Close() error {
        s.closeOnce.Do(func() {
                s.closed.Store(true)
                s.events.close()
                if s.closeStore != nil {
                        s.closeErr = s.closeStore()
                }
        })
        return s.closeErr
}

// Generate 256 unpredictable bits for opaque single-use tokens, identities and device credentials.
func secret() (string, error) {
        b := make([]byte, 32)
        if _, err := rand.Read(b); err != nil {
                return "", err
        }
        return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(b), nil
}

// Store a fixed-size verifier instead of persisting a recoverable bearer secret.
func hash(value string) []byte { h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(value)); return h[:] }

// CreateEnrollment creates a single-use token bound to the authorized scope and current verified DPC APK.
// Usage: enrollment, err := sdk.CreateEnrollment(ctx, scope, 15*time.Minute).
func (s *SDK) CreateEnrollment(ctx context.Context, scope string, ttl time.Duration) (Enrollment, error) {
        return s.CreateEnrollmentWithOptions(ctx, EnrollmentOptions{ScopeID: scope, TTL: ttl})
}

// CreateEnrollmentWithOptions creates an opaque correlation binding and a private single-use token.
// Nonempty correlation IDs are unique in their scope, including after consumption/expiry.
// Duplicate creation returns ErrConflict, never the original token. If the creation response
// is lost, use a new correlation ID and let the unclaimed token expire; no read exposes it.
// Usage: e, err := sdk.CreateEnrollmentWithOptions(ctx, EnrollmentOptions{ScopeID: scope, TTL: 15*time.Minute, CorrelationID: requestID}).
func (s *SDK) CreateEnrollmentWithOptions(ctx context.Context, input EnrollmentOptions) (Enrollment, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return Enrollment{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(input.ScopeID) || input.TTL < time.Minute || input.TTL > 24*time.Hour || (input.CorrelationID != "" && !idPattern.MatchString(input.CorrelationID)) {
                return Enrollment{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: scope/correlation invalid; TTL must be 1 minute to 24 hours", ErrInvalidArgument)
        }
        token, err := secret()
        if err != nil {
                return Enrollment{}, err
        }
        expiry := s.clock().UTC().Add(input.TTL).Truncate(time.Millisecond)
        e := ports.Enrollment{TokenHash: hash(token), ScopeID: input.ScopeID, ExpiresAt: expiry, ArtifactSHA256: s.bootstrap.Metadata().SHA256, CorrelationID: input.CorrelationID}
        if err = s.store.CreateEnrollment(ctx, e); err != nil {
                return Enrollment{}, err
        }
        return Enrollment{Token: token, ScopeID: input.ScopeID, ExpiresAt: expiry, CorrelationID: input.CorrelationID}, nil
}

// QR renders private provisioning JSON using this host's configured API and bootstrap origins.
// Usage: payload, err := sdk.QR(ctx, enrollment.Token); keep the returned JSON private.
func (s *SDK) QR(ctx context.Context, token string) ([]byte, error) {
        return s.QRWithOrigins(ctx, token, s.origin, s.bootstrapURL)
}

// QRWithOrigins issues the same verified enrollment through host-selected API
// and APK origins, for example another bound LAN interface or a custom domain.
// Changing a QR does not change an already enrolled device's endpoint.
// Usage: payload, err := sdk.QRWithOrigins(ctx, token, "http://192.168.1.5:8080", "http://192.168.1.5:8080").
func (s *SDK) QRWithOrigins(ctx context.Context, token, apiOrigin, bootstrapOrigin string) ([]byte, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        apiOrigin, err := provisioning.Origin(apiOrigin)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        bootstrapOrigin, err = provisioning.Origin(bootstrapOrigin)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        e, err := s.store.Enrollment(ctx, hash(token))
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if !s.clock().Before(e.ExpiresAt) {
                return nil, ports.ErrExpired
        }
        if e.ArtifactSHA256 != s.bootstrap.Metadata().SHA256 {
                return nil, errors.New("enrollment references a different bootstrap APK")
        }
        extras := map[string]string{"kylok.server": apiOrigin, "kylok.enrollment_token": token}
        return s.bootstrap.QR(bootstrapOrigin, extras)
}

// ListDevices and RevokeDevice are in-process management APIs. Hosts must authorize
// the business user and scope before invoking them; no remote management route exists.
// Usage: devices, err := sdk.ListDevices(ctx, scope, "", 100).
func (s *SDK) ListDevices(ctx context.Context, scope, after string, limit int) ([]Device, error) {
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || limit < 1 || limit > 100 {
                return nil, invalidArgument("scope required; limit must be 1–100")
        }
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if after != "" && !devicePattern.MatchString(after) {
                return nil, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        values, err := s.store.ListDevices(ctx, scope, after, limit)
        return publicDevices(values), err
}

// RevokeDevice rejects future authenticated activity and recovery for one authorized scope/device.
// Usage: err := sdk.RevokeDevice(ctx, scope, deviceID).
func (s *SDK) RevokeDevice(ctx context.Context, scope, id string) error {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(id) {
                return invalidArgument("scope and device ID required")
        }
        err := s.store.Revoke(ctx, scope, id)
        if err == nil {
                s.emit("device_revoked", scope, id, 0, "")
        }
        return err
}

// Handler exposes agent RPCs, public bootstrap APKs and authorized managed APKs,
// never administrator routes. Hosts enable HTTP/2 (including h2c for HTTP). TLS
// is required for HTTPS origins; forwarded TLS headers are not trusted.
// Usage: server.Handler = sdk.Handler(); enable HTTP/2 and drain the listener before sdk.Close().
func (s *SDK) Handler() http.Handler {
        mux := http.NewServeMux()
        path, handler := kylokv1connect.NewAgentServiceHandler(&agentService{s}, connect.WithReadMaxBytes(1<<20))
        mux.Handle(path, handler)
        mux.Handle("/bootstrap/", s.bootstrapHandler())
        mux.Handle("/artifacts/", s.applicationHandler())
        mux.HandleFunc("/network-probe", s.networkProbe)
        return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
                if s.closed.Load() {
                        http.Error(w, "SDK closed", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
                        return
                }
                if strings.HasPrefix(s.origin, "https://") && r.TLS == nil {
                        http.Error(w, "HTTPS required", http.StatusUpgradeRequired)
                        return
                }
                mux.ServeHTTP(w, r)
        })
}

// BootstrapHandler serves only immutable APKs, never agent/management routes or QR secrets.
// It follows the selected BootstrapURL scheme, independently of the API origin.
// Usage: bootstrapServer.Handler = sdk.BootstrapHandler().
func (s *SDK) BootstrapHandler() http.Handler {
        handler := s.bootstrapHandler()
        return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
                if s.closed.Load() {
                        http.Error(w, "SDK closed", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
                        return
                }
                if strings.HasPrefix(s.bootstrapURL, "https://") && r.TLS == nil {
                        http.Error(w, "HTTPS required", http.StatusUpgradeRequired)
                        return
                }
                handler.ServeHTTP(w, r)
        })
}

// Serve only explicitly public bootstrap catalog entries and verify their retained bytes before delivery.
func (s *SDK) bootstrapHandler() http.Handler {
        // Keep already-issued bootstrap URLs valid after a host APK upgrade/restart.
        // Only empty-scope catalog entries are public; managed app bytes stay authorized.
        return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
                if s.closed.Load() {
                        http.Error(w, "SDK closed", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
                        return
                }
                if r.Method != http.MethodGet && r.Method != http.MethodHead {
                        w.Header().Set("Allow", "GET, HEAD")
                        w.WriteHeader(http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)
                        return
                }
                digest := strings.TrimSuffix(strings.TrimPrefix(r.URL.Path, "/bootstrap/"), ".apk")
                decoded, err := hex.DecodeString(digest)
                if err != nil || len(decoded) != 32 || r.URL.Path != "/bootstrap/"+digest+".apk" {
                        http.NotFound(w, r)
                        return
                }
                if _, err := s.store.Artifact(r.Context(), "", digest); err != nil {
                        http.NotFound(w, r)
                        return
                }
                reader, err := s.artifacts.Open(r.Context(), digest)
                if err != nil {
                        http.NotFound(w, r)
                        return
                }
                defer reader.Close()
                data, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(reader, provisioning.MaxAPKBytes+1))
                actual := sha256.Sum256(data)
                if err != nil || len(data) > provisioning.MaxAPKBytes || hex.EncodeToString(actual[:]) != digest {
                        http.Error(w, "Artifact integrity failure", http.StatusInternalServerError)
                        return
                }
                w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/vnd.android.package-archive")
                w.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=31536000, immutable")
                w.Header().Set("ETag", `"`+digest+`"`)
                w.Header().Set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
                http.ServeContent(w, r, digest+".apk", time.Time{}, bytes.NewReader(data))
        })
}

// Project stored self-reported inventory into the protocol without adding support or attestation claims.
func inventoryMessage(i ports.Inventory) *kylokv1.Inventory {
        result := &kylokv1.Inventory{ApiLevel: i.APILevel, Model: i.Model, BuildFingerprint: i.BuildFingerprint, AgentVersion: i.AgentVersion, DeviceOwner: i.DeviceOwner, Capabilities: i.Capabilities, SupportedAbis: i.SupportedABIs}
        for _, app := range i.Applications {
                result.Applications = append(result.Applications, &kylokv1.InstalledApplication{PackageName: app.PackageName, VersionCode: app.VersionCode, SigningCertificates: app.SigningCertificates})
        }
        return result
}

// Expose public device identity, revisions and timestamps without its credential verifier.
func deviceMessage(d ports.Device) *kylokv1.Device {
        state := kylokv1.DeviceState_DEVICE_STATE_ENROLLED
        if d.State == "MANAGED" {
                state = kylokv1.DeviceState_DEVICE_STATE_MANAGED
        }
        if d.State == "REVOKED" {
                state = kylokv1.DeviceState_DEVICE_STATE_REVOKED
        }
        var seen int64
        if !d.LastSeenAt.IsZero() {
                seen = d.LastSeenAt.UnixMilli()
        }
        return &kylokv1.Device{Id: d.ID, ScopeId: d.ScopeID, State: state, Inventory: inventoryMessage(d.Inventory), CreatedAtUnixMillis: d.CreatedAt.UnixMilli(), LastSeenAtUnixMillis: seen, DesiredRevision: d.DesiredRevision, ReportedRevision: d.ReportedRevision}
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package kylok

import (
        "bytes"
        "context"
        "crypto/sha256"
        "encoding/base64"
        "encoding/hex"
        "encoding/json"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "io"
        "regexp"
        "slices"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// Management errors support errors.Is independently of transport and persistence adapters.
var (
        ErrInvalidArgument = ports.ErrInvalidArgument
        ErrNotFound        = ports.ErrNotFound
        ErrConflict        = ports.ErrConflict
        ErrExpired         = ports.ErrExpired
        ErrReferenced      = ports.ErrReferenced
        ErrConsumed        = ports.ErrConsumed
        ErrRecoveryExpired = ports.ErrRecoveryExpired
        ErrRevoked         = ports.ErrRevoked
        ErrUnauthorized    = ports.ErrUnauthorized
        ErrStale           = ports.ErrStale
        ErrClosed          = errors.New("SDK closed")
)

// EnrollmentOptions binds a private token to an authorized scope and optional opaque host correlation.
// CorrelationID is association metadata, never authorization; it must not contain secrets.
type EnrollmentOptions struct {
        ScopeID       string
        TTL           time.Duration
        CorrelationID string
}

// Device is a detached host-facing identity and observation. CorrelationID is host-only association metadata.
type Device struct {
        ID               string    `json:"id"`
        ScopeID          string    `json:"scope_id"`
        CorrelationID    string    `json:"correlation_id,omitempty"`
        State            string    `json:"state"`
        Inventory        Inventory `json:"inventory"`
        CreatedAt        time.Time `json:"created_at"`
        LastSeenAt       time.Time `json:"last_seen_at"`
        DesiredRevision  uint64    `json:"desired_revision"`
        ReportedRevision uint64    `json:"reported_revision"`
}

// Inventory contains device-reported observations, not attestation or a firmware support guarantee.
type Inventory struct {
        APILevel         uint32                 `json:"api_level"`
        Model            string                 `json:"model"`
        BuildFingerprint string                 `json:"build_fingerprint"`
        AgentVersion     string                 `json:"agent_version"`
        DeviceOwner      bool                   `json:"device_owner"`
        Capabilities     []string               `json:"capabilities"`
        Applications     []InstalledApplication `json:"applications"`
        SupportedABIs    []string               `json:"supported_abis"`
}

// InstalledApplication identifies an observed package version and signing certificates.
type InstalledApplication struct {
        PackageName         string   `json:"package_name"`
        VersionCode         int64    `json:"version_code"`
        SigningCertificates []string `json:"signing_certificates"`
}

// Artifact is immutable verified application metadata, with no storage location or download credentials.
type Artifact struct {
        ScopeID             string   `json:"scope_id"`
        SHA256              string   `json:"sha256"`
        PackageName         string   `json:"package_name"`
        VersionCode         int64    `json:"version_code"`
        MinSDK              uint32   `json:"min_sdk"`
        MaxSDK              uint32   `json:"max_sdk"`
        SigningCertificates []string `json:"signing_certificates"`
        SupportedABIs       []string `json:"supported_abis"`
        SizeBytes           int64    `json:"size_bytes"`
}

// Operation exposes durable progress/results without agent lease tokens or report checkpoint internals.
type Operation struct {
        ID              string                `json:"id"`
        DeviceID        string                `json:"device_id"`
        Artifact        Artifact              `json:"artifact"`
        Revision        uint64                `json:"revision"`
        State           string                `json:"state"`
        NotBefore       time.Time             `json:"not_before"`
        Deadline        time.Time             `json:"deadline"`
        DownloadedBytes int64                 `json:"downloaded_bytes"`
        ErrorCode       string                `json:"error_code"`
        ErrorMessage    string                `json:"error_message"`
        Observed        *InstalledApplication `json:"observed,omitempty"`
}

// DeviceFilter selects exact state/correlation matches within one authorized scope.
type DeviceFilter struct {
        State         string
        CorrelationID string
}

// OperationFilter selects an exact operation state for one scoped device.
type OperationFilter struct{ State string }

// PageRequest selects a live ascending-ID page; zero Limit defaults to 20, and the maximum is 100.
// Cursor is opaque and bound to the scope/device/filter; authorization is still required on every request.
type PageRequest struct {
        Cursor string
        Limit  int
}

// DevicePage returns detached observations and a cursor only when more matching results exist.
type DevicePage struct {
        Items      []Device
        NextCursor string
}

// OperationPage returns detached progress/results and a cursor only when more matching results exist.
type OperationPage struct {
        Items      []Operation
        NextCursor string
}

type pageCursor struct {
        Version int    `json:"v"`
        Binding string `json:"b"`
        After   string `json:"a"`
}

const defaultPageSize = 20
const maximumPageSize = 100
const maximumCursorBytes = 1024

// Reject work after Close, without closing or claiming ownership of supplied providers.
func (s *SDK) checkOpen(ctx context.Context) error {
        if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if s.closed.Load() {
                return ErrClosed
        }
        return nil
}

// Ready checks current backend/bootstrap availability without mutating state or starting a listener.
// Drain host handlers before Close. Supplied providers remain host-owned and readiness is a point-in-time observation.
// Usage: if err := sdk.Ready(ctx); err != nil { respondUnavailable(err) }.
func (s *SDK) Ready(ctx context.Context) error {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if err := s.store.Check(ctx); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        reader, err := s.artifacts.Open(ctx, s.bootstrap.Metadata().SHA256)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if err = reader.Close(); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return s.checkOpen(ctx)
}

// GetDevice reads one identity in an authorized scope; absent and foreign identities both return ErrNotFound.
// Usage: device, err := sdk.GetDevice(ctx, scope, deviceID).
func (s *SDK) GetDevice(ctx context.Context, scope, id string) (Device, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return Device{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(id) {
                return Device{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        value, err := s.store.GetDevice(ctx, scope, id)
        return publicDevice(value), err
}
