// GetOperation reads scoped durable progress and expires overdue work; it never exposes a download lease.
// Absent, foreign-scope and foreign-device operations all return ErrNotFound.
// Usage: operation, err := sdk.GetOperation(ctx, scope, deviceID, operationID).
func (s *SDK) GetOperation(ctx context.Context, scope, device, id string) (Operation, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return Operation{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(device) || !devicePattern.MatchString(id) {
                return Operation{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        value, err := s.store.GetOperation(ctx, scope, device, id, s.clock().UTC())
        return publicOperation(value), err
}

// Bind a keyset cursor to every authorization/filter dimension; cursors are not authorization capabilities.
func cursorBinding(kind, scope, device, state, correlation string) string {
        data, _ := json.Marshal([]string{kind, scope, device, state, correlation})
        digest := sha256.Sum256(data)
        return hex.EncodeToString(digest[:])
}

// Decode only the supported bounded cursor shape and reject reuse across scopes/devices/filters.
func readPage(page PageRequest, binding string) (string, int, error) {
        return readKeysetPage(page, binding, devicePattern)
}

// Validate the key shape appropriate to the resource while preserving query-bound opaque cursors.
func readKeysetPage(page PageRequest, binding string, pattern *regexp.Regexp) (string, int, error) {
        if page.Limit == 0 {
                page.Limit = defaultPageSize
        }
        if page.Limit < 1 || page.Limit > maximumPageSize || len(page.Cursor) > maximumCursorBytes {
                return "", 0, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        if page.Cursor == "" {
                return "", page.Limit, nil
        }
        data, err := base64.RawURLEncoding.DecodeString(page.Cursor)
        if err != nil {
                return "", 0, fmt.Errorf("%w: malformed cursor", ErrInvalidArgument)
        }
        var cursor pageCursor
        decoder := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(data))
        decoder.DisallowUnknownFields()
        if err = decoder.Decode(&cursor); err != nil {
                return "", 0, fmt.Errorf("%w: malformed cursor", ErrInvalidArgument)
        }
        var extra any
        if decoder.Decode(&extra) != io.EOF || cursor.Version != 1 || cursor.Binding != binding || !pattern.MatchString(cursor.After) {
                return "", 0, fmt.Errorf("%w: cursor does not match query", ErrInvalidArgument)
        }
        return cursor.After, page.Limit, nil
}

// Encode a versioned continuation token without any bearer credentials or storage internals.
func nextPage(binding, after string) string {
        data, _ := json.Marshal(pageCursor{Version: 1, Binding: binding, After: after})
        return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(data)
}

// QueryDevices reads a live keyset page with exact optional filters. Concurrent inserts behind
// the cursor or mutable filter changes require a new scan; this is not a frozen snapshot.
// Usage: page, err := sdk.QueryDevices(ctx, scope, DeviceFilter{CorrelationID: requestID}, PageRequest{Limit: 20}).
func (s *SDK) QueryDevices(ctx context.Context, scope string, filter DeviceFilter, page PageRequest) (DevicePage, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return DevicePage{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !slices.Contains([]string{"", "ENROLLED", "MANAGED", "REVOKED"}, filter.State) || (filter.CorrelationID != "" && !idPattern.MatchString(filter.CorrelationID)) {
                return DevicePage{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        binding := cursorBinding("devices", scope, "", filter.State, filter.CorrelationID)
        after, limit, err := readPage(page, binding)
        if err != nil {
                return DevicePage{}, err
        }
        values, err := s.store.QueryDevices(ctx, ports.DeviceQuery{ScopeID: scope, After: after, State: filter.State, CorrelationID: filter.CorrelationID, Limit: limit + 1})
        if err != nil {
                return DevicePage{}, err
        }
        result := DevicePage{}
        if len(values) > limit {
                result.NextCursor = nextPage(binding, values[limit-1].ID)
                values = values[:limit]
        }
        result.Items = publicDevices(values)
        return result, nil
}

// QueryOperations reads a live operation page for one authorized device; expiry is evaluated on each read.
// Usage: page, err := sdk.QueryOperations(ctx, scope, deviceID, OperationFilter{State: "SUCCEEDED"}, PageRequest{Limit: 20}).
func (s *SDK) QueryOperations(ctx context.Context, scope, device string, filter OperationFilter, page PageRequest) (OperationPage, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return OperationPage{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(device) || !slices.Contains([]string{"", "QUEUED", "RUNNING", "WAITING_USER", "SUCCEEDED", "FAILED", "SUPERSEDED", "CANCELLED"}, filter.State) {
                return OperationPage{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        binding := cursorBinding("operations", scope, device, filter.State, "")
        after, limit, err := readPage(page, binding)
        if err != nil {
                return OperationPage{}, err
        }
        values, err := s.store.QueryOperations(ctx, ports.OperationQuery{ScopeID: scope, DeviceID: device, After: after, State: filter.State, Limit: limit + 1, Now: s.clock().UTC()})
        if err != nil {
                return OperationPage{}, err
        }
        result := OperationPage{}
        if len(values) > limit {
                result.NextCursor = nextPage(binding, values[limit-1].ID)
                values = values[:limit]
        }
        result.Items = publicOperations(values)
        return result, nil
}

// Detach nested arrays so a consumer cannot mutate provider-owned memory through public values.
func publicInstalled(value ports.InstalledApplication) InstalledApplication {
        return InstalledApplication{PackageName: value.PackageName, VersionCode: value.VersionCode, SigningCertificates: slices.Clone(value.SigningCertificates)}
}

// Project observation fields into host domain types without aliasing provider slices.
func publicDevice(value ports.Device) Device {
        inventory := Inventory{APILevel: value.Inventory.APILevel, Model: value.Inventory.Model, BuildFingerprint: value.Inventory.BuildFingerprint, AgentVersion: value.Inventory.AgentVersion, DeviceOwner: value.Inventory.DeviceOwner, Capabilities: slices.Clone(value.Inventory.Capabilities), SupportedABIs: slices.Clone(value.Inventory.SupportedABIs)}
        for _, app := range value.Inventory.Applications {
                inventory.Applications = append(inventory.Applications, publicInstalled(app))
        }
        return Device{ID: value.ID, ScopeID: value.ScopeID, CorrelationID: value.CorrelationID, State: value.State, Inventory: inventory, CreatedAt: value.CreatedAt, LastSeenAt: value.LastSeenAt, DesiredRevision: value.DesiredRevision, ReportedRevision: value.ReportedRevision}
}

// Keep catalog metadata detached from provider-owned backing storage.
func publicArtifact(value ports.Artifact) Artifact {
        return Artifact{ScopeID: value.ScopeID, SHA256: value.SHA256, PackageName: value.PackageName, VersionCode: value.VersionCode, MinSDK: value.MinSDK, MaxSDK: value.MaxSDK, SigningCertificates: slices.Clone(value.SigningCertificates), SupportedABIs: slices.Clone(value.SupportedABIs), SizeBytes: value.SizeBytes}
}

// Strip lease/checkpoint fields at the host boundary while retaining truthful observed results.
func publicOperation(value ports.Operation) Operation {
        result := Operation{ID: value.ID, DeviceID: value.DeviceID, Artifact: publicArtifact(value.Artifact), Revision: value.Revision, State: value.State, Deadline: value.Deadline, NotBefore: value.NotBefore, DownloadedBytes: value.DownloadedBytes, ErrorCode: value.ErrorCode, ErrorMessage: value.ErrorMessage}
        if value.Observed != nil {
                observed := publicInstalled(*value.Observed)
                result.Observed = &observed
        }
        return result
}

// Project a page without leaking persistence types to host application services.
func publicDevices(values []ports.Device) []Device {
        result := make([]Device, 0, len(values))
        for _, value := range values {
                result = append(result, publicDevice(value))
        }
        return result
}

// Project a page without exposing operation authorization leases.
func publicOperations(values []ports.Operation) []Operation {
        result := make([]Operation, 0, len(values))
        for _, value := range values {
                result = append(result, publicOperation(value))
        }
        return result
}

// Classify caller input failures independently of transport-specific error codes.
func invalidArgument(message string) error { return fmt.Errorf("%w: %s", ErrInvalidArgument, message) }

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package kylok

import (
        "bytes"
        "context"
        "crypto/x509"
        "encoding/hex"
        "encoding/json"
        "errors"
        "net/http"
        "strings"
        "unicode/utf8"

        v1 "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/gen/kylok/v1"
        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// ErrNetworkSecretUnavailable hides provider diagnostics so credential values cannot leak through SDK errors.
var ErrNetworkSecretUnavailable = errors.New("network secret unavailable")

// NetworkPolicy owns managed saved Wi-Fi profiles and private CAs; an empty document releases managed resources safely.
type NetworkPolicy = ports.NetworkPolicy

// WifiProfile uses a scoped immutable SecretRef; no management read contains a delivered password.
type WifiProfile = ports.WifiProfile

// CACertificate carries a CA's DER bytes, without its private key.
type CACertificate = ports.CACertificate

// DesiredNetworkPolicy binds normalized references and CA ownership to a desired revision.
type DesiredNetworkPolicy = ports.DesiredNetworkPolicy

// NetworkReport separates authenticated route verification from association/setter results.
type NetworkReport = ports.NetworkReport

// NetworkStatus keeps desired and reported state distinct and excludes resolved network secrets.
type NetworkStatus = ports.NetworkStatus

// NetworkSecretProvider is a host-owned immutable scoped vault contract; rotate using a new reference version.
type NetworkSecretProvider = ports.NetworkSecretProvider

// SetNetworkPolicy replaces bounded managed networks/trust. Hosts supply immutable passphrase versions through NetworkSecrets.
// Android requires permissions, an independently verified saved recovery network and ownership readback before mutation.
// A failed migration/release retains the working route; a new request key creates the host's retry revision.
// Usage: desired, err := sdk.SetNetworkPolicy(ctx, scope, deviceID, "network-v2", kylok.NetworkPolicy{PrimaryID:"office", Profiles:[]kylok.WifiProfile{{ID:"office",SSID:"Office",Security:"WPA2_PSK",SecretRef:"office-key-v2"}}}).
func (s *SDK) SetNetworkPolicy(ctx context.Context, scope, device, key string, input NetworkPolicy) (DesiredNetworkPolicy, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return DesiredNetworkPolicy{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(device) || !idPattern.MatchString(key) {
                return DesiredNetworkPolicy{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        policy, err := ports.NormalizeNetworkPolicy(input)
        if err != nil {
                return DesiredNetworkPolicy{}, err
        }
        target, err := s.store.GetDevice(ctx, scope, device)
        if err != nil {
                return DesiredNetworkPolicy{}, err
        }
        if target.State == "REVOKED" {
                return DesiredNetworkPolicy{}, ErrRevoked
        }
        previous, err := s.store.GetNetworkRequest(ctx, scope, device, key)
        if err == nil {
                before, _ := json.Marshal(previous.Policy)
                after, _ := json.Marshal(policy)
                if !bytes.Equal(before, after) {
                        return DesiredNetworkPolicy{}, ErrConflict
                }
                return previous, nil
        }
        if !errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound) {
                return DesiredNetworkPolicy{}, err
        }
        // Reject obvious expired/future CA submissions before they can affect installed trust.
        for _, certificate := range policy.Certificates {
                cert, err := x509.ParseCertificate(certificate.DER)
                if err != nil || s.clock().Before(cert.NotBefore) || !s.clock().Before(cert.NotAfter) {
                        return DesiredNetworkPolicy{}, invalidArgument("CA must be valid at submission")
                }
        }
        for _, profile := range policy.Profiles {
                if profile.Security != "OPEN" {
                        if _, err = s.networkSecret(ctx, scope, profile); err != nil {
                                return DesiredNetworkPolicy{}, err
                        }
                }
        }
        return s.store.SetNetworkPolicy(ctx, ports.NetworkAssignment{ScopeID: scope, DeviceID: device, IdempotencyKey: key, Policy: policy})
}

// GetNetworkPolicy returns reference-only desired/report/compliance state in the authorized scope.
// Usage: status, err := sdk.GetNetworkPolicy(ctx, scope, deviceID).
func (s *SDK) GetNetworkPolicy(ctx context.Context, scope, device string) (NetworkStatus, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return NetworkStatus{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(device) {
                return NetworkStatus{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        return s.store.GetNetworkPolicy(ctx, scope, device)
}

// Resolve immutable scoped credentials; malformed or unavailable secrets fail without a fallback or value-bearing error.
func (s *SDK) networkSecret(ctx context.Context, scope string, profile WifiProfile) ([]byte, error) {
        if profile.Security == "OPEN" {
                return nil, nil
        }
        if s.networkSecrets == nil {
                return nil, invalidArgument("NetworkSecrets provider required for protected Wi-Fi")
        }
        value, err := s.networkSecrets.ReadNetworkSecret(ctx, scope, profile.SecretRef)
        if err != nil {
                if ctx.Err() != nil {
                        return nil, ctx.Err()
                }
                return nil, ErrNetworkSecretUnavailable
        }
        minimum := 1
        if profile.Security == "WPA2_PSK" {
                minimum = 8
        }
        _, hexError := hex.DecodeString(string(value))
        hexPSK := profile.Security == "WPA2_PSK" && len(value) == 64 && hexError == nil
        valid := len(value) >= minimum && len(value) <= 63 && utf8.Valid(value)
        for _, b := range value {
                if b < 32 || b == 127 {
                        valid = false
                }
                if profile.Security == "WPA2_PSK" && b > 126 {
                        valid = false
                }
        }
        if !valid && !hexPSK {
                return nil, invalidArgument("invalid Wi-Fi secret format")
        }
        return value, nil
}

// Resolve passwords only after authenticated store.Sync; its cached snapshot stores references, never credentials.
func (s *SDK) networkMessage(ctx context.Context, scope string, desired *DesiredNetworkPolicy) (*v1.DesiredNetworkPolicy, error) {
        if desired == nil {
                return nil, nil
        }
        policy := &v1.NetworkPolicy{PrimaryId: desired.Policy.PrimaryID}
        for _, profile := range desired.Policy.Profiles {
                passphrase, err := s.networkSecret(ctx, scope, profile)
                if err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                policy.Profiles = append(policy.Profiles, &v1.WifiProfile{Id: profile.ID, Ssid: profile.SSID, Security: profile.Security, Passphrase: passphrase, Hidden: profile.Hidden})
        }
        for _, certificate := range desired.Policy.Certificates {
                policy.Certificates = append(policy.Certificates, &v1.CACertificate{Id: certificate.ID, Der: certificate.DER})
        }
        return &v1.DesiredNetworkPolicy{Revision: desired.Revision, Policy: policy}, nil
}

// Authenticate a nonce-bound connectivity check without changing sync sequence or exposing other management routes.
func (s *SDK) networkProbe(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
        w.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        if r.Method != http.MethodGet {
                w.WriteHeader(http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)
                return
        }
        credential, ok := bearer(r.Header.Get("Authorization"))
        id, nonce := r.Header.Get("X-Kylok-Device-ID"), r.Header.Get("X-Kylok-Probe")
        if !ok || !devicePattern.MatchString(id) || len(nonce) != 43 || !devicePattern.MatchString(nonce) {
                w.WriteHeader(http.StatusUnauthorized)
                return
        }
        if err := s.checkOpen(r.Context()); err != nil {
                w.WriteHeader(http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
                return
        }
        if _, err := s.store.Authenticate(r.Context(), id, hash(credential)); err != nil {
                w.WriteHeader(http.StatusUnauthorized)
                return
        }
        w.Header().Set("X-Kylok-Probe", strings.Clone(nonce))
        w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package kylok

import (
        "context"
        "fmt"

        v1 "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/gen/kylok/v1"
        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

type Policy = ports.Policy
type LockTaskPolicy = ports.LockTaskPolicy
type PermissionRule = ports.PermissionRule
type RestrictionRule = ports.RestrictionRule
type DesiredPolicy = ports.DesiredPolicy
type PolicyStatus = ports.PolicyStatus
type ManagedConfiguration = ports.ManagedConfiguration
type ConfigurationBundle = ports.ConfigurationBundle
type ConfigurationEntry = ports.ConfigurationEntry
type ConfigurationValue = ports.ConfigurationValue

// SetPolicy replaces this device's policy. Required packages must already have
// scoped APK assignments; callers authorize scope/device before calling the SDK.
// An empty document releases only settings captured by Kylok's policy journal.
// Usage: desired, err := sdk.SetPolicy(ctx, scope, deviceID, requestKey, policy).
func (s *SDK) SetPolicy(ctx context.Context, scope, device, key string, p Policy) (DesiredPolicy, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return DesiredPolicy{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(device) || !idPattern.MatchString(key) {
                return DesiredPolicy{}, invalidArgument("scope/device/idempotency key required")
        }
        p, err := ports.NormalizePolicy(p)
        if err != nil {
                return DesiredPolicy{}, invalidArgument(err.Error())
        }
        want, err := s.store.SetPolicy(ctx, ports.PolicyAssignment{ScopeID: scope, DeviceID: device, IdempotencyKey: key, Policy: p})
        if err == nil {
                s.emit("policy_assigned", scope, device, want.Revision, fmt.Sprint(want.Revision))
        }
        return want, err
}

// GetPolicy reads scoped desired/reported policy and per-rule compliance without treating assignment as enforcement.
// Usage: status, err := sdk.GetPolicy(ctx, scope, deviceID).
func (s *SDK) GetPolicy(ctx context.Context, scope, device string) (PolicyStatus, error) {
        if err := s.checkOpen(ctx); err != nil {
                return PolicyStatus{}, err
        }
        if !idPattern.MatchString(scope) || !devicePattern.MatchString(device) {
                return PolicyStatus{}, invalidArgument("scope/device required")
        }
        return s.store.GetPolicy(ctx, scope, device)
}

// Preserve optional setting presence and typed permission states when encoding the policy document.
func policyMessage(p ports.Policy) *v1.Policy {
        m := &v1.Policy{RequiredPackages: p.RequiredPackages, CameraDisabled: p.CameraDisabled, ScreenCaptureDisabled: p.ScreenCaptureDisabled, MaximumTimeToLockMillis: p.MaximumTimeToLockMillis}
        for _, config := range p.ManagedConfigurations {
                m.ManagedConfigurations = append(m.ManagedConfigurations, &v1.ManagedConfiguration{PackageName: config.PackageName, Values: ports.ConfigurationMessage(config.Values)})
        }
        if p.LockTask != nil {
                m.LockTask = &v1.LockTaskPolicy{Packages: p.LockTask.Packages, KioskPackage: p.LockTask.KioskPackage}
        }
        for _, r := range p.Restrictions {
                m.Restrictions = append(m.Restrictions, &v1.RestrictionRule{Name: r.Name, Enabled: r.Enabled})
        }
        for _, r := range p.Permissions {
                m.Permissions = append(m.Permissions, &v1.PermissionRule{PackageName: r.PackageName, Permission: r.Permission, State: v1.PermissionState(v1.PermissionState_value["PERMISSION_STATE_"+r.State])})
        }
        return m
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package ports

import (
        "crypto/sha256"
        "encoding/hex"
        "encoding/json"
        "errors"
        "regexp"
        "sort"
        "unicode/utf8"

        v1 "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/gen/kylok/v1"
        "google.golang.org/protobuf/proto"
)

// ManagedConfiguration owns the complete restrictions bundle for a required managed app.
// Absence from a replacement policy releases only the bundle journaled by Kylok.
type ManagedConfiguration struct {
        PackageName string              `json:"package_name"`
        Values      ConfigurationBundle `json:"values"`
}

// ConfigurationBundle is a bounded typed Android Bundle. Normalization sorts keys; array order remains significant.
type ConfigurationBundle struct {
        Entries []ConfigurationEntry `json:"entries"`
}

// ConfigurationEntry assigns one unique application-defined key to exactly one typed value.
type ConfigurationEntry struct {
        Key   string             `json:"key"`
        Value ConfigurationValue `json:"value"`
}

// ConfigurationValue selects exactly one nonnil pointer. Empty strings/arrays/bundles are supported values.
// Boolean/int/string pointers preserve explicit zero values and prevent type inference from generic JSON.
type ConfigurationValue struct {
        Boolean *bool                  `json:"boolean,omitempty"`
        Integer *int32                 `json:"integer,omitempty"`
        String  *string                `json:"string,omitempty"`
        Strings *[]string              `json:"strings,omitempty"`
        Bundle  *ConfigurationBundle   `json:"bundle,omitempty"`
        Bundles *[]ConfigurationBundle `json:"bundles,omitempty"`
}

// MaximumPolicyResults covers 64 required apps, permissions and configurations, four restrictions, three scalars, kiosk and cleanup.
const MaximumPolicyResults = 201

const maximumConfigurationBytes = 65536
const maximumConfigurationEntries = 256
const maximumConfigurationDepth = 4

var configurationKey = regexp.MustCompile(`^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,127}$`)

// Validate recursive value types/limits, then canonicalize unordered keys for idempotency and cross-platform digests.
func normalizeBundle(bundle *ConfigurationBundle, depth int, nodes *int) error {
        if bundle.Entries == nil {
                bundle.Entries = []ConfigurationEntry{}
        }
        if depth > maximumConfigurationDepth || len(bundle.Entries) > 64 {
                return errors.New("configuration nesting/key limit exceeded")
        }
        sort.Slice(bundle.Entries, func(i, j int) bool { return bundle.Entries[i].Key < bundle.Entries[j].Key })
        for i := range bundle.Entries {
                entry := &bundle.Entries[i]
                *nodes += 1
                if *nodes > maximumConfigurationEntries || !configurationKey.MatchString(entry.Key) || i > 0 && bundle.Entries[i-1].Key == entry.Key {
                        return errors.New("invalid/duplicate configuration key or entry limit exceeded")
                }
                value := &entry.Value
                types := 0
                if value.Boolean != nil {
                        types++
                }
                if value.Integer != nil {
                        types++
                }
                validString := func(value string) bool { return len(value) <= 4096 && utf8.ValidString(value) }
                if value.String != nil {
                        types++
                        if !validString(*value.String) {
                                return errors.New("invalid/oversize configuration string")
                        }
                }
                if value.Strings != nil {
                        if *value.Strings == nil {
                                *value.Strings = []string{}
                        }
                        types++
                        if len(*value.Strings) > 64 {
                                return errors.New("configuration array limit exceeded")
                        }
                        for _, item := range *value.Strings {
                                if !validString(item) {
                                        return errors.New("invalid configuration array string")
                                }
                        }
                }
                if value.Bundle != nil {
                        types++
                        if err := normalizeBundle(value.Bundle, depth+1, nodes); err != nil {
                                return err
                        }
                }
                if value.Bundles != nil {
                        if *value.Bundles == nil {
                                *value.Bundles = []ConfigurationBundle{}
                        }
                        types++
                        if len(*value.Bundles) > 64 {
                                return errors.New("configuration bundle-array limit exceeded")
                        }
                        for i := range *value.Bundles {
                                if err := normalizeBundle(&(*value.Bundles)[i], depth+1, nodes); err != nil {
                                        return err
                                }
                        }
                }
                if types != 1 {
                        return errors.New("configuration value must select exactly one type")
                }
        }
        return nil
}

// Normalize configuration documents within the policy's verified required-app boundary.
func normalizeConfigurations(policy *Policy) error {
        if len(policy.ManagedConfigurations) > 64 {
                return errors.New("at most 64 managed configurations")
        }
        sort.Slice(policy.ManagedConfigurations, func(i, j int) bool {
                return policy.ManagedConfigurations[i].PackageName < policy.ManagedConfigurations[j].PackageName
        })
        nodes := 0
        for i := range policy.ManagedConfigurations {
                config := &policy.ManagedConfigurations[i]
                if !packagePattern.MatchString(config.PackageName) || len(config.PackageName) > 256 || i > 0 && policy.ManagedConfigurations[i-1].PackageName == config.PackageName {
                        return errors.New("invalid/duplicate configuration package")
                }
                assigned := false
                for _, name := range policy.RequiredPackages {
                        if name == config.PackageName {
                                assigned = true
                        }
                }
                if !assigned {
                        return errors.New("configuration target must be a required package")
                }
                if err := normalizeBundle(&config.Values, 1, &nodes); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        data, err := json.Marshal(policy.ManagedConfigurations)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if len(data) > maximumConfigurationBytes {
                return errors.New("managed configuration payload exceeds 64 KiB")
        }
        return nil
}

// ConfigurationMessage preserves explicit scalar values and typed arrays at the wire boundary.
// The input must be normalized with NormalizePolicy before hashing or transmission.
// Usage: wire := ports.ConfigurationMessage(normalized.ManagedConfigurations[0].Values).
func ConfigurationMessage(bundle ConfigurationBundle) *v1.ConfigurationBundle {
        result := &v1.ConfigurationBundle{}
        for _, entry := range bundle.Entries {
                value := &v1.ConfigurationValue{}
                switch {
                case entry.Value.Boolean != nil:
                        value.Value = &v1.ConfigurationValue_BooleanValue{BooleanValue: *entry.Value.Boolean}
                case entry.Value.Integer != nil:
                        value.Value = &v1.ConfigurationValue_IntegerValue{IntegerValue: *entry.Value.Integer}
                case entry.Value.String != nil:
                        value.Value = &v1.ConfigurationValue_StringValue{StringValue: *entry.Value.String}
                case entry.Value.Strings != nil:
                        value.Value = &v1.ConfigurationValue_StringsValue{StringsValue: &v1.ConfigurationStrings{Values: *entry.Value.Strings}}
                case entry.Value.Bundle != nil:
                        value.Value = &v1.ConfigurationValue_BundleValue{BundleValue: ConfigurationMessage(*entry.Value.Bundle)}
                case entry.Value.Bundles != nil:
                        list := &v1.ConfigurationBundles{}
                        for _, item := range *entry.Value.Bundles {
                                list.Values = append(list.Values, ConfigurationMessage(item))
                        }
                        value.Value = &v1.ConfigurationValue_BundlesValue{BundlesValue: list}
                }
                result.Entries = append(result.Entries, &v1.ConfigurationEntry{Key: entry.Key, Value: value})
        }
        return result
}

// ConfigurationDigest reports only a digest of normalized typed content, never configuration values.
// A matching DPC readback is separate from acknowledgement that an application applied the configuration.
// Usage: expected := ports.ConfigurationDigest(normalized.ManagedConfigurations[0].Values).
func ConfigurationDigest(bundle ConfigurationBundle) string {
        data, _ := proto.MarshalOptions{Deterministic: true}.Marshal(ConfigurationMessage(bundle))
        sum := sha256.Sum256(data)
        return "sha256:" + hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package ports

import (
        "context"
        "crypto/x509"
        "encoding/asn1"
        "encoding/json"
        "slices"
        "sort"
        "strings"
        "unicode/utf8"
)

// WifiProfile describes a managed saved network. Secrets are immutable host references, never passwords.
// Security is OPEN, WPA2_PSK or WPA3_SAE. SSID is literal UTF-8, limited to 32 bytes.
type WifiProfile struct {
        ID        string `json:"id"`
        SSID      string `json:"ssid"`
        Security  string `json:"security"`
        SecretRef string `json:"secret_ref,omitempty"`
        Hidden    bool   `json:"hidden"`
}

// CACertificate is one DER-encoded CA, without a private key. ID is an opaque ownership label.
type CACertificate struct {
        ID  string `json:"id"`
        DER []byte `json:"der"`
}

// NetworkPolicy is a full replacement of at most eight Wi-Fi profiles and eight private CAs.
// PrimaryID chooses the network requiring authenticated management connectivity. An empty document releases ownership.
// The Android agent preserves an independent saved recovery route before applying or releasing managed settings.
type NetworkPolicy struct {
        PrimaryID    string          `json:"primary_id"`
        Profiles     []WifiProfile   `json:"profiles"`
        Certificates []CACertificate `json:"certificates"`
}

// DesiredNetworkPolicy binds the normalized document to a shared device desired revision.
type DesiredNetworkPolicy struct {
        Revision uint64        `json:"revision"`
        Policy   NetworkPolicy `json:"policy"`
}

// NetworkReport distinguishes readback and authenticated route verification from setters and Wi-Fi association.
// APPLIED requires primary/recovery connectivity and completion of only the managed-resource cleanup.
type NetworkReport struct {
        Revision             uint64 `json:"revision"`
        State                string `json:"state"`
        Reason               string `json:"reason"`
        ActiveProfileID      string `json:"active_profile_id"`
        ConnectivityVerified bool   `json:"connectivity_verified"`
        CleanupComplete      bool   `json:"cleanup_complete"`
}

// NetworkStatus contains no delivered passwords. Reported state remains separate from current desired compliance.
type NetworkStatus struct {
        Desired  *DesiredNetworkPolicy `json:"desired,omitempty"`
        Reported *NetworkReport        `json:"reported,omitempty"`
        State    string                `json:"state"`
        Reason   string                `json:"reason"`
}

// NetworkAssignment atomically binds a complete normalized replacement to a scoped device/request key.
type NetworkAssignment struct {
        ScopeID, DeviceID, IdempotencyKey string
        Policy                            NetworkPolicy
}

// NetworkSecretProvider resolves immutable versioned references only within an explicitly authorized scope.
// References must retain identical bytes across retries/restarts. Rotate with a new reference and desired revision.
// The host owns persistence, encryption, access control and lifetime; failures never permit empty-secret fallback.
// Returned bytes belong to the caller. Do not log secret values or place them in public snapshots/events.
type NetworkSecretProvider interface {
        // ReadNetworkSecret returns the passphrase bound to an immutable scoped reference; nil/error fails closed.
        // Usage: secret, err := vault.ReadNetworkSecret(ctx, scope, "wifi-key-version-2").
        ReadNetworkSecret(context.Context, string, string) ([]byte, error)
}

// NetworkStore shares policy/secret-reference state and report invalidations with the serialized device store.
type NetworkStore interface {
        // GetNetworkRequest reads the original scoped replacement receipt without resolving secret references.
        // Usage: desired, err := store.GetNetworkRequest(ctx, scope, deviceID, requestKey).
        GetNetworkRequest(context.Context, string, string, string) (DesiredNetworkPolicy, error)
        // SetNetworkPolicy binds a normalized document/key, advances desired revision once and records a device change.
        // Usage: desired, err := store.SetNetworkPolicy(ctx, assignment).
        SetNetworkPolicy(context.Context, NetworkAssignment) (DesiredNetworkPolicy, error)
        // GetNetworkPolicy reads consistent desired/reported/compliance state in exactly one authorized scope.
        // Usage: status, err := store.GetNetworkPolicy(ctx, scope, deviceID).
        GetNetworkPolicy(context.Context, string, string) (NetworkStatus, error)
}

// Bound profile count and certificate allocation independently of transport's aggregate message limit.
const maximumNetworkEntries = 8
const maximumNetworkDocumentBytes = 200 * 1024
const maximumNetworkCertificateBytes = 16 * 1024

// NormalizeNetworkPolicy detaches, validates and sorts a bounded replacement before idempotency comparison.
// Certificate validity is checked again at delivery/application time; certificates must be complete CA DER objects.
// Usage: policy, err := ports.NormalizeNetworkPolicy(input).
func NormalizeNetworkPolicy(input NetworkPolicy) (NetworkPolicy, error) {
        for _, profile := range input.Profiles {
                if !utf8.ValidString(profile.SSID) {
                        return NetworkPolicy{}, ErrInvalidArgument
                }
        }
        data, err := json.Marshal(input)
        if err != nil || len(data) > maximumNetworkDocumentBytes {
                return NetworkPolicy{}, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        var p NetworkPolicy
        if err = json.Unmarshal(data, &p); err != nil {
                return p, err
        }
        if len(p.Profiles) > maximumNetworkEntries || len(p.Certificates) > maximumNetworkEntries {
                return p, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        if p.Profiles == nil {
                p.Profiles = []WifiProfile{}
        }
        if p.Certificates == nil {
                p.Certificates = []CACertificate{}
        }
        sort.Slice(p.Profiles, func(i, j int) bool { return p.Profiles[i].ID < p.Profiles[j].ID })
        sort.Slice(p.Certificates, func(i, j int) bool { return p.Certificates[i].ID < p.Certificates[j].ID })
        seenSSID := map[string]bool{}
        primary := false
        for i, profile := range p.Profiles {
                if !configurationKey.MatchString(profile.ID) || i > 0 && p.Profiles[i-1].ID == profile.ID || len(profile.SSID) < 1 || len(profile.SSID) > 32 || !utf8.ValidString(profile.SSID) || strings.ContainsRune(profile.SSID, 0) || seenSSID[profile.SSID] {
                        return p, ErrInvalidArgument
                }
                if !slices.Contains([]string{"OPEN", "WPA2_PSK", "WPA3_SAE"}, profile.Security) || profile.Security == "OPEN" && profile.SecretRef != "" || profile.Security != "OPEN" && !configurationKey.MatchString(profile.SecretRef) {
                        return p, ErrInvalidArgument
                }
                seenSSID[profile.SSID] = true
                primary = primary || profile.ID == p.PrimaryID
        }
        if len(p.Profiles) > 0 && !primary || len(p.Profiles) == 0 && p.PrimaryID != "" {
                return p, ErrInvalidArgument
        }
        seenCA := map[string]bool{}
        for i, certificate := range p.Certificates {
                if !configurationKey.MatchString(certificate.ID) || i > 0 && p.Certificates[i-1].ID == certificate.ID || len(certificate.DER) > maximumNetworkCertificateBytes {
                        return p, ErrInvalidArgument
                }
                cert, err := x509.ParseCertificate(certificate.DER)
                if err != nil || !cert.IsCA || !cert.BasicConstraintsValid || seenCA[string(certificate.DER)] {
                        return p, ErrInvalidArgument
                }
                // X.509 Key Usage is optional; an explicitly present extension must authorize CA signing.
                for _, extension := range cert.Extensions {
                        if extension.Id.Equal(asn1.ObjectIdentifier{2, 5, 29, 15}) && cert.KeyUsage&x509.KeyUsageCertSign == 0 {
                                return p, ErrInvalidArgument
                        }
                }
                seenCA[string(certificate.DER)] = true
        }
        return p, nil
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package ports

import (
        "encoding/json"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "regexp"
        "slices"
        "sort"
        "strconv"
        "strings"
)

type Policy struct {
        ManagedConfigurations   []ManagedConfiguration `json:"managed_configurations,omitempty"`
        RequiredPackages        []string               `json:"required_packages,omitempty"`
        LockTask                *LockTaskPolicy        `json:"lock_task,omitempty"`
        CameraDisabled          *bool                  `json:"camera_disabled,omitempty"`
        ScreenCaptureDisabled   *bool                  `json:"screen_capture_disabled,omitempty"`
        Restrictions            []RestrictionRule      `json:"restrictions,omitempty"`
        Permissions             []PermissionRule       `json:"permissions,omitempty"`
        MaximumTimeToLockMillis *int64                 `json:"maximum_time_to_lock_millis,omitempty"`
}
type LockTaskPolicy struct {
        Packages     []string `json:"packages"`
        KioskPackage string   `json:"kiosk_package"`
}
type RestrictionRule struct {
        Name    string `json:"name"`
        Enabled bool   `json:"enabled"`
}
type PermissionRule struct {
        PackageName string `json:"package_name"`
        Permission  string `json:"permission"`
        State       string `json:"state"`
}
type DesiredPolicy struct {
        Revision uint64 `json:"revision"`
        Policy   Policy `json:"policy"`
}
type PolicyAssignment struct {
        ScopeID, DeviceID, IdempotencyKey string
        Policy                            Policy
}
type PolicyResult struct {
        RuleID        string `json:"rule_id"`
        State         string `json:"state"`
        Reason        string `json:"reason"`
        ObservedValue string `json:"observed_value"`
}
type PolicyReport struct {
        Revision         uint64         `json:"revision"`
        SnapshotRevision uint64         `json:"snapshot_revision"`
        Results          []PolicyResult `json:"results"`
}
type PolicyStatus struct {
        Desired    *DesiredPolicy `json:"desired,omitempty"`
        Reported   *PolicyReport  `json:"reported,omitempty"`
        Compliance []PolicyResult `json:"compliance"`
}

var packagePattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*(\.[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*)+$`)
var restrictions = []string{"no_add_user", "no_debugging_features", "no_modify_accounts", "no_uninstall_apps"}

// NormalizePolicy validates and copies the document, sorting unordered sets for
// stable idempotency. A setting's absence differs from an explicit false/zero.
// Usage: normalized, err := ports.NormalizePolicy(policy).
func NormalizePolicy(input Policy) (Policy, error) {
        data, err := json.Marshal(input)
        if err != nil {
                return Policy{}, err
        }
        var p Policy
        if err = json.Unmarshal(data, &p); err != nil {
                return p, err
        }
        validPackage := func(name string) bool { return len(name) <= 256 && packagePattern.MatchString(name) }
        validateSet := func(values []string) error {
                if len(values) > 64 {
                        return errors.New("at most 64 packages per policy set")
                }
                sort.Strings(values)
                for i, v := range values {
                        if !validPackage(v) || i > 0 && values[i-1] == v {
                                return errors.New("invalid or duplicate package")
                        }
                }
                return nil
        }
        if err = validateSet(p.RequiredPackages); err != nil {
                return p, err
        }
        if p.LockTask != nil {
                if p.LockTask.Packages == nil {
                        p.LockTask.Packages = []string{}
                }
                if err = validateSet(p.LockTask.Packages); err != nil {
                        return p, err
                }
                for _, name := range p.LockTask.Packages {
                        if !slices.Contains(p.RequiredPackages, name) {
                                return p, errors.New("Lock Task packages must be required packages")
                        }
                }
                if p.LockTask.KioskPackage != "" && !slices.Contains(p.LockTask.Packages, p.LockTask.KioskPackage) {
                        return p, errors.New("kiosk package must be allowlisted")
                }
        }
        if len(p.Restrictions) > len(restrictions) || len(p.Permissions) > 64 {
                return p, errors.New("policy rule limit exceeded")
        }
        sort.Slice(p.Restrictions, func(i, j int) bool { return p.Restrictions[i].Name < p.Restrictions[j].Name })
        for i, r := range p.Restrictions {
                if !slices.Contains(restrictions, r.Name) || i > 0 && p.Restrictions[i-1].Name == r.Name {
                        return p, errors.New("unsupported or duplicate restriction")
                }
        }
        sort.Slice(p.Permissions, func(i, j int) bool {
                a, b := p.Permissions[i], p.Permissions[j]
                return a.PackageName+":"+a.Permission < b.PackageName+":"+b.Permission
        })
        for i, r := range p.Permissions {
                if !validPackage(r.PackageName) || !slices.Contains(p.RequiredPackages, r.PackageName) || !strings.HasPrefix(r.Permission, "android.permission.") || len(r.Permission) > 256 || !packagePattern.MatchString(r.Permission) || (r.State != "DEFAULT" && r.State != "GRANTED" && r.State != "DENIED") {
                        return p, errors.New("valid required package, Android permission and DEFAULT/GRANTED/DENIED required")
                }
                if i > 0 && p.Permissions[i-1].PackageName == r.PackageName && p.Permissions[i-1].Permission == r.Permission {
                        return p, errors.New("duplicate permission rule")
                }
        }
        if p.MaximumTimeToLockMillis != nil && (*p.MaximumTimeToLockMillis < 0 || *p.MaximumTimeToLockMillis > 86400000) {
                return p, errors.New("maximum time to lock must be 0–86400000 ms")
        }
        if err = normalizeConfigurations(&p); err != nil {
                return p, err
        }
        return p, nil
}

// PolicyExpectations defines canonical values shared with the Android observer.
// Required apps bind package/version/signers, so changing an APK invalidates an
// earlier policy observation even if the policy document itself is unchanged.
// Usage: values := ports.PolicyExpectations(policy, desiredApps).
func PolicyExpectations(p Policy, apps []DesiredApplication) map[string]string {
        values := map[string]string{"cleanup": "complete"}
        for _, config := range p.ManagedConfigurations {
                values["configuration:"+config.PackageName] = ConfigurationDigest(config.Values)
        }
        for _, name := range p.RequiredPackages {
                values["required:"+name] = "missing_assignment"
                for _, app := range apps {
                        if app.Artifact.PackageName == name {
                                certs := slices.Clone(app.Artifact.SigningCertificates)
                                sort.Strings(certs)
                                values["required:"+name] = fmt.Sprintf("%s@%d:%s", name, app.Artifact.VersionCode, strings.Join(certs, ","))
                        }
                }
        }
        if p.CameraDisabled != nil {
                values["camera"] = strconv.FormatBool(*p.CameraDisabled)
        }
        if p.ScreenCaptureDisabled != nil {
                values["screen_capture"] = strconv.FormatBool(*p.ScreenCaptureDisabled)
        }
        if p.MaximumTimeToLockMillis != nil {
                values["maximum_time_to_lock"] = strconv.FormatInt(*p.MaximumTimeToLockMillis, 10)
        }
        for _, r := range p.Restrictions {
                values["restriction:"+r.Name] = strconv.FormatBool(r.Enabled)
        }
        for _, r := range p.Permissions {
                values["permission:"+r.PackageName+":"+r.Permission] = r.State
        }
        if p.LockTask != nil {
                // A fixed field order and sorted packages keep Go/Kotlin values identical.
                list := slices.Clone(p.LockTask.Packages)
                if list == nil {
                        list = []string{}
                }
                sort.Strings(list)
                encoded, _ := json.Marshal(struct {
                        Packages []string `json:"packages"`
                        Kiosk    string   `json:"kiosk_package"`
                }{list, p.LockTask.KioskPackage})
                values["lock_task"] = string(encoded)
        }
        return values
}

// PolicyCapability identifies the agent capability required to implement one canonical rule ID.
// Usage: capability := ports.PolicyCapability("camera").
func PolicyCapability(key string) string {
        switch {
        case strings.HasPrefix(key, "configuration:"):
                return "policy.managed_configuration"
        case strings.HasPrefix(key, "required:"):
                return "install_apk"
        case strings.HasPrefix(key, "permission:"):
                return "policy.permissions"
        case strings.HasPrefix(key, "restriction:"):
                return "policy.restrictions"
        case key == "lock_task":
                return "policy.kiosk"
        case key == "camera":
                return "policy.camera"
        case key == "screen_capture":
                return "policy.screen_capture"
        case key == "maximum_time_to_lock":
                return "policy.maximum_time_to_lock"
        default:
                return "durable_sync"
        }
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package portstest

import (
        "context"
        "crypto/sha256"
        "errors"
        "reflect"
        "testing"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// Changes verifies transactional invalidations, scoped replay, immutable snapshots and explicit expiry/eviction.
// Usage: portstest.Changes(t, factory); run unchanged for both durable SQL adapters.
func Changes(t *testing.T, factory func(*testing.T) ports.StateStore) {
        t.Helper()
        t.Run("change-feed-rollback-snapshot-replay", func(t *testing.T) {
                ctx := context.Background()
                s := factory(t)
                now := time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Millisecond)
                token := sha256.Sum256([]byte("feed-token"))
                key := sha256.Sum256([]byte("feed-key"))
                credential := sha256.Sum256([]byte("feed-credential"))
                if err := s.CreateEnrollment(ctx, ports.Enrollment{ScopeID: "scope", TokenHash: token[:], ExpiresAt: now.Add(time.Hour)}); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                attempt := ports.Attempt{TokenHash: token[:], ID: "attempt", KeyHash: key[:], Device: ports.Device{ID: "device", ScopeID: "scope"}, CredentialHash: credential[:], Receipt: []byte("encrypted"), Now: now, RecoverUntil: now.Add(time.Hour)}
                if _, err := s.Consume(ctx, attempt); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                artifact := ports.Artifact{ScopeID: "scope", SHA256: "digest", PackageName: "app.example", VersionCode: 1, SigningCertificates: []string{"signer"}}
                if err := s.RegisterArtifact(ctx, artifact); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                first, err := s.ListChanges(ctx, "scope", 0, 100)
                if err != nil || len(first.Items) != 2 || first.Next != 2 {
                        t.Fatal(first, err)
                }
                replay, err := s.ListChanges(ctx, "scope", 0, 100)
                if err != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(first, replay) {
                        t.Fatal("replay changed", first, replay, err)
                }
                // Duplicate creation/delivery does not generate another logical change.
                s.Consume(ctx, attempt)
                s.RegisterArtifact(ctx, artifact)
                duplicate, err := s.ListChanges(ctx, "scope", first.Next, 100)
                if err != nil || len(duplicate.Items) != 0 {
                        t.Fatal(duplicate, err)
                }
                foreign, err := s.ListChanges(ctx, "other", 0, 100)
                if err != nil || len(foreign.Items) != 0 || foreign.Head != 0 {
                        t.Fatal("scope leak", foreign, err)
                }
                snapshot, err := s.ReadProjection(ctx, "scope", "", 0, 1, now)
                if err != nil || !snapshot.More || len(snapshot.Items) != 1 || snapshot.Items[0].Device == nil || snapshot.Watermark != 2 {
                        t.Fatal(snapshot, err)
                }
                immutable, err := s.ReadProjection(ctx, "scope", snapshot.SnapshotID, 0, 1, now)
                if err != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(immutable, snapshot) {
                        t.Fatal("snapshot replay", immutable, err)
                }
                if _, err = s.ReadProjection(ctx, "other", snapshot.SnapshotID, 1, 1, now); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrCursorExpired) {
                        t.Fatal("foreign scan readable", err)
                }
                assignment := ports.Assignment{ScopeID: "scope", DeviceID: "device", ArtifactSHA256: "digest", IdempotencyKey: "assign", OperationID: "operation", Now: now, Deadline: now.Add(time.Hour)}
                op, err := s.Assign(ctx, assignment)
                if err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                input := ports.SyncInput{DeviceID: "device", CredentialHash: credential[:], Sequence: 1, Now: now, Inventory: ports.Inventory{DeviceOwner: true, Capabilities: []string{"install_apk"}}}
                leased, err := s.Sync(ctx, input)
                if err != nil || len(leased.Operations) != 1 {
                        t.Fatal(leased, err)
                }
                before, err := s.ListChanges(ctx, "scope", 0, 100)
                if err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                report := ports.OperationReport{ID: "report", OperationID: op.ID, LeaseID: leased.Operations[0].LeaseID, Sequence: 1, State: "SUCCEEDED", Observed: &ports.InstalledApplication{PackageName: "app.example", VersionCode: 1, SigningCertificates: []string{"signer"}}}
                conflict := report
                conflict.ErrorCode = "different"
                input.Sequence = 2
                input.ReportedRevision = 1
                input.Reports = []ports.OperationReport{report, conflict}
                if _, err = s.Sync(ctx, input); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConflict) {
                        t.Fatal("expected transactional rollback", err)
                }
                after, err := s.ListChanges(ctx, "scope", 0, 100)
                if err != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(before, after) {
                        t.Fatal("rolled-back effect entered feed", before, after, err)
                }
                current, err := s.GetDeviceState(ctx, "scope", "device")
                if err != nil || current.Operations[0].State != "RUNNING" || len(current.Desired) != 1 {
                        t.Fatal(current, err)
                }
                input.Reports = []ports.OperationReport{report}
                if _, err = s.Sync(ctx, input); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                committed, err := s.ListChanges(ctx, "scope", after.Next, 100)
                if err != nil || len(committed.Items) != 1 || committed.Items[0].Kind != "device_changed" {
                        t.Fatal(committed, err)
                }
                s.Sync(ctx, input)
                again, err := s.ListChanges(ctx, "scope", committed.Next, 100)
                if err != nil || len(again.Items) != 0 {
                        t.Fatal("sync replay entered log", again, err)
                }
                // Continue the saved snapshot after mutations: old device state and catalog remain consistent with watermark.
                old, err := s.ReadProjection(ctx, "scope", snapshot.SnapshotID, 0, 100, now)
                if err != nil || len(old.Items) != 2 || old.Items[0].Device.Device.State != "ENROLLED" || len(old.Items[0].Device.Desired) != 0 || old.Watermark != snapshot.Watermark {
                        t.Fatal("mutable snapshot", old, err)
                }
                gap, err := s.ListChanges(ctx, "scope", snapshot.Watermark, 100)
                if err != nil || len(gap.Items) < 3 {
                        t.Fatal("snapshot handoff missed changes", gap, err)
                }
                // Unreferenced catalog deletion must be a replayable tombstone.
                unreferenced := artifact
                unreferenced.SHA256 = "unreferenced"
                if err = s.RegisterArtifact(ctx, unreferenced); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if err = s.RemoveArtifact(ctx, "scope", unreferenced.SHA256); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                tail, err := s.ListChanges(ctx, "scope", gap.Next, 100)
                if err != nil || len(tail.Items) != 2 || tail.Items[1].Kind != "application_removed" {
                        t.Fatal(tail, err)
                }
                if _, err = s.ListChanges(ctx, "scope", tail.Head+1, 1); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrInvalidArgument) {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if _, err = s.ReadProjection(ctx, "scope", snapshot.SnapshotID, 0, 1, now.Add(ports.SnapshotLifetime)); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrCursorExpired) {
                        t.Fatal("expired snapshot accepted", err)
                }
                for range ports.SnapshotLimit {
                        if _, err = s.ReadProjection(ctx, "scope", "", 0, 1, now); err != nil {
                                t.Fatal(err)
                        }
                }
                if _, err = s.ReadProjection(ctx, "scope", snapshot.SnapshotID, 0, 1, now); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrCursorExpired) {
                        t.Fatal("evicted snapshot accepted", err)
                }
                cancelled, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
                cancel()
                if _, err = s.ListChanges(cancelled, "scope", 0, 1); !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
        })
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package portstest

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "testing"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// Commands verifies capability gates, stable delivery, report binding, honest uncertainty and scope isolation.
// Usage: portstest.Commands(t, factory).
func Commands(t *testing.T, factory func(*testing.T) ports.StateStore) {
        t.Helper()
        t.Run("commands-capability-observation-and-replay", func(t *testing.T) {
                ctx := context.Background()
                s := factory(t)
                now, credential := fleetFixture(t, s)
                a := ports.CommandAssignment{ScopeID: "scope", DeviceID: "device-a", Kind: "REBOOT", IdempotencyKey: "reboot", ID: "command-a", Now: now, Deadline: now.Add(time.Hour)}
                if _, err := s.SubmitCommand(ctx, a); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrUnsupported) {
                        t.Fatal("missing capability accepted", err)
                }
                input := ports.SyncInput{DeviceID: "device-a", CredentialHash: credential, Sequence: 1, Now: now, Inventory: ports.Inventory{DeviceOwner: true, Capabilities: []string{"command.reboot", "command.lock"}}}
                if _, err := s.Sync(ctx, input); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                command, err := s.SubmitCommand(ctx, a)
                if err != nil || command.State != "QUEUED" {
                        t.Fatal(command, err)
                }
                a.ID = "unused"
                a.Deadline = a.Deadline.Add(time.Hour)
                if retry, err := s.SubmitCommand(ctx, a); err != nil || retry.ID != command.ID || !retry.Deadline.Equal(command.Deadline) {
                        t.Fatal("request duplicate", retry, err)
                }
                conflict := a
                conflict.Kind = "LOCK"
                if _, err := s.SubmitCommand(ctx, conflict); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConflict) {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                input.Sequence++
                dispatched, err := s.Sync(ctx, input)
                if err != nil || len(dispatched.Commands) != 1 || dispatched.Commands[0].DispatchToken == "" {
                        t.Fatal(dispatched, err)
                }
                token := dispatched.Commands[0].DispatchToken
                input.Sequence++
                repeated, err := s.Sync(ctx, input)
                if err != nil || len(repeated.Commands) != 1 || repeated.Commands[0].DispatchToken != token {
                        t.Fatal("dispatch replaced", repeated, err)
                }
                report := ports.CommandReport{CommandID: command.ID, DispatchToken: token, State: "SUCCEEDED", Observation: &ports.CommandObservation{BootBefore: 7, BootAfter: 7}}
                input.Sequence++
                input.CommandReports = []ports.CommandReport{report}
                if _, err := s.Sync(ctx, input); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrInvalidArgument) {
                        t.Fatal("reconnection accepted as reboot", err)
                }
                report.Observation = &ports.CommandObservation{BootBefore: 7, BootAfter: 8}
                input.CommandReports = []ports.CommandReport{report}
                done, err := s.Sync(ctx, input)
                if err != nil || len(done.Commands) != 0 || len(done.CommandReceipts) != 1 {
                        t.Fatal(done, err)
                }
                original, err := s.GetCommand(ctx, "scope", "device-a", command.ID, now)
                if err != nil || original.State != "SUCCEEDED" || original.Observation.BootAfter != 8 {
                        t.Fatal(original, err)
                }
                input.Sequence++
                if again, err := s.Sync(ctx, input); err != nil || len(again.CommandReceipts) != 1 || len(again.Commands) != 0 {
                        t.Fatal(again, err)
                }
                changed := report
                changed.ErrorCode = "changed"
                input.Sequence++
                input.CommandReports = []ports.CommandReport{changed}
                if _, err := s.Sync(ctx, input); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConflict) {
                        t.Fatal("report rebound", err)
                }
                for _, query := range [][2]string{{"foreign", "device-a"}, {"scope", "device-b"}} {
                        if _, err := s.GetCommand(ctx, query[0], query[1], command.ID, now); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                                t.Fatal("command scope leak", err)
                        }
                        if _, err := s.CancelCommand(ctx, query[0], query[1], command.ID, now); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                                t.Fatal("foreign command cancellation", err)
                        }
                }
                state, err := s.GetDeviceState(ctx, "scope", "device-a")
                if err != nil || len(state.Commands) != 1 || state.Commands[0].State != "SUCCEEDED" {
                        t.Fatal("projection lost command", state, err)
                }
        })
        t.Run("commands-cancellation-deadlines-and-lock-readback", func(t *testing.T) {
                ctx := context.Background()
                s := factory(t)
                now, credential := fleetFixture(t, s)
                input := ports.SyncInput{DeviceID: "device-a", CredentialHash: credential, Sequence: 1, Now: now, Inventory: ports.Inventory{DeviceOwner: true, Capabilities: []string{"command.reboot", "command.lock"}}}
                if _, err := s.Sync(ctx, input); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                assignment := ports.CommandAssignment{ScopeID: "scope", DeviceID: "device-a", Kind: "LOCK", IdempotencyKey: "queued", ID: "queued", Now: now, Deadline: now.Add(time.Hour)}
                if _, err := s.SubmitCommand(ctx, assignment); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if cancelled, err := s.CancelCommand(ctx, "scope", "device-a", "queued", now); err != nil || cancelled.State != "CANCELLED" {
                        t.Fatal(cancelled, err)
                }
                assignment.ID = "lock"
                assignment.IdempotencyKey = "lock"
                if _, err := s.SubmitCommand(ctx, assignment); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                input.Sequence++
                active, err := s.Sync(ctx, input)
                if err != nil || len(active.Commands) != 1 || active.Commands[0].ID != "lock" {
                        t.Fatal(active, err)
                }
                if uncertain, err := s.CancelCommand(ctx, "scope", "device-a", "lock", now); err != nil || uncertain.State != "UNCERTAIN" {
                        t.Fatal(uncertain, err)
                }
                input.Sequence++
                input.CommandReports = []ports.CommandReport{{CommandID: "lock", DispatchToken: active.Commands[0].DispatchToken, State: "SUCCEEDED", Observation: &ports.CommandObservation{Locked: true, Secure: false}}}
                if _, err := s.Sync(ctx, input); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrInvalidArgument) {
                        t.Fatal("unsecured lock accepted", err)
                }
                input.CommandReports[0].Observation.Secure = true
                if observed, err := s.Sync(ctx, input); err != nil || len(observed.Commands) != 0 {
                        t.Fatal(observed, err)
                }
                if done, err := s.GetCommand(ctx, "scope", "device-a", "lock", now); err != nil || done.State != "SUCCEEDED" {
                        t.Fatal("late observation could not resolve uncertainty", done, err)
                }
                assignment.ID = "expiry"
                assignment.IdempotencyKey = "expiry"
                if _, err := s.SubmitCommand(ctx, assignment); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                input.Sequence++
                input.CommandReports = nil
                if active, err = s.Sync(ctx, input); err != nil || len(active.Commands) != 1 {
                        t.Fatal(active, err)
                }
                if expired, err := s.GetCommand(ctx, "scope", "device-a", "expiry", assignment.Deadline); err != nil || expired.State != "UNCERTAIN" {
                        t.Fatal("delivered deadline claimed failure", expired, err)
                }
                input.Sequence++
                input.Now = assignment.Deadline
                if stopped, err := s.Sync(ctx, input); err != nil || len(stopped.Commands) != 0 {
                        t.Fatal("expired command redispatched", stopped, err)
                }
                assignment.ID = "undispatched"
                assignment.IdempotencyKey = "undispatched"
                assignment.Now = input.Now
                assignment.Deadline = input.Now.Add(time.Hour)
                if _, err := s.SubmitCommand(ctx, assignment); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if expired, err := s.GetCommand(ctx, "scope", "device-a", "undispatched", assignment.Deadline); err != nil || expired.State != "FAILED" {
                        t.Fatal(expired, err)
                }
                history, err := s.ListCommands(ctx, "scope", "device-a", "", 100, assignment.Deadline)
                if err != nil || len(history) != 4 {
                        t.Fatal(history, err)
                }
        })
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package portstest

import (
        "context"
        "crypto/sha256"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "sync"
        "testing"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// Prepare independent scoped identities without depending on a facade or transport implementation.
func fleetFixture(t *testing.T, s ports.StateStore) (time.Time, []byte) {
        t.Helper()
        now := time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Millisecond)
        credential := sha256.Sum256([]byte("fleet credential"))
        key := sha256.Sum256([]byte("fleet key"))
        for _, target := range []struct{ scope, id string }{{"scope", "device-a"}, {"scope", "device-b"}, {"scope", "device-c"}, {"foreign", "device-foreign"}} {
                token := sha256.Sum256([]byte(target.id))
                if err := s.CreateEnrollment(context.Background(), ports.Enrollment{TokenHash: token[:], ScopeID: target.scope, ExpiresAt: now.Add(time.Hour)}); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if _, err := s.Consume(context.Background(), ports.Attempt{TokenHash: token[:], ID: target.id, KeyHash: key[:], Device: ports.Device{ID: target.id, ScopeID: target.scope}, CredentialHash: credential[:], Receipt: []byte("encrypted"), Now: now, RecoverUntil: now.Add(time.Hour)}); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
        }
        if err := s.RegisterArtifact(context.Background(), ports.Artifact{ScopeID: "scope", SHA256: "digest", PackageName: "io.test.app", VersionCode: 2, SigningCertificates: []string{"signer"}}); err != nil {
                t.Fatal(err)
        }
        return now, credential[:]
}

// Fleet verifies frozen membership, bounded/restartable fan-out, windows, cancellation, isolation and replay on every provider.
// Usage: portstest.Fleet(t, factory).
func Fleet(t *testing.T, factory func(*testing.T) ports.StateStore) {
        t.Helper()
        t.Run("fleet-frozen-membership-partial-results", func(t *testing.T) {
                ctx := context.Background()
                s := factory(t)
                now, _ := fleetFixture(t, s)
                group := ports.Group{ScopeID: "scope", ID: "lobby", DeviceIDs: []string{"device-b", "device-a"}}
                saved, err := s.ReplaceGroup(ctx, group, 0)
                if err != nil || saved.Revision != 1 || saved.DeviceIDs[0] != "device-a" {
                        t.Fatal(saved, err)
                }
                group.DeviceIDs[0] = "mutated"
                if again, err := s.GetGroup(ctx, "scope", "lobby"); err != nil || again.DeviceIDs[1] != "device-b" {
                        t.Fatal("aliased group", again, err)
                }
                group.DeviceIDs = []string{"device-a", "device-b"}
                if retry, err := s.ReplaceGroup(ctx, group, 0); err != nil || retry.Revision != 1 {
                        t.Fatal("group retry", retry, err)
                }
                foreign := group
                foreign.DeviceIDs = []string{"device-foreign"}
                if _, err := s.ReplaceGroup(ctx, foreign, 1); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                        t.Fatal("foreign group member", err)
                }
                request := ports.FleetRequest{ScopeID: "scope", ID: "batch", GroupID: "lobby", ArtifactSHA256: "digest", NotBefore: now.Add(time.Hour), Deadline: now.Add(2 * time.Hour)}
                batch, err := s.CreateFleetAssignment(ctx, request, now)
                if err != nil || len(batch.Items) != 2 || batch.GroupRevision != 1 || batch.Complete {
                        t.Fatal(batch, err)
                }
                if err = s.RemoveArtifact(ctx, "scope", "digest"); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrReferenced) {
                        t.Fatal("pending batch lost artifact", err)
                }
                group.DeviceIDs = []string{"device-c"}
                if _, err = s.ReplaceGroup(ctx, group, 1); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if _, err = s.ReplaceGroup(ctx, group, 0); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConflict) {
                        t.Fatal("lost group update", err)
                }
                if retry, err := s.CreateFleetAssignment(ctx, request, now); err != nil || len(retry.Items) != 2 || retry.GroupRevision != 1 {
                        t.Fatal("retry expanded membership", retry, err)
                }
                changed := request
                changed.Deadline = changed.Deadline.Add(time.Second)
                if _, err = s.CreateFleetAssignment(ctx, changed, now); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConflict) {
                        t.Fatal("window conflict", err)
                }
                first, err := s.AdvanceFleetAssignment(ctx, "scope", "batch", now, 1)
                if err != nil || first.Complete || first.Items[0].State != "ASSIGNED" || first.Items[1].State != "PENDING" {
                        t.Fatal(first, err)
                }
                if err := s.Revoke(ctx, "scope", "device-b"); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                done, err := s.AdvanceFleetAssignment(ctx, "scope", "batch", now, 1)
                if err != nil || !done.Complete || done.Items[0].OperationID != first.Items[0].OperationID || done.Items[1].ErrorCode != "DEVICE_REVOKED" {
                        t.Fatal(done, err)
                }
                if retry, err := s.AdvanceFleetAssignment(ctx, "scope", "batch", now, 100); err != nil || retry.Items[0].OperationID != first.Items[0].OperationID {
                        t.Fatal(retry, err)
                }
                for _, id := range []string{"device-b", "device-c"} {
                        ops, err := s.ListOperations(ctx, "scope", id, "", 100, now)
                        if err != nil || len(ops) != 0 {
                                t.Fatal("fanout reached wrong member", id, ops, err)
                        }
                }
                if _, err := s.GetFleetAssignment(ctx, "foreign", "batch"); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                        t.Fatal("batch scope leak", err)
                }
                if _, err := s.CancelFleetAssignment(ctx, "foreign", "batch", now); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                        t.Fatal("foreign cancellation", err)
                }
                if _, err := s.GetGroup(ctx, "foreign", "lobby"); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                        t.Fatal("group scope leak", err)
                }
                feed, err := s.ListChanges(ctx, "scope", 0, 100)
                if err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                kinds := map[string]bool{}
                for _, change := range feed.Items {
                        kinds[change.Kind] = true
                }
                if !kinds["group_changed"] || !kinds["fleet_changed"] {
                        t.Fatal("missing durable notifications", feed)
                }
                projection, err := s.ReadProjection(ctx, "scope", "", 0, 100, now)
                if err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                groups, batches := 0, 0
                for _, item := range projection.Items {
                        if item.Group != nil {
                                groups++
                                if item.Group.DeviceIDs[0] != "device-c" {
                                        t.Fatal(item)
                                }
                        }
                        if item.Fleet != nil {
                                batches++
                                if !item.Fleet.Complete {
                                        t.Fatal(item)
                                }
                        }
                }
                if groups != 1 || batches != 1 {
                        t.Fatal("projection omitted fleet state", projection)
                }
        })
        t.Run("fleet-windows-cancel-and-stale-delivery", func(t *testing.T) {
                ctx := context.Background()
                s := factory(t)
                now, credential := fleetFixture(t, s)
                request := ports.FleetRequest{ScopeID: "scope", ID: "window", DeviceIDs: []string{"device-a", "device-b"}, ArtifactSHA256: "digest", NotBefore: now.Add(time.Hour), Deadline: now.Add(2 * time.Hour)}
                if _, err := s.CreateFleetAssignment(ctx, request, now); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                batch, err := s.AdvanceFleetAssignment(ctx, "scope", "window", now, 1)
                if err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                opID := batch.Items[0].OperationID
                in := ports.SyncInput{DeviceID: "device-a", CredentialHash: credential, Sequence: 1, Now: now, Inventory: ports.Inventory{Capabilities: []string{"install_apk"}}}
                before, err := s.Sync(ctx, in)
                if err != nil || len(before.Operations) != 0 || before.Compliance[0].Reason != "SCHEDULED" {
                        t.Fatal(before, err)
                }
                in.Sequence++
                in.ReportedRevision = 1
                in.Now = request.NotBefore
                active, err := s.Sync(ctx, in)
                if err != nil || len(active.Operations) != 1 || active.Operations[0].Attempts != 1 {
                        t.Fatal(active, err)
                }
                leased := active.Operations[0]
                if _, err := s.AuthorizeDownload(ctx, "device-a", credential, opID, "digest", leased.LeaseID, now); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrUnauthorized) {
                        t.Fatal("clock rollback downloaded early", err)
                }
                cancelled, err := s.CancelFleetAssignment(ctx, "scope", "window", in.Now)
                if err != nil || !cancelled.Cancelled || !cancelled.Complete || cancelled.Items[1].State != "CANCELLED" {
                        t.Fatal(cancelled, err)
                }
                if _, err := s.AuthorizeDownload(ctx, "device-a", credential, opID, "digest", leased.LeaseID, in.Now); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrUnauthorized) {
                        t.Fatal("cancelled download", err)
                }
                // An exact lost-response replay remains immutable; cancellation arrives on the next sequence.
                replay, err := s.Sync(ctx, in)
                if err != nil || len(replay.Operations) != 1 || replay.Operations[0].LeaseID != leased.LeaseID {
                        t.Fatal(replay, err)
                }
                in.Sequence++
                in.RetainedOperationIDs = []string{opID}
                in.Reports = []ports.OperationReport{{ID: "late", OperationID: opID, LeaseID: leased.LeaseID, Sequence: 1, State: "SUCCEEDED", Observed: &ports.InstalledApplication{PackageName: "io.test.app", VersionCode: 2, SigningCertificates: []string{"signer"}}}}
                stopped, err := s.Sync(ctx, in)
                if err != nil || len(stopped.Operations) != 0 || len(stopped.StoppedOperationIDs) != 1 || stopped.StoppedOperationIDs[0] != opID {
                        t.Fatal(stopped, err)
                }
                op, err := s.GetOperation(ctx, "scope", "device-a", opID, in.Now)
                if err != nil || op.State != "CANCELLED" || op.Observed != nil {
                        t.Fatal("late report overwrote cancellation", op, err)
                }
                // Repeated inventory mismatch cannot silently resume cancelled desired intent.
                in.Sequence++
                in.Reports = nil
                in.Now = in.Now.Add(time.Minute)
                if next, err := s.Sync(ctx, in); err != nil || len(next.Operations) != 0 {
                        t.Fatal(next, err)
                }
                if retry, err := s.AdvanceFleetAssignment(ctx, "scope", "window", now, 100); err != nil || !retry.Cancelled {
                        t.Fatal(retry, err)
                }
        })
        t.Run("fleet-concurrent-workers-and-expiry", func(t *testing.T) {
                ctx := context.Background()
                s := factory(t)
