                now, _ := fleetFixture(t, s)
                request := ports.FleetRequest{ScopeID: "scope", ID: "concurrent", DeviceIDs: []string{"device-a", "device-b", "missing"}, ArtifactSHA256: "digest", NotBefore: now, Deadline: now.Add(time.Hour)}
                if _, err := s.CreateFleetAssignment(ctx, request, now); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                var wait sync.WaitGroup
                for i := 0; i < 8; i++ {
                        wait.Add(1)
                        go func() {
                                defer wait.Done()
                                if _, err := s.AdvanceFleetAssignment(ctx, "scope", "concurrent", now, 1); err != nil {
                                        t.Error(err)
                                }
                        }()
                }
                wait.Wait()
                batch, err := s.GetFleetAssignment(ctx, "scope", "concurrent")
                if err != nil || !batch.Complete || batch.Items[2].ErrorCode != "NOT_FOUND" {
                        t.Fatal(batch, err)
                }
                for _, id := range []string{"device-a", "device-b"} {
                        ops, err := s.ListOperations(ctx, "scope", id, "", 100, now)
                        if err != nil || len(ops) != 1 || ops[0].Revision != 1 {
                                t.Fatal("duplicate fanout", ops, err)
                        }
                }
                request.ID = "expired"
                request.DeviceIDs = []string{"device-c"}
                if _, err := s.CreateFleetAssignment(ctx, request, now); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                cancelledCtx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
                cancel()
                if _, err := s.AdvanceFleetAssignment(cancelledCtx, "scope", "expired", now, 1); !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
                        t.Fatal("ignored context", err)
                }
                if expired, err := s.AdvanceFleetAssignment(ctx, "scope", "expired", request.Deadline, 100); err != nil || !expired.Complete || expired.Items[0].ErrorCode != "DEADLINE_EXCEEDED" {
                        t.Fatal(expired, err)
                }
                if retry, err := s.CreateFleetAssignment(ctx, request, request.Deadline.Add(time.Hour)); err != nil || !retry.Complete {
                        t.Fatal("expired retry lost checkpoint", retry, err)
                }
                for _, count := range []int{0, 101} {
                        if _, err := s.AdvanceFleetAssignment(ctx, "scope", "concurrent", now, count); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrInvalidArgument) {
                                t.Fatal(fmt.Sprint(count), err)
                        }
                }
        })
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package portstest

import (
        "context"
        "crypto/sha256"
        "errors"
        "testing"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// Management verifies correlation binding, scoped point/filter reads, expiry and cancellation.
// Usage: portstest.Management(t, factory); run this unchanged for every state adapter.
func Management(t *testing.T, factory func(*testing.T) ports.StateStore) {
        t.Helper()
        t.Run("management-correlation-scope-query-expiry", func(t *testing.T) {
                ctx := context.Background()
                store := factory(t)
                now := time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Millisecond)
                if err := store.Check(ctx); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                token := sha256.Sum256([]byte("management-token"))
                key := sha256.Sum256([]byte("management-key"))
                credential := sha256.Sum256([]byte("management-credential"))
                enrollment := ports.Enrollment{TokenHash: token[:], ScopeID: "scope", CorrelationID: "host-intent", ExpiresAt: now.Add(time.Minute)}
                if err := store.CreateEnrollment(ctx, enrollment); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                other := enrollment
                changed := sha256.Sum256([]byte("another-token"))
                other.TokenHash = changed[:]
                if err := store.CreateEnrollment(ctx, other); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConflict) {
                        t.Fatal("duplicate correlation accepted", err)
                }
                other.ScopeID = "other"
                if err := store.CreateEnrollment(ctx, other); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal("cross-scope correlation rejected", err)
                }
                attempt := ports.Attempt{TokenHash: token[:], ID: "attempt", KeyHash: key[:], Device: ports.Device{ID: "device", ScopeID: "scope", CorrelationID: "untrusted-candidate"}, CredentialHash: credential[:], Receipt: []byte("encrypted"), Now: now, RecoverUntil: now.Add(time.Hour)}
                if _, err := store.Consume(ctx, attempt); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if _, err := store.Consume(ctx, attempt); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal("retry lost identity", err)
                }
                value, err := store.GetDevice(ctx, "scope", "device")
                if err != nil || value.CorrelationID != "host-intent" {
                        t.Fatal("correlation was not bound atomically", value, err)
                }
                for _, scope := range []string{"other", "missing"} {
                        if _, err = store.GetDevice(ctx, scope, "device"); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                                t.Fatal("point read leaked scope", err)
                        }
                }
                values, err := store.QueryDevices(ctx, ports.DeviceQuery{ScopeID: "scope", CorrelationID: "host-intent", State: "ENROLLED", Limit: 20})
                if err != nil || len(values) != 1 {
                        t.Fatal(values, err)
                }
                for _, q := range []ports.DeviceQuery{{ScopeID: "other", Limit: 20}, {ScopeID: "scope", CorrelationID: "different", Limit: 20}, {ScopeID: "scope", State: "MANAGED", Limit: 20}, {ScopeID: "scope", After: "device", Limit: 20}} {
                        values, err = store.QueryDevices(ctx, q)
                        if err != nil || len(values) != 0 {
                                t.Fatal("filter/cursor isolation", values, err)
                        }
                }
                artifact := ports.Artifact{ScopeID: "scope", SHA256: "digest", PackageName: "app.example", VersionCode: 1, SigningCertificates: []string{"signer"}}
                if err = store.RegisterArtifact(ctx, artifact); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                assigned, err := store.Assign(ctx, ports.Assignment{ScopeID: "scope", DeviceID: "device", ArtifactSHA256: "digest", IdempotencyKey: "assign", OperationID: "operation", Now: now, Deadline: now.Add(time.Minute)})
                if err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if _, err = store.GetOperation(ctx, "other", "device", assigned.ID, now); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if _, err = store.GetOperation(ctx, "scope", "foreign", assigned.ID, now); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                operations, err := store.QueryOperations(ctx, ports.OperationQuery{ScopeID: "scope", DeviceID: "device", State: "QUEUED", Limit: 20, Now: now})
                if err != nil || len(operations) != 1 {
                        t.Fatal(operations, err)
                }
                expired, err := store.GetOperation(ctx, "scope", "device", assigned.ID, now.Add(2*time.Minute))
                if err != nil || expired.State != "FAILED" || expired.ErrorCode != "DEADLINE_EXCEEDED" {
                        t.Fatal(expired, err)
                }
                operations, err = store.QueryOperations(ctx, ports.OperationQuery{ScopeID: "scope", DeviceID: "device", State: "QUEUED", Limit: 20, Now: now.Add(2 * time.Minute)})
                if err != nil || len(operations) != 0 {
                        t.Fatal(operations, err)
                }
                cancelled, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
                cancel()
                if err = store.Check(cancelled); !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if _, err = store.GetDevice(cancelled, "scope", "device"); !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if _, err = store.QueryDevices(cancelled, ports.DeviceQuery{ScopeID: "scope", Limit: 20}); !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
        })
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package portstest

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "testing"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// Network verifies scoped reference-only intent, idempotency, observed connectivity and atomic rollback with command effects.
// Usage: portstest.Network(t, factory).
func Network(t *testing.T, factory func(*testing.T) ports.StateStore) {
        t.Helper()
        t.Run("network-references-observation-release-rollback", func(t *testing.T) {
                ctx := context.Background()
                s := factory(t)
                now, credential := fleetFixture(t, s)
                assignment := ports.NetworkAssignment{ScopeID: "scope", DeviceID: "device-a", IdempotencyKey: "network", Policy: ports.NetworkPolicy{PrimaryID: "office", Profiles: []ports.WifiProfile{{ID: "office", SSID: "Office", Security: "WPA2_PSK", SecretRef: "key-v1"}}}}
                desired, err := s.SetNetworkPolicy(ctx, assignment)
                if err != nil || desired.Revision != 1 {
                        t.Fatal(desired, err)
                }
                if retry, err := s.SetNetworkPolicy(ctx, assignment); err != nil || retry.Revision != 1 {
                        t.Fatal(retry, err)
                }
                changed := assignment
                changed.Policy.Profiles = []ports.WifiProfile{{ID: "office", SSID: "Office", Security: "WPA2_PSK", SecretRef: "key-v2"}}
                if _, err := s.SetNetworkPolicy(ctx, changed); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConflict) {
                        t.Fatal("reference rebound", err)
                }
                for _, scope := range []string{"foreign", ""} {
                        if _, err := s.GetNetworkPolicy(ctx, scope, "device-a"); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                                t.Fatal("network scope leak", err)
                        }
                        if _, err := s.GetNetworkRequest(ctx, scope, "device-a", "network"); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                                t.Fatal("receipt scope leak", err)
                        }
                }
                unsupported, err := s.GetNetworkPolicy(ctx, "scope", "device-a")
                if err != nil || unsupported.State != "UNSUPPORTED" {
                        t.Fatal(unsupported, err)
                }
                input := ports.SyncInput{DeviceID: "device-a", CredentialHash: credential, Sequence: 1, Now: now, Inventory: ports.Inventory{DeviceOwner: true, Capabilities: []string{"network.lifecycle", "command.reboot"}}}
                initial, err := s.Sync(ctx, input)
                if err != nil || initial.Network.State != "PENDING" || initial.Network.Desired.Revision != 1 {
                        t.Fatal(initial, err)
                }
                if _, err := s.SubmitCommand(ctx, ports.CommandAssignment{ScopeID: "scope", DeviceID: "device-a", Kind: "REBOOT", ID: "command", IdempotencyKey: "command", Now: now, Deadline: now.Add(time.Hour)}); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                input.Sequence++
                input.ReportedRevision = 1
                dispatched, err := s.Sync(ctx, input)
                if err != nil || len(dispatched.Commands) != 1 {
                        t.Fatal(dispatched, err)
                }
                before, err := s.ListChanges(ctx, "scope", 0, 100)
                if err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                input.Sequence++
                input.CommandReports = []ports.CommandReport{{CommandID: "command", DispatchToken: dispatched.Commands[0].DispatchToken, State: "SUCCEEDED", Observation: &ports.CommandObservation{BootBefore: 7, BootAfter: 8}}}
                input.NetworkReport = &ports.NetworkReport{Revision: 1, State: "APPLIED", ActiveProfileID: "office", ConnectivityVerified: false, CleanupComplete: true}
                if _, err := s.Sync(ctx, input); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrInvalidArgument) {
                        t.Fatal("setter accepted as network success", err)
                }
                command, err := s.GetCommand(ctx, "scope", "device-a", "command", now)
                if err != nil || command.State != "DISPATCHED" {
                        t.Fatal("invalid network report partially committed command", command, err)
                }
                after, err := s.ListChanges(ctx, "scope", before.Next, 100)
                if err != nil || len(after.Items) != 0 {
                        t.Fatal("rolled-back network report entered feed", after, err)
                }
                input.NetworkReport.ConnectivityVerified = true
                if applied, err := s.Sync(ctx, input); err != nil || applied.Network.State != "APPLIED" {
                        t.Fatal(applied, err)
                }
                changed.IdempotencyKey = "rotation"
                next, err := s.SetNetworkPolicy(ctx, changed)
                if err != nil || next.Revision != 2 {
                        t.Fatal(next, err)
                }
                status, err := s.GetNetworkPolicy(ctx, "scope", "device-a")
                if err != nil || status.State != "PENDING" || status.Reported.Revision != 1 {
                        t.Fatal("stale report became current compliance", status, err)
                }
                input.Sequence++
                input.CommandReports = nil
                if stale, err := s.Sync(ctx, input); err != nil || stale.Network.State != "PENDING" {
                        t.Fatal(stale, err)
                }
                input.Sequence++
                input.ReportedRevision = 2
                input.NetworkReport = &ports.NetworkReport{Revision: 2, State: "FAILED", Reason: "MANAGEMENT_UNREACHABLE"}
                if failed, err := s.Sync(ctx, input); err != nil || failed.Network.State != "FAILED" {
                        t.Fatal(failed, err)
                }
                release, err := s.SetNetworkPolicy(ctx, ports.NetworkAssignment{ScopeID: "scope", DeviceID: "device-a", IdempotencyKey: "release", Policy: ports.NetworkPolicy{}})
                if err != nil || release.Revision != 3 {
                        t.Fatal(release, err)
                }
                input.Sequence++
                input.ReportedRevision = 3
                input.NetworkReport = &ports.NetworkReport{Revision: 3, State: "APPLIED", ConnectivityVerified: true, CleanupComplete: true}
                if released, err := s.Sync(ctx, input); err != nil || released.Network.State != "APPLIED" || len(released.Network.Desired.Policy.Profiles) != 0 {
                        t.Fatal(released, err)
                }
                state, err := s.GetDeviceState(ctx, "scope", "device-a")
                if err != nil || state.Network.Desired.Revision != 3 || state.Network.Reported.Revision != 3 {
                        t.Fatal("aggregate omitted network state", state, err)
                }
                if err := s.Revoke(ctx, "scope", "device-a"); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                assignment.IdempotencyKey = "revoked"
                if _, err := s.SetNetworkPolicy(ctx, assignment); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrRevoked) {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
        })
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package portstest

import (
        "context"
        "crypto/sha256"
        "errors"
        "testing"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// Operations verifies storage-level atomic reports, replay, assignments and retention.
// Usage: portstest.Operations(t, factory).
func Operations(t *testing.T, factory func(*testing.T) ports.StateStore) {
        t.Helper()
        t.Run("sync-assignment-rollback-retention", func(t *testing.T) {
                ctx := context.Background()
                s := factory(t)
                now := time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Millisecond)
                token := sha256.Sum256([]byte("token"))
                key := sha256.Sum256([]byte("key"))
                credential := sha256.Sum256([]byte("credential"))
                if err := s.CreateEnrollment(ctx, ports.Enrollment{TokenHash: token[:], ScopeID: "scope", ExpiresAt: now.Add(time.Hour)}); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if _, err := s.Consume(ctx, ports.Attempt{TokenHash: token[:], ID: "attempt", KeyHash: key[:], Device: ports.Device{ID: "device", ScopeID: "scope"}, CredentialHash: credential[:], Receipt: []byte("encrypted"), Now: now, RecoverUntil: now.Add(time.Hour)}); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                artifact := ports.Artifact{ScopeID: "scope", SHA256: "digest", PackageName: "io.test", VersionCode: 2, MinSDK: 33, SizeBytes: 100, SigningCertificates: []string{"certificate"}}
                if err := s.RegisterArtifact(ctx, artifact); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                catalog, err := s.ListArtifacts(ctx, "scope", "", 1)
                if err != nil || len(catalog) != 1 || catalog[0].SHA256 != artifact.SHA256 {
                        t.Fatal("registered catalog entry missing", catalog, err)
                }
                for _, query := range [][2]string{{"other", ""}, {"scope", artifact.SHA256}} {
                        catalog, err = s.ListArtifacts(ctx, query[0], query[1], 1)
                        if err != nil || len(catalog) != 0 {
                                t.Fatal("catalog leaked scope or repeated cursor", catalog, err)
                        }
                }
                altered := artifact
                altered.VersionCode = 3
                if err := s.RegisterArtifact(ctx, altered); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConflict) {
                        t.Fatal("mutable catalog", err)
                }
                assignment := ports.Assignment{ScopeID: "scope", DeviceID: "device", ArtifactSHA256: "digest", IdempotencyKey: "same", OperationID: "operation", Now: now, Deadline: now.Add(time.Hour)}
                op, err := s.Assign(ctx, assignment)
                if err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                again, err := s.Assign(ctx, assignment)
                if err != nil || again.ID != op.ID || again.Revision != 1 {
                        t.Fatal("duplicate assignment", again, err)
                }
                input := ports.SyncInput{DeviceID: "device", CredentialHash: credential[:], Sequence: 1, Now: now, Inventory: ports.Inventory{APILevel: 35, DeviceOwner: true, Capabilities: []string{"install_apk"}}}
                snapshot, err := s.Sync(ctx, input)
                if err != nil || len(snapshot.Operations) != 1 {
                        t.Fatal("sync", snapshot, err)
                }
                lease := snapshot.Operations[0].LeaseID
                input.Sequence = 2
                input.ReportedRevision = 1
                report := ports.OperationReport{ID: "receipt", OperationID: op.ID, LeaseID: lease, Sequence: 1, State: "SUCCEEDED", Observed: &ports.InstalledApplication{PackageName: "io.test", VersionCode: 2, SigningCertificates: []string{"certificate"}}}
                conflict := report
                conflict.ErrorCode = "different-payload"
                input.Reports = []ports.OperationReport{report, conflict}
                if _, err := s.Sync(ctx, input); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConflict) {
                        t.Fatal("conflicting report", err)
                }
                ops, err := s.ListOperations(ctx, "scope", "device", "", 100, now)
                if err != nil || ops[0].State != "RUNNING" {
                        t.Fatal("failed sync partially committed", ops, err)
                }
                // Retrying the same sequence with the valid body succeeds after rollback.
                input.Reports = []ports.OperationReport{report}
                completed, err := s.Sync(ctx, input)
                if err != nil || !completed.Receipts[0].Accepted {
                        t.Fatal("completion", completed, err)
                }
                replay, err := s.Sync(ctx, input)
                if err != nil || replay.Sequence != 2 || len(replay.Receipts) != 1 {
                        t.Fatal("lost response", replay, err)
                }
                input.Inventory.Model = "conflict"
                if _, err := s.Sync(ctx, input); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConflict) {
                        t.Fatal("sync binding", err)
                }
                if err := s.RemoveArtifact(ctx, "scope", "digest"); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrReferenced) {
                        t.Fatal("desired reference removed", err)
                }
                if _, err := s.Artifact(ctx, "other", "digest"); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                        t.Fatal("scope leak", err)
                }
        })
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
package portstest

import (
        "context"
        "crypto/sha256"
        "errors"
        "testing"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// Policies checks scope, idempotent replacement, observed compliance, atomic
// rollback and revocation for custom providers as well as the default adapter.
// Usage: portstest.Policies(t, factory).
func Policies(t *testing.T, factory func(*testing.T) ports.StateStore) {
        t.Helper()
        t.Run("policy-replacement-observation-rollback", func(t *testing.T) {
                ctx := context.Background()
                s := factory(t)
                now := time.Now().UTC()
                token := sha256.Sum256([]byte("policy-token"))
                key := sha256.Sum256([]byte("key"))
                credential := sha256.Sum256([]byte("credential"))
                if err := s.CreateEnrollment(ctx, ports.Enrollment{TokenHash: token[:], ScopeID: "site", ExpiresAt: now.Add(time.Hour)}); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if _, err := s.Consume(ctx, ports.Attempt{TokenHash: token[:], ID: "attempt", KeyHash: key[:], Device: ports.Device{ID: "device", ScopeID: "site"}, CredentialHash: credential[:], Receipt: []byte("sealed"), Now: now, RecoverUntil: now.Add(time.Hour)}); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                disabled := true
                a := ports.PolicyAssignment{ScopeID: "site", DeviceID: "device", IdempotencyKey: "request", Policy: ports.Policy{CameraDisabled: &disabled}}
                want, err := s.SetPolicy(ctx, a)
                if err != nil || want.Revision != 1 {
                        t.Fatal(want, err)
                }
                again, err := s.SetPolicy(ctx, a)
                if err != nil || again.Revision != 1 {
                        t.Fatal("duplicate", again, err)
                }
                a.ScopeID = "other"
                if _, err = s.SetPolicy(ctx, a); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                        t.Fatal("scope", err)
                }
                a.ScopeID = "site"
                a.Policy = ports.Policy{}
                if _, err = s.SetPolicy(ctx, a); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConflict) {
                        t.Fatal("payload binding", err)
                }
                in := ports.SyncInput{DeviceID: "device", CredentialHash: credential[:], Sequence: 1, Now: now, ReportedRevision: 1,
                        Inventory:    ports.Inventory{Capabilities: []string{"durable_sync", "policy.camera"}},
                        PolicyReport: &ports.PolicyReport{Revision: 1, SnapshotRevision: 1, Results: []ports.PolicyResult{{RuleID: "unknown", State: "APPLIED", ObservedValue: "true"}}}}
                if _, err = s.Sync(ctx, in); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConflict) {
                        t.Fatal("unknown policy result", err)
                }
                in.PolicyReport.Results = []ports.PolicyResult{{RuleID: "camera", State: "APPLIED", ObservedValue: "false"}, {RuleID: "cleanup", State: "APPLIED", ObservedValue: "complete"}}
                snapshot, err := s.Sync(ctx, in)
                if err != nil {
                        t.Fatal("same sequence after rollback", err)
                }
                if snapshot.PolicyCompliance[0].State != "FAILED" || snapshot.PolicyCompliance[0].Reason != "OBSERVATION_MISMATCH" {
                        t.Fatal(snapshot.PolicyCompliance)
                }
                in.Sequence = 2
                in.PolicyReport.Results[0].ObservedValue = "true"
                if _, err = s.Sync(ctx, in); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                status, err := s.GetPolicy(ctx, "site", "device")
                if err != nil || status.Compliance[0].State != "APPLIED" {
                        t.Fatal(status, err)
                }
                a.IdempotencyKey = "release"
                if want, err = s.SetPolicy(ctx, a); err != nil || want.Revision != 2 {
                        t.Fatal(want, err)
                }
                status, err = s.GetPolicy(ctx, "site", "device")
                if err != nil || status.Reported != nil || len(status.Compliance) != 1 || status.Compliance[0].RuleID != "cleanup" || status.Compliance[0].State == "APPLIED" {
                        t.Fatal("release unobserved", status, err)
                }
                if err = s.Revoke(ctx, "site", "device"); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                a.IdempotencyKey = "revoked"
                if _, err = s.SetPolicy(ctx, a); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrRevoked) {
                        t.Fatal("revoked policy", err)
                }
        })
}

// ManagedConfigurations verifies typed config idempotency and honest current-snapshot readback on any adapter.
// Usage: portstest.ManagedConfigurations(t, factory).
func ManagedConfigurations(t *testing.T, factory func(*testing.T) ports.StateStore) {
        t.Helper()
        t.Run("managed-configuration-binding-observation-release", func(t *testing.T) {
                ctx := context.Background()
                s := factory(t)
                now := time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Millisecond)
                token := sha256.Sum256([]byte("configuration-token"))
                key := sha256.Sum256([]byte("configuration-key"))
                credential := sha256.Sum256([]byte("configuration-credential"))
                if err := s.CreateEnrollment(ctx, ports.Enrollment{TokenHash: token[:], ScopeID: "scope", ExpiresAt: now.Add(time.Hour)}); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if _, err := s.Consume(ctx, ports.Attempt{TokenHash: token[:], ID: "configuration-attempt", KeyHash: key[:], Device: ports.Device{ID: "device", ScopeID: "scope"}, CredentialHash: credential[:], Receipt: []byte("encrypted"), Now: now, RecoverUntil: now.Add(time.Hour)}); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                artifact := ports.Artifact{ScopeID: "scope", SHA256: "digest", PackageName: "app.example", VersionCode: 1, SigningCertificates: []string{"signer"}}
                if err := s.RegisterArtifact(ctx, artifact); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if _, err := s.Assign(ctx, ports.Assignment{ScopeID: "scope", DeviceID: "device", ArtifactSHA256: "digest", IdempotencyKey: "assign", OperationID: "operation", Now: now, Deadline: now.Add(time.Hour)}); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                label := "private managed value"
                policy := ports.Policy{RequiredPackages: []string{artifact.PackageName}, ManagedConfigurations: []ports.ManagedConfiguration{{PackageName: artifact.PackageName, Values: ports.ConfigurationBundle{Entries: []ports.ConfigurationEntry{{Key: "label", Value: ports.ConfigurationValue{String: &label}}}}}}}
                assigned, err := s.SetPolicy(ctx, ports.PolicyAssignment{ScopeID: "scope", DeviceID: "device", IdempotencyKey: "configure", Policy: policy})
                if err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                duplicate, err := s.SetPolicy(ctx, ports.PolicyAssignment{ScopeID: "scope", DeviceID: "device", IdempotencyKey: "configure", Policy: policy})
                if err != nil || duplicate.Revision != assigned.Revision {
                        t.Fatal(duplicate, err)
                }
                input := ports.SyncInput{DeviceID: "device", CredentialHash: credential[:], Sequence: 1, ReportedRevision: assigned.Revision, Now: now,
                        Inventory:    ports.Inventory{DeviceOwner: true, Capabilities: []string{"install_apk", "policy.managed_configuration"}, Applications: []ports.InstalledApplication{{PackageName: artifact.PackageName, VersionCode: 1, SigningCertificates: []string{"signer"}}}},
                        PolicyReport: &ports.PolicyReport{Revision: assigned.Revision, SnapshotRevision: assigned.Revision, Results: []ports.PolicyResult{{RuleID: "configuration:" + artifact.PackageName, State: "APPLIED", ObservedValue: "sha256:incorrect"}}}}
                if _, err = s.Sync(ctx, input); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                status, err := s.GetPolicy(ctx, "scope", "device")
                if err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                found := false
                for _, r := range status.Compliance {
                        if r.RuleID == "configuration:"+artifact.PackageName {
                                found = true
                                if r.State != "FAILED" || r.Reason != "OBSERVATION_MISMATCH" {
                                        t.Fatal("false config success", r)
                                }
                        }
                }
                if !found {
                        t.Fatal(status)
                }
                input.Sequence++
                input.PolicyReport.Results[0].ObservedValue = ports.ConfigurationDigest(assigned.Policy.ManagedConfigurations[0].Values)
                if _, err = s.Sync(ctx, input); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                status, err = s.GetPolicy(ctx, "scope", "device")
                if err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                for _, r := range status.Compliance {
                        if r.RuleID == "configuration:"+artifact.PackageName && r.State != "APPLIED" {
                                t.Fatal("digest readback rejected", r)
                        }
                }
                released, err := s.SetPolicy(ctx, ports.PolicyAssignment{ScopeID: "scope", DeviceID: "device", IdempotencyKey: "release", Policy: ports.Policy{}})
                if err != nil || len(released.Policy.ManagedConfigurations) != 0 {
                        t.Fatal(released, err)
                }
                if _, err = s.GetPolicy(ctx, "other", "device"); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                        t.Fatal("configuration scope leak", err)
                }
        })
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
// Package portstest exposes transactional persistence conformance checks to adapter authors.
package portstest

import (
        "bytes"
        "context"
        "crypto/sha256"
        "errors"
        "sync"
        "testing"
        "time"

        "github.com/thienvu18/kylok/go/ports"
)

// State runs against a fresh durable store. Callers should separately prove
// process-restart persistence, resource ownership and backend-specific failures.
// Usage: portstest.State(t, factory).
func State(t *testing.T, factory func(*testing.T) ports.StateStore) {
        t.Helper()
        t.Run("atomic-consume-recovery-scope-revoke", func(t *testing.T) {
                ctx := context.Background()
                s := factory(t)
                now := time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Millisecond)
                token := sha256.Sum256([]byte("token"))
                key := sha256.Sum256([]byte("key"))
                credential := sha256.Sum256([]byte("credential"))
                e := ports.Enrollment{TokenHash: token[:], ScopeID: "site", ExpiresAt: now.Add(time.Minute), ArtifactSHA256: "apk"}
                if err := s.CreateEnrollment(ctx, e); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                a := ports.Attempt{TokenHash: token[:], ID: "original-attempt", KeyHash: key[:], Device: ports.Device{ID: "device", ScopeID: "site", Inventory: ports.Inventory{APILevel: 35}}, CredentialHash: credential[:], Receipt: []byte("opaque-encrypted-receipt"), Now: now, RecoverUntil: now.Add(time.Hour)}
                var wg sync.WaitGroup
                for i := 0; i < 16; i++ {
                        wg.Add(1)
                        go func() {
                                defer wg.Done()
                                receipt, err := s.Consume(ctx, a)
                                if err != nil || !bytes.Equal(receipt, a.Receipt) {
                                        t.Error("consume", err)
                                }
                        }()
                }
                wg.Wait()
                list, err := s.ListDevices(ctx, "site", "", 100)
                if err != nil || len(list) != 1 {
                        t.Fatal("atomic rows", list, err)
                }
                list, err = s.ListDevices(ctx, "other", "", 100)
                if err != nil || len(list) != 0 {
                        t.Fatal("scope leak", list, err)
                }
                wrong := a
                wrong.ID = "other-attempt"
                if _, err = s.Consume(ctx, wrong); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConsumed) {
                        t.Fatal("ID binding", err)
                }
                wrong = a
                wrong.KeyHash = make([]byte, 32)
                if _, err = s.Consume(ctx, wrong); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrConsumed) {
                        t.Fatal("key binding", err)
                }
                a.Now = now.Add(2 * time.Minute)
                if _, err = s.Consume(ctx, a); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal("bound recovery after token expiry", err)
                }
                if _, err = s.Sync(ctx, ports.SyncInput{DeviceID: "device", CredentialHash: make([]byte, 32), Sequence: 1, Now: now}); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrUnauthorized) {
                        t.Fatal("bad credential", err)
                }
                if _, err = s.Sync(ctx, ports.SyncInput{DeviceID: "device", CredentialHash: credential[:], Inventory: ports.Inventory{APILevel: 35, DeviceOwner: true}, Sequence: 1, Now: now}); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if err = s.Revoke(ctx, "other", "device"); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                        t.Fatal("cross-scope revoke", err)
                }
                if err = s.Revoke(ctx, "site", "device"); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if _, err = s.Authenticate(ctx, "device", credential[:]); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrRevoked) {
                        t.Fatal("auth after revoke", err)
                }
                if _, err = s.Sync(ctx, ports.SyncInput{DeviceID: "device", CredentialHash: credential[:], Sequence: 2, Now: now}); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrRevoked) {
                        t.Fatal("sync after revoke", err)
                }
                if _, err = s.Consume(ctx, a); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrRevoked) {
                        t.Fatal("recovery after revoke", err)
                }
        })
        t.Run("expiry-boundaries", func(t *testing.T) {
                ctx := context.Background()
                s := factory(t)
                now := time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Millisecond)
                token := sha256.Sum256([]byte("expired"))
                e := ports.Enrollment{TokenHash: token[:], ScopeID: "site", ExpiresAt: now, ArtifactSHA256: "apk"}
                if err := s.CreateEnrollment(ctx, e); err != nil {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
                if _, err := s.Consume(ctx, ports.Attempt{TokenHash: token[:], Now: now}); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrExpired) {
                        t.Fatal("expiry inclusive", err)
                }
                if _, err := s.Enrollment(ctx, []byte("unknown")); !errors.Is(err, ports.ErrNotFound) {
                        t.Fatal(err)
                }
        })
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: LGPL-3.0-only
// Package provisioning verifies signed host APKs and constructs Android QR payloads.
package provisioning

import (
        "archive/zip"
        "bytes"
        "context"
        "crypto/sha256"
        "encoding/base64"
        "encoding/hex"
        "encoding/json"
        "encoding/xml"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "io"
        "net/http"
        "net/url"
        "os"
        "os/exec"
        "regexp"
        "sort"
        "strconv"
        "strings"
        "time"
)

const MaxAPKBytes = 64 << 20

type Tools struct{ APKSigner, APKAnalyzer string }
type Metadata struct {
        PackageName         string   `json:"package_name"`
        Receiver            string   `json:"receiver"`
        VersionCode         int64    `json:"version_code"`
        MinSDK              int      `json:"min_sdk"`
        MaxSDK              int      `json:"max_sdk"`
        SupportedABIs       []string `json:"supported_abis"`
        SizeBytes           int64    `json:"size_bytes"`
        TargetSDK           int      `json:"target_sdk"`
        Debuggable          bool     `json:"debuggable"`
        SHA256              string   `json:"sha256"`
        SigningCertificates []string `json:"signing_certificates_sha256"`
}

// APK retains the exact verified bytes; callers cannot mutate metadata or content.
type APK struct {
        metadata    Metadata
        data        []byte
        verified    bool
        deviceAdmin bool
}

// Verified distinguishes an inspected immutable artifact from a zero-value APK.
// Usage: if !apk.Verified() { return errors.New("verified APK required") }.
func (a *APK) Verified() bool { return a != nil && a.verified }

// IsDPC distinguishes a verified provisioning-capable host from an ordinary signed application.
// Usage: if !apk.IsDPC() { return errors.New("provisioning host required") }.
func (a *APK) IsDPC() bool { return a.Verified() && a.deviceAdmin }

// Metadata returns detached verified properties so callers cannot mutate registered identity.
// Usage: metadata := apk.Metadata().
func (a *APK) Metadata() Metadata {
        m := a.metadata
        m.SigningCertificates = append([]string(nil), m.SigningCertificates...)
        m.SupportedABIs = append([]string(nil), m.SupportedABIs...)
        return m
}

// Bytes returns a copy of the exact verified APK content for immutable publication.
// Usage: content := apk.Bytes().
func (a *APK) Bytes() []byte { return bytes.Clone(a.data) }

// Inspect snapshots the input before invoking Android's signature verifier and XML decoder.
// Both tools inspect the same bytes that are later registered and served.
// Usage: apk, err := provisioning.Inspect(ctx, signedAPKPath, provisioning.Tools{}).
func Inspect(ctx context.Context, path string, tools Tools) (*APK, error) {
        return inspect(ctx, path, tools, true)
}

// InspectApplication verifies a single/universal signed APK without requiring DPC components.
// Usage: apk, err := provisioning.InspectApplication(ctx, applicationPath, provisioning.Tools{}).
func InspectApplication(ctx context.Context, path string, tools Tools) (*APK, error) {
        return inspect(ctx, path, tools, false)
}

// Snapshot one bounded APK, verify its signature and parse the same bytes before granting verified status.
func inspect(ctx context.Context, path string, tools Tools, requireDPC bool) (*APK, error) {
        source, err := os.Open(path)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("open APK: %w", err)
        }
        defer source.Close()
        data, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(source, MaxAPKBytes+1))
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if len(data) == 0 || len(data) > MaxAPKBytes {
                return nil, errors.New("APK must be between 1 byte and 64 MiB")
        }
        temp, err := os.CreateTemp("", "kylok-verified-*.apk")
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer os.Remove(temp.Name())
        if _, err = temp.Write(data); err != nil {
                temp.Close()
                return nil, err
        }
        if err = temp.Close(); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        signer := tools.APKSigner
        if signer == "" {
                signer = "apksigner"
        }
        analyzer := tools.APKAnalyzer
        if analyzer == "" {
                analyzer = "apkanalyzer"
        }
        // A finite deadline also bounds misconfigured external tools during construction.
        ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, time.Minute)
        defer cancel()
        certs, err := exec.CommandContext(ctx, signer, "verify", "--verbose", "--print-certs", temp.Name()).Output()
        if err != nil {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("APK signature verification failed: %w", err)
        }
        matches := regexp.MustCompile(`(?m)^.*Signer[^\n]*certificate SHA-256 digest: ([0-9a-fA-F]{64})\s*$`).FindAllSubmatch(certs, -1)
        if len(matches) == 0 {
                return nil, errors.New("signature verifier returned no signing certificates")
        }
        manifest, err := exec.CommandContext(ctx, analyzer, "manifest", "print", temp.Name()).Output()
        if err != nil {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("decode merged APK manifest: %w", err)
        }
        m, err := parseManifest(manifest, requireDPC)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        digest := sha256.Sum256(data)
        m.SHA256 = hex.EncodeToString(digest[:])
        seen := map[string]bool{}
        for _, match := range matches {
                cert := strings.ToLower(string(match[1]))
                if !seen[cert] {
                        m.SigningCertificates = append(m.SigningCertificates, cert)
                        seen[cert] = true
                }
        }
        sort.Strings(m.SigningCertificates)
        archive, err := zip.NewReader(bytes.NewReader(data), int64(len(data)))
        if err != nil {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid APK archive: %w", err)
        }
        abis := map[string]bool{}
        for _, entry := range archive.File {
                parts := strings.Split(entry.Name, "/")
                if len(parts) >= 3 && parts[0] == "lib" && strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name, ".so") {
                        abis[parts[1]] = true
                }
        }
        for abi := range abis {
                m.SupportedABIs = append(m.SupportedABIs, abi)
        }
        sort.Strings(m.SupportedABIs)
        m.SizeBytes = int64(len(data))
        _, dpcErr := parseManifest(manifest, true)
        return &APK{metadata: m, data: data, verified: true, deviceAdmin: dpcErr == nil}, nil
}

type component struct {
        Name       string `xml:"http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android name,attr"`
        Permission string `xml:"http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android permission,attr"`
        Exported   string `xml:"http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android exported,attr"`
        Enabled    string `xml:"http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android enabled,attr"`
        Metadata   []struct {
                Name     string `xml:"http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android name,attr"`
                Resource string `xml:"http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android resource,attr"`
        } `xml:"meta-data"`
        Filters []struct {
                Actions []struct {
                        Name string `xml:"http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android name,attr"`
                } `xml:"action"`
                Categories []struct {
                        Name string `xml:"http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android name,attr"`
                } `xml:"category"`
        } `xml:"intent-filter"`
}

// Check whether an Android component declares the required intent action.
func (c component) action(name string) bool {
        for _, f := range c.Filters {
                for _, a := range f.Actions {
                        if a.Name == name {
                                return true
                        }
                }
        }
        return false
}

// Require both the provisioning action and default category before accepting an exported Activity.
func (c component) defaultAction(name string) bool {
        for _, f := range c.Filters {
                action, category := false, false
                for _, a := range f.Actions {
                        action = action || a.Name == name
                }
                for _, a := range f.Categories {
                        category = category || a.Name == "android.intent.category.DEFAULT"
                }
                if action && category {
                        return true
                }
        }
        return false
}

// Validate APK identity, SDK bounds and protected provisioning components from decoded XML.
func parseManifest(data []byte, requireDPC bool) (Metadata, error) {
        var manifest struct {
                XMLName      xml.Name   `xml:"manifest"`
                Package      string     `xml:"package,attr"`
                VersionCode  string     `xml:"http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android versionCode,attr"`
                VersionMajor string     `xml:"http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android versionCodeMajor,attr"`
                Split        string     `xml:"split,attr"`
                UsesSplit    []struct{} `xml:"uses-split"`
                SDK          struct {
                        Min    int `xml:"http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android minSdkVersion,attr"`
                        Target int `xml:"http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android targetSdkVersion,attr"`
                        Max    int `xml:"http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android maxSdkVersion,attr"`
                } `xml:"uses-sdk"`
                Application struct {
                        SplitRequired bool        `xml:"http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android isSplitRequired,attr"`
                        Debuggable    bool        `xml:"http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android debuggable,attr"`
                        Enabled       string      `xml:"http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android enabled,attr"`
                        Receivers     []component `xml:"receiver"`
                        Activities    []component `xml:"activity"`
                } `xml:"application"`
        }
        if err := xml.Unmarshal(data, &manifest); err != nil {
                return Metadata{}, fmt.Errorf("manifest XML: %w", err)
        }
        low, err := strconv.ParseUint(manifest.VersionCode, 10, 32)
        if err != nil {
                return Metadata{}, errors.New("invalid versionCode")
        }
        var major uint64
        if manifest.VersionMajor != "" {
                major, err = strconv.ParseUint(manifest.VersionMajor, 10, 31)
        }
        version := int64(major<<32 | low)
        if manifest.SDK.Min == 0 {
                manifest.SDK.Min = 1
        }
        if manifest.SDK.Target == 0 {
                manifest.SDK.Target = manifest.SDK.Min
        }
        if err != nil || version < 1 || manifest.SDK.Min < 1 || manifest.SDK.Target < 1 || manifest.Package == "" || manifest.Application.Enabled == "false" || manifest.Split != "" || len(manifest.UsesSplit) > 0 || manifest.Application.SplitRequired || manifest.SDK.Max != 0 && manifest.SDK.Max < manifest.SDK.Min {
                return Metadata{}, errors.New("APK requires a package, positive long version, valid SDK range and no split dependencies")
        }
        metadata := Metadata{PackageName: manifest.Package, VersionCode: version, MinSDK: manifest.SDK.Min, MaxSDK: manifest.SDK.Max, TargetSDK: manifest.SDK.Target, Debuggable: manifest.Application.Debuggable}
        if !requireDPC {
                return metadata, nil
        }
        if manifest.SDK.Min < 33 || manifest.SDK.Min > 37 || manifest.SDK.Target != 37 {
                return Metadata{}, errors.New("DPC must use min SDK 33–37 and target SDK 37")
        }
        var receiver string
        for _, c := range manifest.Application.Receivers {
                valid := c.Exported == "true" && c.Enabled != "false" && c.Permission == "android.permission.BIND_DEVICE_ADMIN" && c.action("android.app.action.DEVICE_ADMIN_ENABLED")
                metadata := false
                for _, m := range c.Metadata {
                        metadata = metadata || m.Name == "android.app.device_admin" && m.Resource != ""
                }
                if valid && metadata {
                        if receiver != "" {
                                return Metadata{}, errors.New("ambiguous device admin receivers")
                        }
                        receiver = c.Name
                }
        }
        if receiver == "" {
                return Metadata{}, errors.New("APK has no exported, protected device admin receiver with metadata")
        }
        for _, action := range []string{"android.app.action.GET_PROVISIONING_MODE", "android.app.action.ADMIN_POLICY_COMPLIANCE"} {
                found := false
                for _, c := range manifest.Application.Activities {
                        found = found || c.Exported == "true" && c.Enabled != "false" && c.Permission == "android.permission.BIND_DEVICE_ADMIN" && c.defaultAction(action)
                }
                if !found {
                        return Metadata{}, fmt.Errorf("missing protected provisioning activity for %s", action)
                }
        }
        if strings.HasPrefix(receiver, ".") {
                receiver = manifest.Package + receiver
        } else if !strings.Contains(receiver, ".") {
                receiver = manifest.Package + "." + receiver
        }
        metadata.Receiver = receiver
        return metadata, nil
}

// Origin accepts an HTTP or HTTPS origin using a domain name or IP address.
// Route prefixes, credentials and token-bearing URLs are unsupported.
// Usage: origin, err := provisioning.Origin("http://192.168.1.5:8080").
func Origin(value string) (string, error) {
        u, err := url.Parse(value)
        if err != nil || (u.Scheme != "http" && u.Scheme != "https") || u.Hostname() == "" || u.User != nil || u.RawQuery != "" || u.ForceQuery || strings.Contains(value, "#") || u.Path != "" && u.Path != "/" || u.Opaque != "" {
                return "", errors.New("URL must be an HTTP or HTTPS origin without credentials, path, query or fragment")
        }
        return strings.TrimSuffix(value, "/"), nil
}

// Path returns the content-addressed public bootstrap route for these verified bytes.
// Usage: route := apk.Path().
func (a *APK) Path() string { return "/bootstrap/" + a.metadata.SHA256 + ".apk" }

// Handler serves this verified APK only at its immutable GET/HEAD bootstrap path.
// Usage: bootstrapServer.Handler = apk.Handler().
func (a *APK) Handler() http.Handler {
        return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
                if r.URL.Path != a.Path() {
                        http.NotFound(w, r)
                        return
                }
                if r.Method != http.MethodGet && r.Method != http.MethodHead {
                        w.Header().Set("Allow", "GET, HEAD")
                        w.WriteHeader(http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)
                        return
                }
                w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/vnd.android.package-archive")
                w.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=31536000, immutable")
                w.Header().Set("ETag", `"`+a.metadata.SHA256+`"`)
                w.Header().Set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
                http.ServeContent(w, r, a.metadata.PackageName+".apk", time.Time{}, bytes.NewReader(a.data))
        })
}

// QR returns secret JSON. Hosts must not publish or log the payload.
// Usage: payload, err := apk.QR(bootstrapOrigin, map[string]string{"kylok.server": apiOrigin, "kylok.enrollment_token": token}).
func (a *APK) QR(publicURL string, extras map[string]string) ([]byte, error) {
        if !a.IsDPC() {
                return nil, errors.New("verified APK required")
        }
        origin, err := Origin(publicURL)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return a.qr(origin, extras)
}

// Encode the verified component, SHA-256 and host extras using Android's provisioning field names.
func (a *APK) qr(origin string, extras map[string]string) ([]byte, error) {
        sum, _ := hex.DecodeString(a.metadata.SHA256)
        const prefix = "android.app.extra."
        return json.Marshal(map[string]any{
                prefix + "PROVISIONING_DEVICE_ADMIN_COMPONENT_NAME":            a.metadata.PackageName + "/" + a.metadata.Receiver,
                prefix + "PROVISIONING_DEVICE_ADMIN_PACKAGE_DOWNLOAD_LOCATION": origin + a.Path(),
                prefix + "PROVISIONING_DEVICE_ADMIN_PACKAGE_CHECKSUM":          base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(sum),
                prefix + "PROVISIONING_ALLOW_OFFLINE":                          true,
                prefix + "PROVISIONING_ADMIN_EXTRAS_BUNDLE":                    extras,
        })
}

package kylok

import (
        _ "embed"
        "strings"
)

//go:embed VERSION
var sdkVersion string

// Version identifies this SDK's source package, independently of its host.
// Usage: version := kylok.Version().
func Version() string { return strings.TrimSpace(sdkVersion) }

// ProtocolVersion is the current wire version. Development versions must be
// paired from the same source revision; the number alone is not compatibility.
const ProtocolVersion uint32 = 1
